     1	# rc.8 Daybreak fix set — 2026-11-09
     2	
     3	Branch `cc125/rc8-daybreak-fixes`.
     4	Input: `C4-report.md`, `C2-report.md`, `C1-report.md` in
     5	the Daybreak review archive (2026-11-09, held by the maintainer and DSR).
     6	The initial `git diff 8de39026 -- contracts/src` was empty. No network, chain,
     7	key access, commits, or Git metadata writes were performed.
     8	The pre-existing OpenZeppelin v5.1.0 `package-lock.json` edit was preserved.
     9	
    10	## Implementation
    11	
    12	### C4-H-01: separate signing cutoff and withdrawal readiness
    13	
    14	`BLSAggregator-4.14.0` retains the two-day public notice. For request time `t`:
    15	
    16	- `readyAt = t + 2 days`: signing stops, exactly as before.
    17	- `withdrawAt = readyAt + GUARDIAN_FRAUD_CHALLENGE = t + 3 days`.
    18	- `expiresAt = withdrawAt + GUARDIAN_EXIT_WINDOW = t + 4 days`.
    19	- Withdrawal is permitted at both endpoints `[withdrawAt, expiresAt]`, absent a
    20	  pending case. `GuardianExitNotReady` now reports `withdrawAt`.
    21	- `GUARDIAN_SLASH_CASE_WINDOW = 5 days > 2 + 1 + 1 days`.
    22	  `VERIFIER_ROTATION_DELAY` remains tied to that constant and becomes five days.
    23	
    24	Every other use of the notice fields was checked:
    25	
    26	- `_reconstructPkAgg` is unchanged: a nonzero notice excludes its key from
    27	  `readyAt`, including throughout the challenge period and after `expiresAt`.
    28	  Expiry never silently restores a potentially fraudulent signer.
    29	- `_requireCommitteeSurvivesExit` already excludes all other announced exits,
    30	  even before their cutoff. It conservatively checks the eventual signer set;
    31	  delaying withdrawal does not change the set or weaken the quorum floor.
    32	- Cancellation deletes the notice, restores signing eligibility, and starts the
    33	  existing one-day cooldown. Pending cases still prohibit cancellation and new
    34	  notices. A cancelled notice cannot be consumed; a fresh notice incurs the full
    35	  notice and challenge again. Expired notices still require cancellation and
    36	  cooldown before replacement.
    37	- `consumeGuardianExit` still checks pending cases before timing, so a case queued
    38	  during the challenge freezes the guardian even after withdrawal would mature.
    39	  Successful consumption deletes the notice atomically with Registry role exit.
    40	- Any notice already open when a case is queued expires before that case's
    41	  five-day deadline. New notices are forbidden during a pending case. The old
    42	  comment suggesting an accused guardian could open a notice after queueing was
    43	  corrected rather than retained.
    44	- Fraud-verifier rotation retains its full-case-window delay and verdict pinning.
    45	  Request structs, event fields and storage layout are unchanged; the public
    46	  `readyAt` event/getter field still means signing cutoff, not withdrawal time.
    47	
    48	Regression coverage includes a real Prague BLS signature executed at `readyAt-1`,
    49	Registry exits rejected at `readyAt` and the last challenge second, a fraud case
    50	queued during the challenge and enforced after maturity, and a subsequent actual
    51	stake slash. EVM-independent Registry/staking tests cover clean withdrawal at
    52	exact maturity, withdrawal at exact new expiry, and rejection one second later.
    53	Existing cutoff, cancellation, cooldown, expired-key exclusion and committee-floor
    54	tests remain in place; existing withdrawal/expiry timings were updated.
    55	
    56	### C4-L-01 and C4-L-02 fixed in rc.8 (BLSAggregator-4.14.0)
    57	
    58	C4-L-02: generic `executeProposal` stores the signer commitment after signature verification and before
    59	calling the arbitrary target (test_GenericCommitmentIsStoredBeforeTargetCall,
    60	test_Prague_GenericProposalRecordsSigners).
    61	
    62	C4-L-01: generic and slash proposals no longer share one id space. This supersedes the 11-09 note that
    63	deferred C4-L-01 to 5.5.1; DSR then ruled it into rc.8, together with the typed keys and
    64	MAX_VALIDATORS = 64 (commit "typed replay/commitment keys, anchored signer commitments, MAX_VALIDATORS 64").
    65	- The generic path keys replay protection and its commitment by
    66	  `uint256(keccak256(abi.encode(TAG_PROPOSAL, proposalId)))`. The `markProposalExecuted` callback to
    67	  DVTValidator is removed, so a generic id can no longer pre-consume a slash id that DVTValidator allocates
    68	  later.
    69	- Raw slash and reputation ids must be below 2^128 (`InvalidProposalId`), which separates them from the
    70	  keccak-derived generic and queue keys by 128 bits.
    71	- Each path stores its commitment under its own key, and the commitment also binds
    72	  `blockhash(block.number - 1)`. Every write emits `SignersCommitted(key, signerMask)`.
    73	- MAX_VALIDATORS is 64, and slot 65 reverts with `SlotOutOfRange`.
    74	
    75	Tests that run on Prague with genuine keys:
    76	- both paths keep both commitments: test_QueueSlashGeneric_OverlappingInputsKeepAllCommitments;
    77	- the crafted collision: test_SlashRawIdEqualToGenericKeyCannotOverwriteGenericCommitment;
    78	- slot 65: test_RegisterBLSPublicKey_SlotAboveMax_Reverts, test_Verify_RejectsBitsBeyondMaxValidators;
    79	- the typed key per path in the recompute: test_QueueSlashGeneric_OverlappingInputsKeepAllCommitments,
    80	  test_GenericCommitmentIsStoredBeforeTargetCall.
    81	
    82	Version: `BLSAggregator-4.14.0`. Main had already carried `4.13.0` for the build with C4-L-01 deferred.
    83	That build was never deployed, but two bytecodes must not share a version.
    84	
    85	### C2-01: invalidate stale standby roles
    86	
    87	`_setCurrentSP` clears a matching designated standby and emits `StandbyInvalidated`.
    88	It separately clears a matching pending standby **and ETA**, emitting
    89	`StandbyProposalCancelled`. No storage was added. Designation activation rechecks
    90	both current SP and emergency-revoked SP after the timelock and before designation.
    91	Tests cover normal promotion of a designated standby, simultaneous mature SP and
    92	standby proposals, cancellation events/ETA clearing, and both activation guards.
    93	The latter seed stale state explicitly, since normal promotion now clears it.
    94	
    95	### C1-L-01: report the applied slash penalty
    96	
    97	`_slash` returns its final capped/clamped penalty. `executeSlashWithBLS` emits that
    98	value in `SlashExecutedWithProof`. The regression deposits 1,000 aPNTs and checks
    99	that a MAJOR slash emits 300, leaves 700, credits 300 revenue and records 300 in
   100	history. SuperPaymaster core/Admin storage is untouched.
   101	
   102	### Registry ENDUSER membership cleanup
   103	
   104	`MySBT.deactivateAllMemberships` cannot attribute a membership to the role that
   105	created it: it iterates every membership stored for the user's SBT and deactivates
   106	every active entry. Therefore `Registry.exitRole(ROLE_ENDUSER)` calls it only when
   107	`userRoleCount[user] == 0` after role removal. If another role remains, all
   108	memberships remain intact. In particular, a per-community membership can outlive
   109	the ENDUSER role while another role is held. Integrators must not treat
   110	`MySBT.verifyCommunityMembership(user, community)` as sufficient authorization;
   111	they must also require `Registry.hasRole(ROLE_ENDUSER, user)`.
   112	
   113	A later final-role exit burns the SBT and deactivates its memberships through
   114	`MySBT.burnSBT`. If a final non-ENDUSER exit occurs while MySBT is paused, the burn
   115	fails non-fatally and sets `pendingSBTBurn`; after unpausing MySBT, anyone can call
   116	`reconcileSBTBurn(user)`. The permissionless
   117	`reconcileMembershipDeactivation` retry applies the same zero-role condition, so
   118	a stale pending flag cannot deactivate memberships while any role remains. If a
   119	third party adds COMMUNITY, ANODE, DVT, KMS, PAYMASTER_AOA, or PAYMASTER_SUPER
   120	(e.g. via a community's `safeMintForRole`) before that retry, recovery requires
   121	the holder to exit every remaining role. COMMUNITY has a zero default lock; the
   122	operator roles use `block.timestamp >= lockedAt + 30 days` (and DVT also requires
   123	its guardian-exit authorization). Exit and reconciliation are separate
   124	transactions, so a sponsor that keeps funding grants can re-grant a role after
   125	each exit and before the retry, delaying recovery indefinitely while it keeps
   126	paying; recovery is therefore not bounded against an actively funded sponsor.
   127	Precondition for any of this: a MySBT cleanup call must first have FAILED
   128	(e.g. MySBT paused or replaced by a reverting implementation). Once `userRoleCount == 0`, call
   129	`reconcileMembershipDeactivation(user)`; if the final burn also failed, unpause
   130	MySBT and call `reconcileSBTBurn(user)`. ENDUSER registration remains blocked until
   131	the membership-cleanup flag is cleared.
   132	
   133	This is the documented fallback because the targeted cleanup implementation did
   134	not fit EIP-170: the round-6 Registry measured 24,509 B, a behavior-preserving
   135	counter simplification reclaimed 29 B (24,480 B), and the minimal implementation
   136	then measured 25,002 B, 426 B over the 24,576 B limit. The attempted code and
   137	reclamation were reverted.
   138	
   139	REG-R5-02 does not require a migration: the Sepolia Registry has never emitted
   140	`SBTBurnFailed` (verified by the evidence script on `cc125/rc8-reg-evidence`), and
   141	the Optimism deployment is fresh.
   142	
   143	### PolicyRegistry velocity and spend reads
   144	
   145	PolicyRegistry 3.0.2 rejects `setTargetVelocity` and `tightenTargetVelocity` while
   146	the target velocity window is zero. Disabling a window invalidates all prior
   147	per-asset limits; re-enabling it remains fail-closed until each asset receives a
   148	fresh timelocked `setTargetVelocity`, which also starts a fresh target counter.
   149	`getTargetVelocity` reports stale/disabled entries as unconfigured, and no setter
   150	or tightening event is emitted for a rejected zero-window call.
   151	
   152	`getAssetSpend` now returns effective spend (zero once its configured window has
   153	expired). The new `getTargetSpend(sender, target, asset)` returns the same
   154	window-aware value for target velocity, including zero for disabled, stale, or
   155	expired entries.
   156	
   157	### I3 release requirement
   158	
   159	Any future validation-time consumer of `PolicyRegistry` must pass the ERC-7562
   160	tracer gate before release. This is a documented release requirement even when
   161	the consumer's direct unit and integration tests pass.
   162	
   163	### Version propagation (historical; superseded by the reviewed redesign below)
   164	
   165	- BLSAggregator: 4.12.0 → 4.13.0.
   166	- SuperPaymaster: stays `SuperPaymaster-5.5.0`. Its ABI is unchanged; 5.5.0 has never been deployed on a live chain, and build identity is the commit plus runtime codehash (03-final-spec §6), not the version string.
   167	- xPNTsTokenV2: XPNTs-4.0.0 → XPNTs-4.0.1.
   168	- SuperPaymasterLens: stays `SuperPaymasterLens-1.2.0` (its SP-version gate stays 5.5.0).
   169	- xPNTsFactoryV2: 3.0.0-v2 → 3.0.1-v2; its exact template-version gate follows 4.0.1.
   170	- PolicyRegistry: 3.0.1 → 3.0.2; ABI adds `getTargetSpend(address,address,address)`
   171	  and `VelocityDisabled()` while preserving existing function signatures.
   172	
   173	The last two changes are required to keep diagnostics and token creation working
   174	with the new versions. Current-version assertions and the experimental
   175	ProfilePaymaster test helper were updated; historical deployment fixtures remain
   176	historical. Downstream DVT/SDK consumers need the new withdrawal timing and ABI
   177	constant/events before release. No downstream notifications were sent.
   178	
   179	## BLS round 4
   180	
   181	### DVT proposal-id rotation
   182	
   183	`DVTValidator-0.8.0` takes `(registry, firstProposalId)`; there is no predecessor
   184	argument or state. `firstProposalId` is nonzero and below `2^128`. The retained
   185	BLSAggregator-4.14.0 owns a monotonic `proposalFloor`. Activation atomically
   186	captures the outgoing DVT's next allocation, including pending IDs, and validates
   187	the successor's aggregator and Registry pointers. Every allocation reads that
   188	floor once. Empty intermediates and A→B→A rollback cannot reuse allocated IDs.
   189	The ordinary setter fails closed if the outgoing counter cannot be read. The
   190	explicit-floor recovery operation requires governance to supply an audited upper
   191	bound on all allocated IDs; it must never lower the stored floor. This recovery
   192	is an immediate owner authority, not automatic discovery of missing history.
   193	
   194	Pending slash and aggregator-routed reputation proposals executed through the
   195	current DVT need fresh IDs and proofs after rotation, including after reactivation.
   196	The floor does not revoke owner-direct `verifyAndExecute` with caller-chosen IDs,
   197	generic proofs, authorized direct Registry reputation proofs or unused consensus
   198	queue pre-flags; see the disclosed M1 residuals below. Full-width hashed
   199	ReputationSystem IDs remain valid on the direct Registry route.
   200	
   201	### Validator-removal quorum floor
   202	
   203	The survival check protects each currently reachable configured threshold:
   204	`minThreshold`, `defaultThreshold`, and every severity threshold. With one
   205	eligible signer leaving, crossing a threshold is exactly equality with the
   206	pre-exit committee count. Higher unreachable thresholds do not hide lower working
   207	ones; caller-selected generic thresholds above `minThreshold` are not standing
   208	configured quorums. At an exactly reachable N-of-N quorum, seat a replacement or
   209	change policy before voluntary exit or DVT removal. Direct owner revocation and
   210	suspension are explicit emergency overrides.
   211	
   212	Suspension is authoritative on the retained aggregator and is checked both during
   213	registration and while reconstructing every aggregate key. It survives rotation
   214	and rollback and can be set even without local admission or an active key. Use
   215	owner `revokeAndSuspend` for atomic emergency eviction. Reinstatement requires
   216	explicit owner clearance on the aggregator; DVT admission alone cannot clear it.
   217	
   218	The DVT and aggregator owners must remain the same Safe-compatible M-of-N owner.
   219	Rotation delegates key-registration authority as well as proposal forwarding;
   220	it is therefore more than the owner's existing direct execution permission.
   221	Verifier rotation separately retains its contract-enforced five-day delay.
   222	
   223	### Reputation rule disablement
   224	
   225	`Reputation-0.6.0` records rule existence and active state separately. A configured
   226	zero-base rule contributes no score and does not receive the default. Only an
   227	unconfigured first rule receives the default. Re-enabling does not duplicate an
   228	active rule. The additional mappings belong to a directly deployed contract;
   229	there is no proxy migration or automatic backfill claim.
   230	
   231	### NFT boost semantics
   232	
   233	NFT boost eligibility means **registration age plus current ownership**. The
   234	seven-day timestamp is not proof of uninterrupted ownership: a third-party NFT
   235	can be transferred away and reacquired before a permissionless watcher calls
   236	`resetNFTHoldStart`. Strict continuity is impossible here without collection
   237	transfer hooks, checkpoints, escrow, or authenticated acquisition history.
   238	
   239	### Raw-id separation wording
   240	
   241	Raw proposal ids remain below `2^128`. Their separation from unrestricted
   242	Keccak-derived generic and queue keys is a 128-bit cryptographic guarantee, not
   243	an absolute structural impossibility.
   244	
   245	## BLS round 7 — Daybreak re-review #4
   246	
   247	R4-01, R4-03, and R4-04 move to the dedicated manifest/executor PR. All scoped
   248	executor, schema, adapter, test, and release-design changes were restored to
   249	`origin/main`. The retained contract, deployment, test, historical-template,
   250	and security-report changes no longer reference that split implementation.
   251	
   252	R4-02 is fixed in every deployment path that previously transferred only the
   253	BLSAggregator owner: `DeployNewBLSModules`, `07b_DeployBLSModules`, and
   254	`DeployStandardV3` now transfer the paired DVTValidator to the same declared
   255	governance owner and assert `dvt.owner() == bls.owner()`. The other scripts that
   256	instantiate DVTValidator were checked: DeployLive, DeployAnvil, and
   257	DeployRepCreditSepolia already transfer both owners. Archive scripts contain no
   258	one-sided governance transfer; a fresh paired deployment leaves both contracts
   259	with the same broadcaster owner. A script-level regression covers
   260	DeployNewBLSModules. Removing its DVT transfer makes the test fail with

   217	        // CC-48 round-6 HIGH-1: hand the DISARM AUTHORITY to governance as the very last
   218	        // owner-gated action, then refuse to finish if it is still an EOA anywhere it
   219	        // matters. `emergencyDisarmFraudProofVerifier()` is immediate and unannounced, so
   220	        // an EOA-owned aggregator means one hot key can censor every future guardian-slash
   221	        // accusation by front-running it out of the mempool -- and no Timelock can cover
   222	        // that path. DVT ownership moves to the same boundary because its validator-removal
   223	        // route must not remain with the deployer. Everything above still runs deployer-owned.
   224	        _settleAggregatorGovernance();
   225	        _settleTokenGovernance();
   226	
   227	        vm.stopBroadcast();
   228	        GovernanceOwnerGate.requireGovernanceOwner(address(aggregator), aggregator.owner(), "BLSAggregator");
   229	        require(dvt.owner() == aggregator.owner(), "DeployLive: DVT owner != BLS owner");
   230	        // Not wrapped in a `declaredGovernanceOwner() != 0` check, and the aggregator's
   231	        // is not either. With the variable unset the gate REFUSES rather than passing,
   232	        // even when the owner is already a Safe -- it proves "this is the Safe the
   233	        // operator declared", not "this is a Safe" (pinned by
   234	        // test_SafeOwnedSubjectIsStillRefusedWhenNothingWasDeclared). So a guard here
   235	        // was redundant, and leaving it in meant the three subjects were gated by two
   236	        // different rules for no reason. Raised by pr-daemon on #404.
   237	        GovernanceOwnerGate.requireGovernanceOwner(address(apnts), apnts.communityOwner(), "aPNTs");
   238	        GovernanceOwnerGate.requireGovernanceOwner(address(xpntsFactory), xpntsFactory.owner(), "xPNTsFactory");
   239	        // The v2 factory and the AOA allowlist hold the same class of power (factory: SP pointer,
   240	        // tier source and CC-28 inputs for every v2 token; registry: which SP / spender / tier
   241	        // source a token may ever activate), so they are gated by the same rule.
   242	        GovernanceOwnerGate.requireGovernanceOwner(v55.factory, xPNTsFactoryV2Owner(), "xPNTsFactoryV2");
   243	        GovernanceOwnerGate.requireGovernanceOwner(v55.aoaRegistry, aoaRegistryOwner(), "AOAProtocolRegistry");
   244	
   245	        // v5.5.0 read-back (runbook steps 4 / 6 / 7a). View-only.
   246	        _verifyV55Stack(v55, address(superPaymaster), address(registry));
   247	        _verifySPFactory(address(superPaymaster), v55.factory);
   248	        require(_strEq(superPaymaster.version(), SP_V55_VERSION), "DeployLive: SP is not 5.5.0");
   249	        require(_implOf(address(superPaymaster)) == spImplAddr, "DeployLive: SP proxy impl != deployed impl");
   250	        _requireDefaultArtifact(spImplAddr, "SuperPaymaster"); // AUD-4
   251	        if (pntsAddr != address(0)) {
   252	            address anni = vm.addr(vm.envUint("PRIVATE_KEY_ANNI"));
   253	            _verifyV2Token(pntsAddr, v55.factory, anni, address(superPaymaster));
   254	            _verifyOperatorV2(address(superPaymaster), anni, pntsAddr, anni);
   255	        }
   256	        _assertAllDefaultArtifacts();
   257	        _generateConfig();
   258	    }
   259	
   260	    /// @notice T-4 closing check: every contract this script created — directly, through a
   261	    ///         proxy, or as a factory clone — runs the profile.default bytes. View-only.
   262	    function _assertAllDefaultArtifacts() internal view {
   263	        console.log("=== T-4: all contracts == profile.default artifact ===");
   264	        _requireDefaultProxy(address(registry), "Registry");
   265	        _requireDefaultProxy(address(superPaymaster), "SuperPaymaster");
   266	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(xpntsFactory), "xPNTsFactory");
   267	        require(
   268	            _requireDefaultClone(address(apnts), "xPNTsToken") == xpntsFactory.implementation(),
   269	            "T-4: aPNTs not a clone of the factory template"
   270	        );
   271	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(gtoken), "GTokenAuthorization");
   272	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(staking), "GTokenStaking");
   273	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(mysbt), "MySBT");
   274	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(repSystem), "ReputationSystem");
   275	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(dvt), "DVTValidator");
   276	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(aggregator), "BLSAggregator");
   277	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(microPaymentCh), "MicroPaymentChannel");
   278	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(pmFactory), "PaymasterFactory");
   279	        _requireDefaultArtifact(address(pmV4Impl), "Paymaster");
   280	        address pmProxy = pmFactory.getPaymasterByOperator(deployer);
   281	        if (pmProxy != address(0)) {
   282	            require(
   283	                _requireDefaultClone(pmProxy, "Paymaster") == address(pmV4Impl), "T-4: V4 proxy not a clone of pmV4Impl"
   284	            );
   285	            require(pmFactory.implementations("v4.2") == address(pmV4Impl), "T-4: factory v4.2 != pmV4Impl");
   286	        }
   287	        _requireDefaultArtifact(x402FacilitatorAddr, "X402Facilitator");
   288	        _requireDefaultArtifact(policyRegistryAddr, "PolicyRegistry");
   289	        _requireDefaultArtifact(timelockControllerAddr, "TimelockController");
   290	        _verifyV55Stack(v55, address(superPaymaster), address(registry)); // the 7 v5.5.0 contracts
   291	        if (pntsAddr != address(0)) {
   292	            require(
   293	                _requireDefaultClone(pntsAddr, "xPNTsTokenV2") == v55.impl, "T-4: PNTs not a clone of the v2 template"
   294	            );
   295	        }
   296	    }
   297	
   298	    function xPNTsFactoryV2Owner() internal view returns (address) {
   299	        return Ownable(v55.factory).owner();
   300	    }
   301	
   302	    function aoaRegistryOwner() internal view returns (address) {
   303	        return Ownable(v55.aoaRegistry).owner();
   304	    }
   305	
   306	    /// @dev Transfers BLSAggregator and DVTValidator ownership to the same
   307	    ///      `GOVERNANCE_OWNER`. DVT rotation remains immediate: it adds no power beyond
   308	    ///      the owner's direct verifyAndExecute route, and DVT paths still require a BLS
   309	    ///      quorum. Verifier rotation keeps its contract-enforced delay. Unset is the
   310	    ///      normal local path and is refused on a production chain by the governance gate.
   311	    /// @dev The governance owner to hand BLSAggregator + DVTValidator to. Reads the
   312	    ///      GOVERNANCE_OWNER gate; virtual only so a test harness can supply it without
   313	    ///      mutating the process environment (vm.setEnv races other suites under parallel forge).
   314	    function _governanceOwner() internal view virtual returns (address) {
   315	        return GovernanceOwnerGate.declaredGovernanceOwner();
   316	    }
   317	
   318	    function _settleAggregatorGovernance() internal {
   319	        address gov = _governanceOwner();
   320	        if (gov == address(0)) return;
   321	        aggregator.transferOwnership(gov);
   322	        dvt.transferOwnership(gov);
   323	        require(dvt.owner() == aggregator.owner(), "DeployLive: DVT owner != BLS owner");
   324	        console.log("  BLSAggregator + DVTValidator ownership transferred to governance:", gov);
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    /// @dev Hands aPNTs and the xPNTsFactory to governance, with the same gate the
   328	    ///      aggregator uses above. The token owners decide how much of the community
   329	    ///      token can exist and what it is worth:
   330	    ///
   331	    ///        aPNTs.communityOwner   mint() is onlyFactoryOrOwner with NO cap check.
   332	    ///                               issuanceCap is a view for DVT, not a mint gate,
   333	    ///                               and stays unset until setIssuanceCap is called.
   334	    ///        xPNTsFactory.owner     updateAPNTsPrice, setIndustryScaleUSD,
   335	    ///                               setCapRatioBps and setTokenCategory are every
   336	    ///                               input isOverIssued() reads.
   337	    ///
   338	    ///      So an EOA left here both mints without bound and grades its own
   339	    ///      over-issuance. Both live examples came out of this path: OP mainnet aPNTs
   340	    ///      0x0B41C780 (communityOwner 0x51Ac6949, an EOA, 1e24 mint simulates fine
   341	    ///      against a 140,000 supply) and Sepolia aPNTs 0x696A7370 (communityOwner
   342	    ///      0x51C00187, also an EOA).
   343	    ///
   344	    ///      Runs after the mint and all wiring, so nothing above becomes a governance
   345	    ///      transaction. Unset GOVERNANCE_OWNER is the local path and a no-op, as for
   346	    ///      the aggregator.
   347	    function _settleTokenGovernance() internal {
   348	        address gov = GovernanceOwnerGate.declaredGovernanceOwner();
   349	        if (gov == address(0)) return;
   350	        apnts.transferCommunityOwnership(gov);
   351	        xpntsFactory.transferOwnership(gov);
   352	        console.log("  aPNTs communityOwner transferred to governance:", gov);
   353	        console.log("  xPNTsFactory ownership transferred to governance:", gov);
   354	        // v5.5.0: the AOA registry is already sealed (step 4), so governance inherits only the
   355	        // 48 h addition path and immediate revocation.
   356	        Ownable(v55.factory).transferOwnership(gov);
   357	        Ownable(v55.aoaRegistry).transferOwnership(gov);
   358	        console.log("  xPNTsFactoryV2 + AOAProtocolRegistry ownership transferred to governance:", gov);
   359	    }
   360	
   361	    /// @dev Deploy the three NEW v5.4 contracts and wire X402Facilitator on the
   362	    ///      deployer-owned xPNTs tokens. Runs under the deployer broadcast (Step 8,
   363	    ///      after all factory tokens exist). On a fresh GA deploy the deployer-owned
   364	    ///      aPNTs clone is new-bytecode so the cap-setter wiring succeeds; community
   365	    ///      operator tokens (Anni's PNTs) are skipped+logged and wired by prepare-test.
   366	    function _deployV54Stack() internal {
   367	        (address governor, address guardian) = _resolveGovernance(deployer);
   368	        V54Addresses memory v54 = _deployV54Contracts(
   369	            address(registry),
   370	            address(superPaymaster),
   371	            address(xpntsFactory),
   372	            governor,
   373	            guardian,
   374	            address(0) // fresh timelock
   375	        );
   376	        x402FacilitatorAddr = v54.facilitator;
   377	        policyRegistryAddr = v54.policyRegistry;
   378	        timelockControllerAddr = v54.timelock;
   379	
   380	        _wireFacilitator(address(xpntsFactory), x402FacilitatorAddr, deployer);
   381	    }
   382	
   383	    /// @notice Asserts every critical contract wiring link is intact.
   384	    ///         Called at the end of run() — after all steps including Step 6
   385	    ///         (aPNTs wired) and before vm.stopBroadcast() — so any silently
   386	    ///         no-op setter reverts the entire deploy script rather than writing
   387	    ///         broken addresses to the config file.  (Security fix: D-H4 / Issue #255 S3)
   388	    /// @dev    All reads are view-only; no transactions are broadcast.
   389	    function _assertWiring() internal view {
   390	        // ── Step 1: Registry ↔ GTokenStaking ↔ MySBT ──────────────────────────
   391	        require(address(registry.GTOKEN_STAKING()) == address(staking), "wire: registry.setStaking");
   392	        require(address(registry.MYSBT()) == address(mysbt), "wire: registry.setMySBT");
   393	        require(address(gtoken.mySBT()) == address(mysbt), "wire: gtoken.setMySBT");
   394	
   395	        // ── Step 5: Registry ↔ core modules ────────────────────────────────────
   396	        require(registry.SUPER_PAYMASTER() == address(superPaymaster), "wire: registry.setSuperPaymaster");
   397	        _assertReputationSources();
   398	        require(registry.blsAggregator() == address(aggregator), "wire: registry.setBLSAggregator");
   399	
   400	        // ── Step 5: DVT / BLS cross-wiring ─────────────────────────────────────
   401	        require(aggregator.DVT_VALIDATOR() == address(dvt), "wire: aggregator.setDVTValidator");
   402	        require(dvt.BLS_AGGREGATOR() == address(aggregator), "wire: dvt.setBLSAggregator");
   403	        // 5.5.0 ships an empty evidence allowlist: no slash-shape proposal can pass DVTValidator.
   404	        require(dvt.categoryCount() == 0, "wire: dvt evidence allowlist must be empty in 5.5.0");
   405	
   406	        // ── Step 5: PaymasterFactory implementation registration ────────────────
   407	        require(pmFactory.implementations("v4.2") == address(pmV4Impl), "wire: factory.addImplementation v4.2");
   408	
   409	        // ── Step 5: SuperPaymaster ↔ xPNTsFactoryV2 (5.5.0) / 3.x factory ──────
   410	        require(superPaymaster.xpntsFactory() == v55.factory, "wire: sp.setXPNTsFactory(factoryV2)");
   411	        require(xpntsFactory.SUPERPAYMASTER() == address(superPaymaster), "wire: factory.setSuperPaymaster");
   412	
   413	        // ── Step 5: Slasher authorization ───────────────────────────────────────
   414	        // 5.5.0 is disclosure-only: the aggregator's only GToken-stake slash (guardian slash,
   415	        // BLSAggregator.executeGuardianSlash) is unreachable while fraudProofVerifier == 0, so
   416	        // the aggregator holds NO slasher role. Granting it is a 5.5.x enablement step that
   417	        // must ship in the same timelock batch as proposeFraudProofVerifier.
   418	        require(!staking.authorizedSlashers(address(aggregator)), "wire: aggregator must not be a staking slasher");
   419	
   420	        // ── Step 5: SuperPaymaster BLS_AGGREGATOR ───────────────────────────────
   421	        require(superPaymaster.BLS_AGGREGATOR() == address(aggregator), "wire: sp.initBLSAggregator");
   422	
   423	        // ── Step 5: Price feed immutable (baked into SP implementation) ─────────
   424	        require(address(superPaymaster.ETH_USD_PRICE_FEED()) == priceFeedAddr, "wire: sp.priceFeed");
   425	
   426	        // ── Step 6: aPNTs ↔ SuperPaymaster ─────────────────────────────────────
   427	        require(apnts.SUPERPAYMASTER_ADDRESS() == address(superPaymaster), "wire: apnts.setSuperPaymaster");
   428	    }
   429	
   430	    /// @dev Reputation sources are exactly ReputationSystem and the aggregator. The aggregator's
   431	    ///      verifyAndExecute reputation branch (floor-checked) needs the role; the generic path can no
   432	    ///      longer reach batchUpdateGlobalReputation (H-1 denylist). Registry.initialize makes its
   433	    ///      owner (the deployer) a source (Registry.sol:172), so that grant is revoked here.
   434	    function _wireReputationSources() internal {
   435	        registry.setReputationSource(address(repSystem), true);
   436	        registry.setReputationSource(address(aggregator), true);
   437	        registry.setReputationSource(deployer, false);
   438	    }
   439	
   440	    /// @dev Same expectation as the Sepolia manifest (step 13): ReputationSystem and the aggregator are
   441	    ///      sources; the deployer and every other address this script deploys or wires are not.
   442	    function _assertReputationSources() internal view {
   443	        require(registry.isReputationSource(address(repSystem)), "wire: registry.setReputationSource(repSystem)");
   444	        require(registry.isReputationSource(address(aggregator)), "wire: registry.setReputationSource(aggregator)");
   445	        address[22] memory notSources = [
   446	            deployer, address(superPaymaster), spImplAddr, address(dvt), address(staking), address(mysbt),
   447	            address(gtoken), address(xpntsFactory), address(registry), registryImplAddr, address(apnts),
   448	            address(pmFactory), address(pmV4Impl), address(microPaymentCh), x402FacilitatorAddr,
   449	            policyRegistryAddr, v55.factory, v55.impl, v55.ext, v55.lens, v55.tierSource, v55.aoaRegistry
   450	        ];
   451	        for (uint256 i; i < notSources.length; ++i) {
   452	            if (notSources[i] == address(0)) continue;
   453	            require(!registry.isReputationSource(notSources[i]), "wire: unexpected reputation source");
   454	        }
   455	    }
   456	
   457	    function _executeWiring() internal {
   458	        registry.setSuperPaymaster(address(superPaymaster));
   459	        _wireReputationSources();
   460	        registry.setBLSAggregator(address(aggregator));
   461	        dvt.setBLSAggregator(address(aggregator));
   462	        aggregator.setDVTValidator(address(dvt));
   463	        pmFactory.addImplementation("v4.2", address(pmV4Impl));
   464	        // 5.5.0 (runbook step 6): SP binds operators to tokens issued by xPNTsFactoryV2.
   465	        _wireSPFactory(address(superPaymaster), v55.factory);
   466	        xpntsFactory.setSuperPaymasterAddress(address(superPaymaster));
   467	        // Wire aPNTs (deployed before SP in Step 2, so SP address must be set retroactively).
   468	        // setSuperPaymasterAddress also sets autoApprovedSpenders[SP] = true.
   469	        apnts.setSuperPaymasterAddress(address(superPaymaster));
   470	
   471	        // Wire BLSAggregator into SuperPaymaster (Tier 1 slash authorization).
   472	        // Uses initBLSAggregator() — a one-time setter that bypasses the 24h timelock
   473	        // because BLS_AGGREGATOR is address(0) on a fresh deployment (no governance asset to protect).
   474	        superPaymaster.initBLSAggregator(address(aggregator));
   475	
   476	        // Integrity assertions moved to _assertWiring(), called at end of run()
   477	        // before vm.stopBroadcast() — see D-H4 fix.
   478	
   479	        // ERC-8004 official agent registry addresses (CREATE2, deterministic across all EVM chains).
   480	        // Mainnet chains: Ethereum, OP, Base, Arbitrum, Polygon, etc.
   481	        // Testnet chains:  Sepolia, OP Sepolia, Base Sepolia, Arbitrum Sepolia, Polygon Amoy.
   482	        // Override via AGENT_IDENTITY_REGISTRY / AGENT_REPUTATION_REGISTRY env vars if needed.
   483	        address erc8004Identity;
   484	        address erc8004Reputation;
   485	        uint256 cid = block.chainid;
   486	        bool isMainnet =
   487	            (cid == 1 || cid == 10 || cid == 137 || cid == 8453 || cid == 42161 || cid == 43114 || cid == 56
   488	                || cid == 534352);
   489	        bool isTestnet = (cid == 11155111 || cid == 11155420 || cid == 84532 || cid == 421614 || cid == 80002);
   490	        if (isMainnet) {
   491	            erc8004Identity = 0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432;
   492	            erc8004Reputation = 0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63;
   493	            erc8004Validation = 0x8004Cc8439f36fd5F9F049D9fF86523Df6dAAB58;
   494	        } else if (isTestnet) {
   495	            erc8004Identity = 0x8004A818BFB912233c491871b3d84c89A494BD9e;
   496	            erc8004Reputation = 0x8004B663056A597Dffe9eCcC1965A193B7388713;
   497	            erc8004Validation = 0x8004Cb1BF31DAf7788923b405b754f57acEB4272;
   498	        }
   499	        // Allow env override
   500	        erc8004Identity = vm.envOr("AGENT_IDENTITY_REGISTRY", erc8004Identity);
   501	        erc8004Reputation = vm.envOr("AGENT_REPUTATION_REGISTRY", erc8004Reputation);
   502	        erc8004Validation = vm.envOr("AGENT_VALIDATION_REGISTRY", erc8004Validation);
   503	        if (erc8004Identity != address(0) && erc8004Reputation != address(0)) {
   504	            superPaymaster.setAgentRegistries(erc8004Identity, erc8004Reputation);
   505	            console.log("  Agent registries wired (ERC-8004 official)");
   506	            console.log("    identity:   ", erc8004Identity);
   507	            console.log("    reputation: ", erc8004Reputation);
   508	            console.log("    validation: ", erc8004Validation, " (recorded, not wired to SP yet)");
   509	        } else {
   510	            console.log("  WARN: Agent registries not wired (unsupported chain or no override set)");
   511	        }
   512	
   513	        // Oracle Init
   514	        try AggregatorV3Interface(priceFeedAddr).latestRoundData() returns (
   515	            uint80, int256 price, uint256, uint256, uint80
   516	        ) {
   517	            try SuperPaymasterAdmin(address(superPaymaster)).updatePriceDVT(price, block.timestamp, "", 0) { // D5b: extension function
   518	                console.log("  Cache Price Force-Initialized");
   519	            } catch {
   520	                superPaymaster.updatePrice();
   521	            }
   522	        } catch {}
   523	
   524	        // Step 24 & 25: 0.1 ETH
   525	        superPaymaster.deposit{value: 0.1 ether}();
   526	        superPaymaster.addStake{value: 0.1 ether}(1 days);
   527	    }
   528	
   529	    function _orchestrateRolesJason() internal {
   530	        // COMMUNITY registration and aPNTs deployment already done in Step 2 of run().
   531	        // aPNTs is wired to SuperPaymaster in _executeWiring().
   532	        console.log("  aPNTs:", address(apnts), "SP:", address(superPaymaster));
   533	        require(apnts.SUPERPAYMASTER_ADDRESS() == address(superPaymaster), "Deploy: aPNTs SP wiring failed!");
   534	
   535	        // Step 31-36: AOA Paymaster
   536	        bytes memory init = abi.encodeWithSignature(
   537	            "initialize(address,address,address,address,uint256,uint256,uint256)",
   538	            entryPointAddr,
   539	            deployer,
   540	            deployer,
   541	            priceFeedAddr,
   542	            100,
   543	            1 ether,
   544	            4200
   545	        );
   546	        address pmProxy = pmFactory.deployPaymaster("v4.2", init);
   547	        Paymaster(payable(pmProxy)).addStake{value: 0.1 ether}(86400); // Increased to 0.1 ETH as requested
   548	        Paymaster(payable(pmProxy)).updatePrice();
   549	        // Step 34: $0.02
   550	        Paymaster(payable(pmProxy)).setTokenPrice(address(apnts), 2_000_000);
   551	
   552	        gtoken.approve(address(staking), 33 ether);
   553	        registry.registerRole(ROLE_PAYMASTER_AOA, deployer, abi.encode(uint256(30 ether)));
   554	
   555	        // Step 37: 0.05 ETH
   556	        IEntryPoint(entryPointAddr).depositTo{value: 0.05 ether}(pmProxy);
   557	
   558	        // Step 38: Mint 20,000 aPNTs
   559	        apnts.mint(deployer, 20_000 ether);
   560	    }
   561	
   562	    function _setupMyceliumCommunity() internal {
   563	        uint256 anniPK = vm.envUint("PRIVATE_KEY_ANNI");
   564	        address anni = vm.addr(anniPK);
   565	        console.log("  Anni (Mycelium Admin):", anni);
   566	
   567	        // --- Deployer prepares Anni (safeMintForRole = deployer pays stake) ---
   568	
   569	        // 6a. Register Mycelium as COMMUNITY (deployer pays 30 stake + burn)
   570	        if (!registry.hasRole(ROLE_COMMUNITY, anni)) {
   571	            gtoken.approve(address(staking), 34 ether);
   572	            Registry.CommunityRoleData memory mycData = Registry.CommunityRoleData({
   573	                name: "Mycelium Community", ensName: "mushroom.box", stakeAmount: 30 ether
   574	            });
   575	            registry.safeMintForRole(ROLE_COMMUNITY, anni, abi.encode(mycData));
   576	            console.log("  Mycelium Community Registered");
   577	        } else {
   578	            console.log("  Mycelium Community (skip - already registered)");
   579	        }
   580	
   581	        // 6b. Register as PAYMASTER_SUPER (deployer pays 50 stake + burn)
   582	        if (!registry.hasRole(ROLE_PAYMASTER_SUPER, anni)) {
   583	            gtoken.approve(address(staking), 56 ether);
   584	            registry.safeMintForRole(ROLE_PAYMASTER_SUPER, anni, "");
   585	            console.log("  Mycelium PAYMASTER_SUPER Registered");
   586	        } else {
   587	            console.log("  Mycelium PAYMASTER_SUPER (skip - already registered)");
   588	        }
   589	
   590	        // 6c. Fund Anni with aPNTs for SuperPaymaster deposit
   591	        if (apnts.balanceOf(anni) < 1000 ether) {
   592	            apnts.transfer(anni, 1000 ether);
   593	            console.log("  1000 aPNTs transferred to Anni");
   594	        } else {
   595	            console.log("  Anni aPNTs (skip - sufficient balance)");
   596	        }
   597	
   598	        // --- Switch to Anni broadcast for operator-specific calls ---
   599	        vm.stopBroadcast();
   600	        vm.startBroadcast(anniPK);
   601	
   602	        // 6d. Deploy PNTs (Mycelium community token — special token name).
   603	        //     5.5.0: an xPNTs v2 token from xPNTsFactoryV2 (SP rejects 3.x tokens).
   604	        pntsAddr = _issueV2Token(v55.factory, anni, "Mycelium PNTs", "PNTs", "Mycelium Community", "mushroom.box", 1e18);
   605	
   606	        // 6e. Configure Anni as SuperPaymaster operator (idempotent; re-binds a stale token)
   607	        _configureOperatorV2(address(superPaymaster), pntsAddr, anni, anni);
   608	        console.log("  Operator Configured (PNTs v2 -> SuperPaymaster)");
   609	
   610	        // 6f. Deposit aPNTs into SuperPaymaster (gasless sponsorship fund)
   611	        (uint128 bal,,,,,,,,) = superPaymaster.operators(anni);
   612	        if (bal < 100 ether) {
   613	            apnts.approve(address(superPaymaster), 1000 ether);
   614	            superPaymaster.deposit(1000 ether);
   615	            console.log("  1000 aPNTs Deposited to SuperPaymaster");
   616	        } else {
   617	            console.log("  SuperPaymaster deposit (skip - sufficient balance)");
   618	        }
   619	
   620	        // 6g. Mint PNTs for test users
   621	        if (IxPNTsV2Script(pntsAddr).balanceOf(anni) < 500 ether) {
   622	            IxPNTsV2Script(pntsAddr).mint(anni, 500 ether); // v2: mint is served by the extension
   623	            console.log("  500 PNTs Minted to Anni");
   624	        } else {
   625	            console.log("  Anni PNTs (skip - sufficient balance)");
   626	        }
   627	
   628	        // --- Switch back to deployer ---
   629	        vm.stopBroadcast();
   630	        vm.startBroadcast();
   631	    }
   632

   634	        string memory network = vm.envString("ENV");

   674	        vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "policyRegistry", policyRegistryAddr);
   675	        vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "timelockController", timelockControllerAddr);
   676	        // v5.5.0 xPNTs v2 stack (NEW keys; "xPNTsFactory"/"aPNTs" keep their 3.x meaning)
   677	        vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "aoaProtocolRegistry", v55.aoaRegistry);
   678	        vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "globalTierSource", v55.tierSource);
   679	        vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "xPNTsTokenV2Ext", v55.ext);

   681	        vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "xPNTsFactoryV2", v55.factory);
   682	        vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "superPaymasterLens", v55.lens);
   683	        // srcHash intentionally written as "" — deploy-core commits the real hash after audit-core passes.
   684	        vm.serializeString(jsonObj, "srcHash", string(""));
   685	        vm.serializeString(jsonObj, "updateTime", vm.envOr("DEPLOY_TIME", string("N/A")));
   686	        vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "priceFeed", priceFeedAddr);
   687	        string memory finalJson = vm.serializeAddress(jsonObj, "entryPoint", entryPointAddr);
   688	        vm.writeFile(finalPath, finalJson);
   689	        console.log("\n--- Live Phase 1 Complete ---");
   690	        console.log("Config saved to: %s", finalPath);
   691	    }

   361	    /// @dev Deploy the three NEW v5.4 contracts and wire X402Facilitator on the

   383	    /// @notice Asserts every critical contract wiring link is intact.
   384	    ///         Called at the end of run() — after all steps including Step 6
   385	    ///         (aPNTs wired) and before vm.stopBroadcast() — so any silently
   386	    ///         no-op setter reverts the entire deploy script rather than writing
   387	    ///         broken addresses to the config file.  (Security fix: D-H4 / Issue #255 S3)
   388	    /// @dev    All reads are view-only; no transactions are broadcast.
   389	    function _assertWiring() internal view {
   390	        // ── Step 1: Registry ↔ GTokenStaking ↔ MySBT ──────────────────────────
     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     2	pragma solidity ^0.8.26;

    15	/// @dev Minimal view over the concrete xPNTsFactory: enumerate every community token it minted.
    16	interface IXPNTsFactoryEnum {
    17	    function getAllTokens() external view returns (address[] memory);
    18	}
    19	
    20	/// @dev Minimal view/mutate surface over the concrete xPNTsToken for X402Facilitator wiring.
    21	///      Not in IxPNTsToken (those are community-admin entrypoints), so declared locally.
    22	interface IXPNTsWiring {
    23	    function communityOwner() external view returns (address);
    24	    function autoApprovedSpenders(address spender) external view returns (bool);
    25	    function approvedFacilitators(address facilitator) external view returns (bool);
    26	    function addAutoApprovedSpender(address spender) external;        // onlyFactoryOrOwner
    27	    function setSpenderDailyCapFor(address spender, uint256 newCap) external; // onlyCommunityOwner
    28	    function addApprovedFacilitator(address facilitator) external;    // onlyCommunityOwner

    31	/**
    32	 * @title V54Bootstrap
    33	 * @notice Shared v5.4 "god-split + DVT policy" deploy + wiring helpers.
    34	 *
    35	 *  Single source of truth for the three NEW standalone (non-upgradeable) v5.4
    36	 *  contracts and the per-xPNTs X402Facilitator wiring loop. Inherited by every
    37	 *  deploy path so they cannot drift:
    38	 *    - DeployAnvil  (local / CI / run_full_regression)
    39	 *    - DeployLive   (fresh GA deploy)
    40	 *    - UpgradeLive  (standard `./deploy-core <env>` upgrade path — idempotent)
    41	 *    - DeployV54    (the proven Sepolia one-shot redeploy-bus; now thin wrapper)
    42	 *
    43	 *  The three contracts:
    44	 *    1. X402Facilitator   — x402 settlement lifted out of SuperPaymaster.
    45	 *                           ctor(IRegistry registry, IxPNTsFactory factory). owner = deployer.
    46	 *    2. TimelockController — OZ governance for PolicyRegistry. minDelay = 2 days.
    47	 *                           proposers/executors/admin = governor (deployer for bootstrap).
    48	 *    3. PolicyRegistry     — sender-keyed DVT spend policy.
    49	 *                           ctor(timelock, guardian, initialConsumer = SuperPaymaster proxy).
    50	 *
    51	 *  SuperPaymaster does NOT call PolicyRegistry yet — layer-1 enforcement is consumed
    52	 *  post-deploy by airaccount-contract#110 via POLICY_REGISTRY_ADDRESS. SP is already
    53	 *  authorized as initialConsumer at PolicyRegistry construction, so no SP -> PolicyRegistry
    54	 *  wiring is performed here.
    55	 */
    56	abstract contract V54Bootstrap is DefaultArtifacts {
    57	    // ERC-1967 implementation slot: bytes32(uint256(keccak256("eip1967.proxy.implementation")) - 1)
    58	    bytes32 internal constant V54_IMPL_SLOT =
    59	        0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc;
    60	
    61	    uint256 internal constant TIMELOCK_MIN_DELAY = 2 days;
    62	    uint256 internal constant FACILITATOR_DAILY_CAP = 10_000 ether;
    63	
    64	    struct V54Addresses {
    65	        address facilitator;
    66	        address timelock;
    67	        address policyRegistry;
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    /// @dev Resolve governance principals. Bootstrap defaults both to the deployer;
    71	    ///      override with GOVERNOR_ADDRESS / GUARDIAN_ADDRESS to point at the real
    72	    ///      2-of-3 multisig. Transferred to multisig as a post-deploy manual step
    73	    ///      (see docs/deployment/v5.4-launch-operations.md).
    74	    function _resolveGovernance(address deployer)
    75	        internal
    76	        view
    77	        returns (address governor, address guardian)
    78	    {
    79	        governor = vm.envOr("GOVERNOR_ADDRESS", deployer);
    80	        guardian = vm.envOr("GUARDIAN_ADDRESS", deployer);
    81	    }
    82	
    83	    /// @dev Deploy the three NEW v5.4 contracts. MUST be called inside an active broadcast.
    84	    ///      `existingTimelock` lets UpgradeLive reuse an already-deployed timelock when only
    85	    ///      PolicyRegistry/Facilitator are missing; pass address(0) to deploy a fresh one.
    86	    function _deployV54Contracts(
    87	        address registryProxy,
    88	        address spProxy,
    89	        address xpntsFactory,
    90	        address governor,
    91	        address guardian,
    92	        address existingTimelock
    93	    ) internal returns (V54Addresses memory a) {
    94	        // --- 1. X402Facilitator (NEW, non-upgradeable). owner = broadcaster. ---
    95	        // T-4: profile.default artifact by explicit path (see DefaultArtifacts.sol)
    96	        X402Facilitator facilitator = X402Facilitator(_deployDefault(
    97	            "X402Facilitator", abi.encode(registryProxy, xpntsFactory)
    98	        ));
    99	        a.facilitator = address(facilitator);
   100	        console.log("  [v5.4] X402Facilitator:", a.facilitator);
   101	        console.log("         version:", facilitator.version());
   102	        console.log("         owner:  ", facilitator.owner());
   103	
   104	        // Hand ownership to the resolved governor ATOMICALLY at deploy time when
   105	        // governance-at-deploy is configured. Leaving X402Facilitator owned by a single
   106	        // deployer EOA on mainnet is a security hole; this closes it for the
   107	        // GOVERNOR_ADDRESS path. Bootstrap (governor == deployer) is a no-op.
   108	        _transferFacilitatorOwnership(a.facilitator, governor);
   109	
   110	        // --- 2. TimelockController (NEW unless reusing existing). minDelay = 2 days. ---
   111	        if (existingTimelock != address(0)) {
   112	            a.timelock = existingTimelock;
   113	            console.log("  [v5.4] TimelockController (reused):", a.timelock);
   114	        } else {
   115	            address[] memory proposers = new address[](1);
   116	            proposers[0] = governor;
   117	            address[] memory executors = new address[](1);
   118	            executors[0] = governor;
   119	            // T-4: profile.default artifact; admin = governor (bootstrap; renounce/transfer to multisig post-deploy)
   120	            TimelockController timelock = TimelockController(payable(_deployDefault(
   121	                "TimelockController", abi.encode(TIMELOCK_MIN_DELAY, proposers, executors, governor)
   122	            )));
   123	            a.timelock = address(timelock);
   124	            console.log("  [v5.4] TimelockController:", a.timelock);
   125	            console.log("         minDelay (s):", TIMELOCK_MIN_DELAY);
   126	            console.log("         proposer/executor/admin:", governor);
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        // --- 3. PolicyRegistry (NEW, non-upgradeable). SP = initialConsumer. ---
   130	        PolicyRegistry policyRegistry = PolicyRegistry(_deployDefault(
   131	            "PolicyRegistry", abi.encode(a.timelock, guardian, spProxy)
   132	        ));
   133	        a.policyRegistry = address(policyRegistry);
   134	        console.log("  [v5.4] PolicyRegistry:", a.policyRegistry);
   135	        console.log("         version: ", policyRegistry.version());
   136	        console.log("         timelock:", policyRegistry.timelock());
   137	        console.log("         guardian:", policyRegistry.guardian());
   138	        console.log("         SP authorized:", policyRegistry.isAuthorizedConsumer(spProxy));
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    /// @dev Transfer X402Facilitator ownership to `governor` when governance-at-deploy is
   142	    ///      configured (governor set AND != the current owner / deployer). MUST run inside the
   143	    ///      active broadcast by the current owner. On bootstrap (governor == deployer, i.e.
   144	    ///      GOVERNOR_ADDRESS unset) it is a no-op and ownership stays on the deployer, to be
   145	    ///      handed to the multisig post-deploy per docs/deployment/v5.4-launch-operations.md.
   146	    ///      Shared by every deploy path (DeployLive / DeployAnvil / DeployV54 via
   147	    ///      _deployV54Contracts, and UpgradeLive's first-deploy needFac branch) so they cannot
   148	    ///      drift and a mainnet deploy never leaves the facilitator on a single deployer EOA.
   149	    function _transferFacilitatorOwnership(address facilitator, address governor) internal {
   150	        address current = X402Facilitator(facilitator).owner();
   151	        if (governor != address(0) && governor != current) {
   152	            X402Facilitator(facilitator).transferOwnership(governor);
   153	            console.log("  [v5.4] X402Facilitator owner -> governor (atomic at deploy):", governor);
   154	        } else {
   155	            console.log("  [v5.4] X402Facilitator owner kept on deployer (no GOVERNOR_ADDRESS):", current);
   156	        }
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    /// @dev Best-effort wiring loop. Authorizes `facilitator` on every factory-minted xPNTs
   160	    ///      token the broadcaster (`deployer`) owns. NEVER queues a reverting broadcast tx:
   161	    ///        - a communityOwner mismatch is skipped+logged;
   162	    ///        - each setter is gated on a low-level staticcall probe of its paired getter,
   163	    ///          so a token whose ON-CHAIN bytecode predates a v5.4 setter (e.g. clones still
   164	    ///          on XPNTs-3.4.0, which lacks setSpenderDailyCapFor) is logged as a manual
   165	    ///          follow-up instead of issuing a doomed tx that fails forge's broadcast
   166	    ///          re-simulation (and would burn gas / a nonce on real broadcast).
   167	    ///      Staticcall probes are view-only and are NOT recorded as broadcast transactions.
   168	    ///      On a FRESH deploy the factory-cloned xPNTs are new-bytecode, so wiring succeeds.
   169	    function _wireFacilitator(address xpntsFactory, address facilitator, address deployer) internal {
   170	        address[] memory tokens = IXPNTsFactoryEnum(xpntsFactory).getAllTokens();
   171	        console.log("");
   172	        console.log("  [v5.4] Wiring X402Facilitator on", tokens.length, "xPNTs token(s)");
   173	
   174	        for (uint256 i; i < tokens.length; ++i) {
   175	            address token = tokens[i];
   176	            IXPNTsWiring t = IXPNTsWiring(token);
   177	
   178	            // communityOwner() exists on all xPNTs versions; guard anyway.
   179	            (bool ownerOk, bytes memory ownerRet) =
   180	                token.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature("communityOwner()"));
   181	            if (!ownerOk || ownerRet.length < 32) {
   182	                console.log("    SKIP token (no communityOwner getter):", token);
   183	                continue;
   184	            }
   185	            address owner = abi.decode(ownerRet, (address));
   186	            if (owner != deployer) {
   187	                console.log("    SKIP token:", token);
   188	                console.log("      communityOwner is not deployer:", owner);
   189	                console.log("      -> manual wiring required by communityOwner");
   190	                continue;
   191	            }
   192	
   193	            bool complete = true;
   194	
   195	            // (a) addAutoApprovedSpender — paired getter: autoApprovedSpenders(address).
   196	            (bool b1, bytes memory r1) =
   197	                token.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature("autoApprovedSpenders(address)", facilitator));
   198	            if (b1 && r1.length >= 32) {
   199	                if (!abi.decode(r1, (bool))) {
   200	                    t.addAutoApprovedSpender(facilitator);
   201	                }
   202	            } else {
   203	                complete = false;
   204	                console.log("      ! addAutoApprovedSpender unsupported (old bytecode)");
   205	            }
   206	
   207	            // (b) setSpenderDailyCapFor — paired getter: spenderDailyCapOverride(address).
   208	            //     Reverts on XPNTs-3.4.0; gate on the getter so we never queue a failing tx.
   209	            (bool b2,) =
   210	                token.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature("spenderDailyCapOverride(address)", facilitator));
   211	            if (b2) {
   212	                t.setSpenderDailyCapFor(facilitator, FACILITATOR_DAILY_CAP);
   213	            } else {
   214	                complete = false;
   215	                console.log("      ! setSpenderDailyCapFor unsupported (old bytecode)");
   216	            }
   217	
   218	            // (c) addApprovedFacilitator — paired getter: approvedFacilitators(address).
   219	            (bool b3, bytes memory r3) =
   220	                token.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature("approvedFacilitators(address)", facilitator));
   221	            if (b3 && r3.length >= 32) {
   222	                if (!abi.decode(r3, (bool))) {
   223	                    t.addApprovedFacilitator(facilitator);
   224	                }
   225	            } else {
   226	                complete = false;
   227	                console.log("      ! addApprovedFacilitator unsupported (old bytecode)");
   228	            }
   229	
   230	            if (complete) {
   231	                console.log("    WIRED token:", token);
   232	            } else {
   233	                console.log("    PARTIAL token (redeploy/upgrade xPNTs, then manual wiring):", token);
   234	            }
   235	        }
   236	    }
   237	
   238	    /// @dev Wire X402Facilitator on a SINGLE freshly-created token. Used by TestAccountPrepare,
   239	    ///      where the broadcaster is the token's communityOwner. Idempotent + staticcall-gated.
   240	    ///      Returns true when all three authorizations are in place (fresh-bytecode tokens).
   241	    function _wireFacilitatorForToken(address token, address facilitator) internal returns (bool complete) {
   242	        IXPNTsWiring t = IXPNTsWiring(token);
   243	        complete = true;
   244	
   245	        (bool b1, bytes memory r1) =
   246	            token.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature("autoApprovedSpenders(address)", facilitator));
   247	        if (b1 && r1.length >= 32) {
   248	            if (!abi.decode(r1, (bool))) t.addAutoApprovedSpender(facilitator);
   249	        } else {
   250	            complete = false;
   251	        }
   252	
   253	        (bool b2,) =
   254	            token.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature("spenderDailyCapOverride(address)", facilitator));
   255	        if (b2) {
   256	            t.setSpenderDailyCapFor(facilitator, FACILITATOR_DAILY_CAP);
   257	        } else {
   258	            complete = false;
   259	        }
   260	
   261	        (bool b3, bytes memory r3) =
   262	            token.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature("approvedFacilitators(address)", facilitator));
   263	        if (b3 && r3.length >= 32) {
   264	            if (!abi.decode(r3, (bool))) t.addApprovedFacilitator(facilitator);
   265	        } else {
   266	            complete = false;
   267	        }
   268	    }
   269	
   270	    function _implOf(address proxy) internal view returns (address) {
   271	        return address(uint160(uint256(vm.load(proxy, V54_IMPL_SLOT))));
   272	    }
   273	
   274	    /// @dev Append `\n` only if the file doesn't already end in one. Idempotent.
   275	    function _ensureTrailingNewline(string memory path) internal {
   276	        bytes memory content = bytes(vm.readFile(path));
   277	        if (content.length == 0) return;
   278	        if (content[content.length - 1] == 0x0a) return;
   279	        vm.writeFile(path, string.concat(string(content), "\n"));
   280	    }
   281	
   282	    /// @dev Parse an optional address key from a config JSON; returns address(0) if absent.
   283	    function _optAddr(string memory config, string memory key) internal view returns (address) {
   284	        try vm.parseJsonAddress(config, key) returns (address a) {
   285	            return a;
   286	        } catch {
   287	            return address(0);
   288	        }
   289	    }
   290	}
     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
