     4	import { SPReleaseVersion } from "./SPReleaseVersion.sol";
     5	import "forge-std/Script.sol";
     6	import "forge-std/console.sol";
     7	import {DefaultArtifacts} from "./DefaultArtifacts.sol";
     8	import { AOAProtocolRegistry } from "src/tokens/v2/AOAProtocolRegistry.sol";
     9	import { GlobalTierSource } from "src/tokens/v2/GlobalTierSource.sol";
    10	import { xPNTsTokenV2 } from "src/tokens/v2/xPNTsTokenV2.sol";
    11	import { xPNTsTokenV2Ext } from "src/tokens/v2/xPNTsTokenV2Ext.sol";
    12	import { xPNTsFactoryV2 } from "src/tokens/v2/xPNTsFactoryV2.sol";
    13	import { SuperPaymasterLens } from "src/paymasters/superpaymaster/v3/SuperPaymasterLens.sol";
    14	
    15	/// @dev Functions of an xPNTs v2 token that live in the EXTENSION (reached through the core's
    16	///      fallback), plus the core views the scripts read back. One address serves both.
    17	interface IxPNTsV2Script {
    18	    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external;
    19	    function balanceOf(address) external view returns (uint256);
    20	    function lockedOf(address) external view returns (uint256);
    21	    function creditReservedOf(address) external view returns (uint256);
    22	    function debts(address) external view returns (uint256);
    23	    function communityOwner() external view returns (address);
    24	    function community() external view returns (address);
    25	    function FACTORY() external view returns (address);
    26	    function SUPERPAYMASTER_ADDRESS() external view returns (address);
    27	    function BALANCE_MODE_VERSION() external view returns (uint16);
    28	    function exchangeRate() external view returns (uint256);
    29	    function creditTierSource() external view returns (address);
    30	    function creditPolicy() external view returns (uint8);
    31	    function usedOpHashes(bytes32) external view returns (bool);
    32	    function version() external view returns (string memory);
    33	}
    34	
    35	/// @dev Minimal SP surface used by the v5.5.0 helpers (kept as an interface so this file does not
    36	///      pull SuperPaymaster's full closure into every script that only needs the helpers).
    37	interface ISPV55Script {
    38	    function version() external view returns (string memory);
    39	    function xpntsFactory() external view returns (address);
    40	    function setXPNTsFactory(address) external;
    41	    function configureOperator(address xPNTsToken, address treasury) external;
    42	    function updatePrice() external;
    43	    function cachedPrice() external view returns (int256 price, uint256 updatedAt, uint80 roundId, uint8 decimals);
    44	    function priceStalenessThreshold() external view returns (uint256);
    45	    function operators(address operator) external view returns (
    46	        uint128 aPNTsBalance, bool isConfigured, bool isPaused, address xPNTsToken,
    47	        uint32 reputation, uint48 minTxInterval, address treasury, uint256 totalSpent, uint256 totalTxSponsored
    48	    );
    49	}
    50	
    51	/**
    52	 * @title V55Bootstrap
    53	 * @notice Shared deploy / read-back helpers for the SuperPaymaster 5.5.0 + xPNTs v2 stack
    54	 *         (spec docs/design/aoa-balance-mode/03-final-spec.md §6 runbook step 4, 6, 7a, 7c).
    55	 * @dev    Order (mirrors contracts/test/helpers/V2TokenDeployer.sol):
    56	 *           GlobalTierSource(registry)
    57	 *           AOAProtocolRegistry(owner) -> bootstrapApprove(KIND_SP, spKey(SP))
    58	 *                                     -> bootstrapApprove(KIND_TIER_SOURCE, tierSource.codehash)
    59	 *                                     -> seal()
    60	 *           xPNTsTokenV2Ext(aoa) -> xPNTsTokenV2(aoa, ext) [template]
    61	 *           xPNTsFactoryV2(SP, registry, template, tierSource)
    62	 *           SuperPaymasterLens
    63	 *         then SP.setXPNTsFactory(factoryV2) (owner) and per community
    64	 *         factoryV2.deployxPNTsToken(...) + SP.configureOperator(v2Token, treasury).
    65	 *
    66	 *         Every `_ensure*` helper is idempotent: a component whose address is supplied AND whose
    67	 *         read-back matches is reused; anything else is (re)deployed. Every step READS BACK and
    68	 *         `require`s the state it claims — a call that silently no-ops fails the script.
    69	 */
    70	abstract contract V55Bootstrap is DefaultArtifacts {
    71	    string internal constant SP_V55_VERSION = SPReleaseVersion.SP;
    72	    string internal constant XPNTS_V2_VERSION = "XPNTs-4.0.1";
    73	    string internal constant FACTORY_V2_VERSION = "xPNTsFactory-3.0.1-v2";
    74	    string internal constant AOA_REG_VERSION = "AOAProtocolRegistry-1.0.0";
    75	    string internal constant TIER_SOURCE_VERSION = "GlobalTierSource-1.0.0";
    76	    string internal constant LENS_VERSION = SPReleaseVersion.LENS;
    77	
    78	    struct V55Stack {
    79	        address tierSource;
    80	        address aoaRegistry;
    81	        address ext;
    82	        address impl;
    83	        address factory;
    84	        address lens;
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    88	    // Step 4: deploy (or resume) the v2 stack
    89	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    90	
    91	    /// @notice Deploy or resume the stack. MUST run inside a broadcast whose sender == `owner`
    92	    ///         (the AOA registry owner bootstraps the approvals; the factory owner wires SP).
    93	    /// @param prev   addresses from a previous (possibly partial) run; zero = deploy fresh
    94	    /// @param sp     SuperPaymaster PROXY address (KIND_SP is address-keyed, §10.5)
    95	    /// @param registry Registry proxy (GlobalTierSource reads Registry.getCreditLimit)
    96	    /// @param owner  broadcaster; becomes owner of the AOA registry and the factory
    97	    function _ensureV55Stack(V55Stack memory prev, address sp, address registry, address owner)
    98	        internal
    99	        returns (V55Stack memory st)
   100	    {
   101	        require(sp != address(0) && registry != address(0), "V55: sp/registry = 0");
   102	
   103	        // (1) GlobalTierSource — stateless, immutable REGISTRY
   104	        if (_hasCode(prev.tierSource) && address(GlobalTierSource(prev.tierSource).REGISTRY()) == registry) {
   105	            st.tierSource = prev.tierSource;
   106	            console.log("  [v55] reuse GlobalTierSource   ", st.tierSource);
   107	        } else {
   108	            st.tierSource = _deployDefault("GlobalTierSource", abi.encode(registry));
   109	            console.log("  [v55] new   GlobalTierSource   ", st.tierSource);
   110	        }
   111	
   112	        // (2) AOAProtocolRegistry — bootstrap approvals, then seal
   113	        AOAProtocolRegistry aoa;
   114	        if (_hasCode(prev.aoaRegistry)) {
   115	            aoa = AOAProtocolRegistry(prev.aoaRegistry);
   116	            console.log("  [v55] reuse AOAProtocolRegistry", address(aoa));
   117	        } else {
   118	            aoa = AOAProtocolRegistry(_deployDefault("AOAProtocolRegistry", abi.encode(owner)));
   119	            console.log("  [v55] new   AOAProtocolRegistry", address(aoa));
   120	        }
   121	        st.aoaRegistry = address(aoa);
   122	        bytes32 tierHash = st.tierSource.codehash;
   123	        bool spOk = aoa.approved(aoa.KIND_SP(), aoa.spKey(sp));
   124	        bool tierOk = aoa.approved(aoa.KIND_TIER_SOURCE(), tierHash);
   125	        if (!aoa.sealed_()) {
   126	            require(aoa.owner() == owner, "V55: AOA registry owner != broadcaster (cannot bootstrap)");
   127	            if (!spOk) aoa.bootstrapApprove(aoa.KIND_SP(), aoa.spKey(sp));
   128	            if (!tierOk) aoa.bootstrapApprove(aoa.KIND_TIER_SOURCE(), tierHash);
   129	            aoa.seal();
   130	        } else {
   131	            // Sealed: additions need the 48 h proposeApproval/executeApproval path. Refuse to
   132	            // pretend — a resumed run with a missing approval must be fixed by hand.
   133	            require(spOk, "V55: AOA registry sealed WITHOUT SP approval (needs 48h proposeApproval)");
   134	            require(tierOk, "V55: AOA registry sealed WITHOUT tier-source approval (needs 48h proposeApproval)");
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        // (3) extension + (4) core template, both bound to this AOA registry
   138	        if (_hasCode(prev.ext) && address(xPNTsTokenV2Ext(prev.ext).PROTOCOL_REGISTRY()) == st.aoaRegistry) {
   139	            st.ext = prev.ext;
   140	            console.log("  [v55] reuse xPNTsTokenV2Ext    ", st.ext);
   141	        } else {
   142	            st.ext = _deployDefault("xPNTsTokenV2Ext", abi.encode(st.aoaRegistry));
   143	            console.log("  [v55] new   xPNTsTokenV2Ext    ", st.ext);
   144	        }
   145	        if (
   146	            _hasCode(prev.impl) && xPNTsTokenV2(prev.impl).EXTENSION() == st.ext
   147	                && address(xPNTsTokenV2(prev.impl).PROTOCOL_REGISTRY()) == st.aoaRegistry
   148	        ) {
   149	            st.impl = prev.impl;
   150	            console.log("  [v55] reuse xPNTsTokenV2 impl  ", st.impl);
   151	        } else {
   152	            st.impl = _deployDefault("xPNTsTokenV2", abi.encode(st.aoaRegistry, st.ext));
   153	            console.log("  [v55] new   xPNTsTokenV2 impl  ", st.impl);
   154	        }
   155	
   156	        // (5) factory: SUPERPAYMASTER = SP, template, default tier source
   157	        if (
   158	            _hasCode(prev.factory) && xPNTsFactoryV2(prev.factory).implementation() == st.impl
   159	                && xPNTsFactoryV2(prev.factory).REGISTRY() == registry
   160	        ) {
   161	            st.factory = prev.factory;
   162	            console.log("  [v55] reuse xPNTsFactoryV2     ", st.factory);
   163	            xPNTsFactoryV2 f = xPNTsFactoryV2(st.factory);
   164	            if (f.SUPERPAYMASTER() != sp) f.setSuperPaymasterAddress(sp);
   165	            if (f.defaultTierSource() != st.tierSource) f.setDefaultTierSource(st.tierSource);
   166	        } else {
   167	            st.factory = _deployDefault("xPNTsFactoryV2", abi.encode(sp, registry, st.impl, st.tierSource));
   168	            console.log("  [v55] new   xPNTsFactoryV2     ", st.factory);
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        // (6) lens (F1: dryRunValidation moved out of SP)
   172	        if (_hasCode(prev.lens) && _strEq(SuperPaymasterLens(prev.lens).version(), LENS_VERSION)) {
   173	            st.lens = prev.lens;
   174	            console.log("  [v55] reuse SuperPaymasterLens ", st.lens);
   175	        } else {
   176	            st.lens = _deployDefault("SuperPaymasterLens", "");
   177	            console.log("  [v55] new   SuperPaymasterLens ", st.lens);
   178	        }
   179	    }
   180	
   181	    /// @notice Read back every property step 4 claims (D5-plan §4 step 4). View-only.
   182	    /// @dev    The template's runtime code is compared byte-for-byte with the compiled artifact
   183	    ///         after masking the immutable ranges the compiler reports (`_artifactMatch`). A
   184	    ///         reference-instance codehash cannot be used: the template carries `address(this)`
   185	    ///         immutables (EIP-712 domain cache), so two correct instances never share a codehash.
   186	    ///         Constructor arguments are checked separately through the immutable getters.
   187	    function _verifyV55Stack(V55Stack memory st, address sp, address registry) internal view {
   188	        AOAProtocolRegistry aoa = AOAProtocolRegistry(st.aoaRegistry);
   189	        require(_strEq(aoa.version(), AOA_REG_VERSION), "V55 readback: AOA registry version");
   190	        require(aoa.sealed_(), "V55 readback: AOA registry not sealed");
   191	        require(aoa.isApprovedSP(sp), "V55 readback: SP proxy not approved (KIND_SP)");
   192	        require(aoa.isApprovedImpl(aoa.KIND_TIER_SOURCE(), st.tierSource), "V55 readback: tier source not approved");
   193	
   194	        require(_strEq(GlobalTierSource(st.tierSource).version(), TIER_SOURCE_VERSION), "V55 readback: tier version");
   195	        require(address(GlobalTierSource(st.tierSource).REGISTRY()) == registry, "V55 readback: tier REGISTRY");
   196	
   197	        require(address(xPNTsTokenV2Ext(st.ext).PROTOCOL_REGISTRY()) == st.aoaRegistry, "V55 readback: ext registry");
   198	        xPNTsTokenV2 impl = xPNTsTokenV2(st.impl);
   199	        require(impl.EXTENSION() == st.ext, "V55 readback: template EXTENSION");
   200	        require(address(impl.PROTOCOL_REGISTRY()) == st.aoaRegistry, "V55 readback: template registry");
   201	        require(_strEq(impl.version(), XPNTS_V2_VERSION), "V55 readback: template version");
   202	        require(impl.BALANCE_MODE_VERSION() == 1, "V55 readback: BALANCE_MODE_VERSION");
   203	        // AUD-4 / R10-M5: every 5.5.0 contract must run the profile.default (runs=500) bytes —
   204	        // the ones that were measured and audited — never the registry-size variant. Also covers
   205	        // components REUSED by a resumed run, which `_deployDefault` never touched.
   206	        _requireDefaultArtifact(st.tierSource, "GlobalTierSource");
   207	        _requireDefaultArtifact(st.aoaRegistry, "AOAProtocolRegistry");
   208	        _requireDefaultArtifact(st.ext, "xPNTsTokenV2Ext");
   209	        _requireDefaultArtifact(st.impl, "xPNTsTokenV2");
   210	        _requireDefaultArtifact(st.factory, "xPNTsFactoryV2");
   211	        _requireDefaultArtifact(st.lens, "SuperPaymasterLens");
   212	
   213	        xPNTsFactoryV2 f = xPNTsFactoryV2(st.factory);
   214	        require(_strEq(f.version(), FACTORY_V2_VERSION), "V55 readback: factory version");
   215	        require(f.SUPERPAYMASTER() == sp, "V55 readback: factory.SUPERPAYMASTER != SP");
   216	        require(f.REGISTRY() == registry, "V55 readback: factory.REGISTRY");
   217	        require(f.implementation() == st.impl, "V55 readback: factory.implementation");
   218	        require(f.defaultTierSource() == st.tierSource, "V55 readback: factory.defaultTierSource");
   219	
   220	        require(_strEq(SuperPaymasterLens(st.lens).version(), LENS_VERSION), "V55 readback: lens version");
   221	        require(
   222	            SuperPaymasterLens(st.lens).EXPECTED_SP_VERSION() == keccak256(bytes(SP_V55_VERSION)),
   223	            "V55 readback: lens bound to another SP version"
   224	        );
   225	        console.log("  [v55] step-4 read-back OK (sealed registry, approvals, template codehash, factory->SP)");
   226	    }
   227	
   228	    // _deployDefault / _requireDefaultArtifact / _codeEqArtifact / _artifactMatch live in
   229	    // DefaultArtifacts.sol (shared with the legacy deploy paths, T-4).
   230	
   231	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   232	    // Step 6: point SP at factoryV2
   233	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   234	
   235	    function _wireSPFactory(address sp, address factory) internal {
   236	        if (ISPV55Script(sp).xpntsFactory() != factory) ISPV55Script(sp).setXPNTsFactory(factory);
   237	    }
   238	
   239	    function _verifySPFactory(address sp, address factory) internal view {
   240	        require(ISPV55Script(sp).xpntsFactory() == factory, "V55 readback: SP.xpntsFactory != factoryV2");
   241	    }
   242	
   243	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   244	    // Step 7a: issue a v2 community token (broadcast as the COMMUNITY)
   245	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   246	
   247	    function _issueV2Token(
   248	        address factory,
   249	        address community,
   250	        string memory name_,
   251	        string memory symbol_,
   252	        string memory communityName,
   253	        string memory ens,
   254	        uint256 rate
   255	    ) internal returns (address token) {
   256	        token = xPNTsFactoryV2(factory).getTokenAddress(community);
   257	        if (token == address(0)) {
   258	            token = xPNTsFactoryV2(factory).deployxPNTsToken(name_, symbol_, communityName, ens, rate, address(0));
   259	            console.log("  [v55] v2 token issued:", symbol_, token);
   260	        } else {
   261	            console.log("  [v55] v2 token reused:", symbol_, token);
   262	        }
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    /// @notice D5-plan §4 step 7a read-back.
   266	    function _verifyV2Token(address token, address factory, address community, address sp) internal view {
   267	        xPNTsFactoryV2 f = xPNTsFactoryV2(factory);
   268	        IxPNTsV2Script t = IxPNTsV2Script(token);
   269	        require(f.getTokenAddress(community) == token, "V55 readback: factory mapping");
   270	        require(f.isXPNTs(token), "V55 readback: factory isXPNTs");
   271	        // EIP-1167 clone resolved to its embedded implementation (AOAProtocolRegistry.implCodehash).
   272	        require(
   273	            AOAProtocolRegistry(_aoaOf(factory)).implCodehash(token) == f.implementation().codehash,
   274	            "V55 readback: token clone codehash != template"
   275	        );
   276	        require(t.BALANCE_MODE_VERSION() == 1, "V55 readback: token BALANCE_MODE_VERSION");
   277	        require(_strEq(t.version(), XPNTS_V2_VERSION), "V55 readback: token version");
   278	        require(t.FACTORY() == factory, "V55 readback: token FACTORY");
   279	        require(t.community() == community, "V55 readback: token community");
   280	        require(t.SUPERPAYMASTER_ADDRESS() == sp, "V55 readback: token genesis SP");
   281	        require(t.creditTierSource() == f.defaultTierSource(), "V55 readback: token default tier source");
   282	        require(t.creditPolicy() == 0, "V55 readback: token creditPolicy must start OFF");
   283	    }
   284	
   285	    function _aoaOf(address factory) internal view returns (address) {
   286	        return address(xPNTsTokenV2(xPNTsFactoryV2(factory).implementation()).PROTOCOL_REGISTRY());
   287	    }
   288	
   289	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   290	    // Step 7c: price cache, then configureOperator (broadcast as the OPERATOR)
   291	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   292	
   293	    /// @notice DSR D3 §8(a): with a zero/expired price cache validate reverts (AA33) or returns an
   294	    ///         expired validUntil (AA32). Refresh and read back BEFORE any operator goes live.
   295	    function _refreshAndCheckPrice(address sp) internal {
   296	        ISPV55Script(sp).updatePrice();
   297	    }
   298	
   299	    function _verifyPriceFresh(address sp) internal view {
   300	        (int256 price, uint256 updatedAt,,) = ISPV55Script(sp).cachedPrice();
   301	        require(price > 0, "V55 readback: cachedPrice.price == 0");
   302	        require(updatedAt > 0, "V55 readback: cachedPrice.updatedAt == 0");
   303	        require(updatedAt <= block.timestamp, "V55 readback: cachedPrice.updatedAt is in the future");
   304	        require(
   305	            updatedAt + ISPV55Script(sp).priceStalenessThreshold() > block.timestamp,
   306	            "V55 readback: cachedPrice expired"
   307	        );
   308	    }
   309	
   310	    function _configureOperatorV2(address sp, address token, address treasury, address operator) internal {
   311	        (, bool cfg,, address cur,,,,,) = ISPV55Script(sp).operators(operator);
   312	        if (!cfg || cur != token) ISPV55Script(sp).configureOperator(token, treasury);
   313	    }
   314	
   315	    function _verifyOperatorV2(address sp, address operator, address token, address treasury) internal view {
   316	        (, bool cfg,, address cur,,, address tr,,) = ISPV55Script(sp).operators(operator);
   317	        require(cfg, "V55 readback: operator not configured");
   318	        require(cur == token, "V55 readback: operator token != v2 token");
   319	        require(tr == treasury, "V55 readback: operator treasury");
   320	        require(IxPNTsV2Script(token).BALANCE_MODE_VERSION() == 1, "V55 readback: operator token is not v2");
   321	    }
   322	
   323	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   324	    // paymasterAndData (SP 5.5.0, spec §3.2)
   325	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   326	
   327	    /// @notice [paymaster 20][verifGas 16][postOpGas 16][operator 20][maxRate 32][token 20][flags 1]
   328	    function _pmdV55(address sp, uint128 verifGas, uint128 postOpGas, address operator, uint256 maxRate, address token, uint8 flags)
   329	        internal
   330	        pure
   331	        returns (bytes memory)
   332	    {
   333	        return abi.encodePacked(sp, verifGas, postOpGas, operator, maxRate, token, flags);
   334	    }
   335	
   336	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   337	    // utils
   338	    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   339	
   340	    function _hasCode(address a) internal view returns (bool) {
   341	        return a != address(0) && a.code.length > 0;
   342	    }
   343	
   344	    function _strEq(string memory a, string memory b) internal pure returns (bool) {
   345	        return keccak256(bytes(a)) == keccak256(bytes(b));
   346	    }
   347	
   348	    function _optAddrV55(string memory json, string memory key) internal view returns (address) {
   349	        if (!vm.keyExistsJson(json, key)) return address(0);
   350	        try vm.parseJsonAddress(json, key) returns (address a) {
   351	            return a;
   352	        } catch {
   353	            return address(0);
   354	        }
   355	    }
   356	
   357	    function _stackFromConfig(string memory json) internal view returns (V55Stack memory st) {
   358	        st.tierSource = _optAddrV55(json, ".globalTierSource");
   359	        st.aoaRegistry = _optAddrV55(json, ".aoaProtocolRegistry");
   360	        st.ext = _optAddrV55(json, ".xPNTsTokenV2Ext");
   361	        st.impl = _optAddrV55(json, ".xPNTsTokenV2Impl");
   362	        st.factory = _optAddrV55(json, ".xPNTsFactoryV2");
   363	        st.lens = _optAddrV55(json, ".superPaymasterLens");
   364	    }
   365	
   366	    /// @notice Patch the six v2 keys into an existing config file WITHOUT removing any key.
   367	    function _writeStackKeys(string memory path, V55Stack memory st) internal {
   368	        vm.writeJson(vm.toString(st.aoaRegistry), path, ".aoaProtocolRegistry");
   369	        vm.writeJson(vm.toString(st.tierSource), path, ".globalTierSource");
   370	        vm.writeJson(vm.toString(st.ext), path, ".xPNTsTokenV2Ext");
   371	        vm.writeJson(vm.toString(st.impl), path, ".xPNTsTokenV2Impl");
   372	        vm.writeJson(vm.toString(st.factory), path, ".xPNTsFactoryV2");
   373	        vm.writeJson(vm.toString(st.lens), path, ".superPaymasterLens");
   374	    }
   375	}

   274	    /// @dev Append `\n` only if the file doesn't already end in one. Idempotent.
   275	    function _ensureTrailingNewline(string memory path) internal {
   276	        bytes memory content = bytes(vm.readFile(path));
   277	        if (content.length == 0) return;
   278	        if (content[content.length - 1] == 0x0a) return;
   279	        vm.writeFile(path, string.concat(string(content), "\n"));
   280	    }

     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     2	pragma solidity ^0.8.26;
     3	
     4	import "forge-std/Script.sol";
     5	import "forge-std/console.sol";
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * @title DefaultArtifacts
     9	 * @notice "Tested == deployed" (AUD-4 / R10-M5 / T-4): deploy every contract from its
    10	 *         profile.default compiler artifact BY EXPLICIT PATH and require the deployed runtime to
    11	 *         equal that artifact (immutable ranges masked).
    12	 * @dev    Why: foundry.toml's Registry `compilation_restrictions` compiles any source file that
    13	 *         imports Registry.sol — and its whole closure — under the runs=200 "registry-size"
    14	 *         compiler profile. Every deploy script imports Registry.sol, so a plain `new X(...)` in a
    15	 *         script ships the runs=200 bytes, not the runs=500 bytes the test suite and the size
    16	 *         evidence were measured on.
    17	 *
    18	 *         Which file is "the default artifact" is NOT decided by its name: forge names an artifact
    19	 *         `X.json` when only one profile built X and `X.default.json` / `X.registry-size.json` when
    20	 *         both did (and nests it, e.g. out/core/EntryPoint.sol/, when source names collide), and
    21	 *         that changes from build to build; the unsuffixed file is simply the LAST compile
    22	 *         (`forge test --evm-version prague` rewrites it with a Prague build). The resolver maps
    23	 *         each contract to its source file, tries every layout forge uses, and accepts only an
    24	 *         artifact whose OWN metadata matches [profile.default] exactly: compilation target,
    25	 *         solc 0.8.33, optimizer on with runs 500 (Registry: its compilation_restrictions 200),
    26	 *         viaIR, evmVersion cancun, and the source keccak256 == the source file now. Zero or
    27	 *         two different matching builds fail.
    28	 *
    29	 *         Run a plain `forge build` before any script using this (deploy-core, prepare-test and
    30	 *         audit-core do): `forge script` only compiles the script's own closure, so it does not
    31	 *         refresh the default artifacts.
    32	 */
    33	/// @notice Artifact lookups / comparisons, each executed in its OWN call frame.
    34	/// @dev    A script's run() is ONE call frame and Solidity never frees memory: reading and handing
    35	///         an artifact JSON to the parsing cheatcodes per check, dozens of times, grows that frame until
    36	///         the quadratic memory-expansion cost ends the script with MemoryOOG (observed after the
    37	///         5th contract). DefaultArtifacts therefore calls this separate helper through EXTERNAL
    38	///         VIEW calls (STATICCALL: never recorded as a broadcast transaction); each call's memory
    39	///         is released on return. (A self-call is not an option: forge rejects `address(this)` in
    40	///         a script contract.) The helper is created in the script's constructor, i.e. outside any
    41	///         broadcast, so it is never deployed on-chain.
    42	contract T4ArtifactReader is Script {
    43	    uint256 internal constant DEFAULT_RUNS = 500;
    44	    uint256 internal constant REGISTRY_RUNS = 200; // foundry.toml compilation_restrictions, every profile
    45	
    46	    struct ImmRef {
    47	        uint256 length; // JSON keys decode in alphabetical order
    48	        uint256 start;
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    function _expectedRuns(string memory name) internal pure returns (uint256) {
    52	        return keccak256(bytes(name)) == keccak256("Registry") ? REGISTRY_RUNS : DEFAULT_RUNS;
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    function _runsOf(string memory json) internal pure returns (uint256) {
    56	        try vm.parseJsonUint(json, ".metadata.settings.optimizer.runs") returns (uint256 r) {
    57	            return r;
    58	        } catch {
    59	            return 0;
    60	        }
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    function _tryRead(string memory rel) internal view returns (bool ok, string memory json) {
    64	        try vm.readFile(string.concat(vm.projectRoot(), "/", rel)) returns (string memory j) {
    65	            return (true, j);
    66	        } catch {
    67	            return (false, "");
    68	        }
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    /// @notice Source file of every contract the deploy scripts create. The artifact is identified
    72	    ///         by its compilation target (source) and optimizer runs, never by file name alone:
    73	    ///         names collide (three EntryPoint.sol in the tree → out/core/EntryPoint.sol/…) and the
    74	    ///         suffix scheme changes with which profiles happened to build a source.
    75	    function _sourceOf(string memory name) internal pure returns (string memory) {
    76	        bytes32 h = keccak256(bytes(name));
    77	        string memory OZ = "singleton-paymaster/lib/openzeppelin-contracts-v5.0.2/contracts/";
    78	        string memory AA = "singleton-paymaster/lib/account-abstraction-v7/contracts/";
    79	        if (h == keccak256("Registry")) return "contracts/src/core/Registry.sol";
    80	        if (h == keccak256("GTokenStaking")) return "contracts/src/core/GTokenStaking.sol";
    81	        if (h == keccak256("PolicyRegistry")) return "contracts/src/core/PolicyRegistry.sol";
    82	        if (h == keccak256("LivenessRegistry")) return "contracts/src/core/LivenessRegistry.sol";
    83	        if (h == keccak256("GTokenAuthorization")) return "contracts/src/tokens/GTokenAuthorization.sol";
    84	        if (h == keccak256("MySBT")) return "contracts/src/tokens/MySBT.sol";
    85	        if (h == keccak256("xPNTsFactory")) return "contracts/src/tokens/xPNTsFactory.sol";
    86	        if (h == keccak256("APNTsCapped")) return "contracts/src/tokens/APNTsCapped.sol";
    87	        if (h == keccak256("xPNTsToken")) return "contracts/src/tokens/xPNTsToken.sol";
    88	        if (h == keccak256("AOAProtocolRegistry")) return "contracts/src/tokens/v2/AOAProtocolRegistry.sol";
    89	        if (h == keccak256("GlobalTierSource")) return "contracts/src/tokens/v2/GlobalTierSource.sol";
    90	        if (h == keccak256("xPNTsTokenV2Ext")) return "contracts/src/tokens/v2/xPNTsTokenV2Ext.sol";
    91	        if (h == keccak256("xPNTsTokenV2")) return "contracts/src/tokens/v2/xPNTsTokenV2.sol";
    92	        if (h == keccak256("xPNTsFactoryV2")) return "contracts/src/tokens/v2/xPNTsFactoryV2.sol";
    93	        if (h == keccak256("ReputationSystem")) return "contracts/src/modules/reputation/ReputationSystem.sol";
    94	        if (h == keccak256("DVTValidator")) return "contracts/src/modules/monitoring/DVTValidator.sol";
    95	        if (h == keccak256("BLSAggregator")) return "contracts/src/modules/monitoring/BLSAggregator.sol";
    96	        if (h == keccak256("SuperPaymaster")) return "contracts/src/paymasters/superpaymaster/v3/SuperPaymaster.sol";
    97	        if (h == keccak256("SuperPaymasterAdmin")) return "contracts/src/paymasters/superpaymaster/v3/SuperPaymasterAdmin.sol";
    98	        if (h == keccak256("SuperPaymasterLens")) return "contracts/src/paymasters/superpaymaster/v3/SuperPaymasterLens.sol";
    99	        if (h == keccak256("MicroPaymentChannel")) return "contracts/src/paymasters/superpaymaster/v3/MicroPaymentChannel.sol";
   100	        if (h == keccak256("X402Facilitator")) return "contracts/src/paymasters/superpaymaster/v3/X402Facilitator.sol";
   101	        if (h == keccak256("PaymasterFactory")) return "contracts/src/paymasters/v4/core/PaymasterFactory.sol";
   102	        if (h == keccak256("Paymaster")) return "contracts/src/paymasters/v4/Paymaster.sol";
   103	        if (h == keccak256("MockAgentIdentityRegistry")) return "contracts/src/mocks/MockAgentIdentityRegistry.sol";
   104	        if (h == keccak256("MockAgentReputationRegistry")) return "contracts/src/mocks/MockAgentReputationRegistry.sol";
   105	        if (h == keccak256("AnvilMockPriceFeed")) return "contracts/test/helpers/AnvilMockPriceFeed.sol";
   106	        if (h == keccak256("ERC1967Proxy")) return string.concat(OZ, "proxy/ERC1967/ERC1967Proxy.sol");
   107	        if (h == keccak256("TimelockController")) return string.concat(OZ, "governance/TimelockController.sol");
   108	        if (h == keccak256("EntryPoint")) return string.concat(AA, "core/EntryPoint.sol");
   109	        if (h == keccak256("SimpleAccountFactory")) return string.concat(AA, "samples/SimpleAccountFactory.sol");
   110	        if (h == keccak256("SimpleAccount")) return string.concat(AA, "samples/SimpleAccount.sol");
   111	        revert(string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: no source mapping for ", name));
   112	    }
