   114	    function t4DefaultArtifact(string calldata name) external view returns (string memory) {
   115	        return _defaultArtifactImpl(name);
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    function t4CodeEqArtifact(address target, string calldata rel) external view returns (bool) {
   119	        return _codeEqArtifactImpl(target, rel);
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    /// @notice Number of distinct immutables (AST ids) in the artifact's runtime.
   123	    function t4ImmutableCount(string calldata rel) external view returns (uint256) {
   124	        (bool ok, string memory json) = _tryRead(rel);
   125	        require(ok, string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: artifact missing: ", rel));
   126	        try vm.parseJsonKeys(json, ".deployedBytecode.immutableReferences") returns (string[] memory k) {
   127	            return k.length;
   128	        } catch {
   129	            return 0;
   130	        }
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    /// @notice The exact [profile.default] build settings (foundry.toml). An artifact is accepted
   134	    ///         only if its OWN metadata carries every one of them — see `_isDefaultBuild`.
   135	    string internal constant DEFAULT_SOLC_PREFIX = "0.8.33+";
   136	    string internal constant DEFAULT_EVM = "cancun";
   137	
   138	    /// @notice Relative path of the profile.default artifact of contract `name` (Registry: its
   139	    ///         only, compilation_restrictions-limited runs=200 build). Reverts unless EXACTLY one
   140	    ///         distinct build matches (run a plain `forge build` first).
   141	    /// @dev Candidate paths are every layout forge uses for a source (plain, `.default` suffix,
   142	    ///      nested under 1–2 parent directories when names collide). File names prove nothing:
   143	    ///      the UNSUFFIXED `X.json` is overwritten by whichever compile ran last — e.g.
   144	    ///      `forge test --evm-version prague` leaves a Prague/500 `SuperPaymaster.json` next to a
   145	    ///      Cancun/500 `SuperPaymaster.default.json`, and a check on optimizer runs alone picked
   146	    ///      the Prague one (Codex stop-review, CRITICAL-1). Several candidates may be byte-identical
   147	    ///      copies of the same build (stale unsuffixed + suffixed); two DIFFERENT matching builds
   148	    ///      is ambiguous and fails. (The forge cache index would give the path directly, but
   149	    ///      handing its ~0.8 MB to a JSON cheatcode costs ~12M gas per lookup.)
   150	    function _defaultArtifactImpl(string memory name) internal view returns (string memory) {
   151	        string memory src = _sourceOf(name);
   152	        (string memory file, string memory d1, string memory d2) = _tail3(src);
   153	        string memory n1 = string.concat("/", name, ".json");
   154	        string memory n2 = string.concat("/", name, ".default.json");
   155	        string memory outDir = _artifactOutDir();
   156	        string[6] memory c = [
   157	            string.concat(outDir, "/", file, n1),
   158	            string.concat(outDir, "/", file, n2),
   159	            string.concat(outDir, "/", d1, "/", file, n1),
   160	            string.concat(outDir, "/", d1, "/", file, n2),
   161	            string.concat(outDir, "/", d2, "/", d1, "/", file, n1),
   162	            string.concat(outDir, "/", d2, "/", d1, "/", file, n2)
   163	        ];
   164	        (bool okSrc, string memory source) = _tryRead(src);
   165	        require(okSrc, string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: source file missing: ", src));
   166	        bytes32 srcHash = keccak256(bytes(source));
   167	        uint256 found;
   168	        string memory pick;
   169	        bytes32 pickCode;
   170	        for (uint256 i; i < c.length; ++i) {
   171	            (bool ok, string memory j) = _tryRead(c[i]);
   172	            if (!ok || !_isDefaultBuild(j, src, srcHash, _expectedRuns(name))) continue;
   173	            bytes32 code = keccak256(vm.parseJsonBytes(j, ".deployedBytecode.object"));
   174	            if (found == 0) {
   175	                pick = c[i];
   176	                pickCode = code;
   177	            } else {
   178	                require(code == pickCode, string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: ambiguous default builds of ", name, ": ", pick, " vs ", c[i]));
   179	            }
   180	            found++;
   181	        }
   182	        require(found != 0, string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: no profile.default build of ", name, " from ", src, " (run a plain `forge build`)"));
   183	        return pick;
   184	    }
   185	
   186	    /// @dev Honoured only by this reader's profile.default resolution. The registry-size
   187	    ///      diagnostic below and DeployAPNTsCapped still read `out/`, so a deploy shell must leave
   188	    ///      DEFAULT_ARTIFACTS_OUT unset (or `out`) and the rc.8 runbook asserts that.
   189	    function _artifactOutDir() internal view virtual returns (string memory) {
   190	        return _envArtifactOutDir("DEFAULT_ARTIFACTS_OUT");
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    /// @dev Variable name is a parameter so a test can drive the env path under its own name
   194	    ///      without touching the process-wide DEFAULT_ARTIFACTS_OUT that concurrent suites read.
   195	    function _envArtifactOutDir(string memory name) internal view returns (string memory) {
   196	        return _validateArtifactOutDir(vm.envOr(name, string("out")));
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    function _validateArtifactOutDir(string memory outDir) internal pure returns (string memory) {
   200	        bytes memory path = bytes(outDir);
   201	        require(path.length != 0, "DefaultArtifacts: output root is empty");
   202	        require(path[0] != "/", "DefaultArtifacts: output root must be relative");
   203	        require(path[path.length - 1] != "/", "DefaultArtifacts: output root has trailing slash");
   204	        uint256 start;
   205	        for (uint256 i; i <= path.length; ++i) {
   206	            // Only "/" is a separator here; a backslash or drive colon is a separator elsewhere.
   207	            require(i == path.length || (path[i] != "\\" && path[i] != ":"),
   208	                "DefaultArtifacts: output root has a backslash or drive separator");
   209	            if (i == path.length || path[i] == "/") {
   210	                require(i - start != 2 || path[start] != "." || path[start + 1] != ".",
   211	                    "DefaultArtifacts: output root contains parent segment");
   212	                start = i + 1;
   213	            }
   214	        }
   215	        return outDir;
   216	    }
   217	
   218	    /// @notice Artifact metadata == [profile.default]: compilation target, compiler version,
   219	    ///         optimizer enabled + runs, viaIR, evmVersion, and the source keccak256 == the source
   220	    ///         file as it is NOW (a stale artifact of an edited source fails).
   221	    function _isDefaultBuild(string memory j, string memory src, bytes32 srcHash, uint256 runs)
   222	        internal view returns (bool)
   223	    {
   224	        if (!vm.keyExistsJson(j, string.concat("$.metadata.settings.compilationTarget['", src, "']"))) return false;
   225	        if (_runsOf(j) != runs) return false;
   226	        try vm.parseJsonBool(j, ".metadata.settings.optimizer.enabled") returns (bool e) { if (!e) return false; } catch { return false; }
   227	        try vm.parseJsonBool(j, ".metadata.settings.viaIR") returns (bool v) { if (!v) return false; } catch { return false; }
   228	        try vm.parseJsonString(j, ".metadata.settings.evmVersion") returns (string memory ev) {
   229	            if (keccak256(bytes(ev)) != keccak256(bytes(DEFAULT_EVM))) return false;
   230	        } catch { return false; }
   231	        try vm.parseJsonString(j, ".metadata.compiler.version") returns (string memory cv) {
   232	            if (!_startsWith(cv, DEFAULT_SOLC_PREFIX)) return false;
   233	        } catch { return false; }
   234	        try vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat("$.metadata.sources['", src, "'].keccak256")) returns (bytes32 k) {
   235	            if (k != srcHash) return false;
   236	        } catch { return false; }
   237	        return _localImportsFresh(j);
   238	    }
   239	
   240	    /// @notice Every first-party source in the build (contracts/src/**, i.e. the target's IMPORTS
   241	    ///         too) must equal the file on disk. Checking only the compilation target let a stale
   242	    ///         artifact through when just an imported base changed — e.g. SuperPaymasterStorage.sol
   243	    ///         or Ownable2StepNamespaced.sol under an unchanged SuperPaymaster.sol (Codex D5b
   244	    ///         review, Medium). Dependencies under lib/ are pinned by the submodule commits.
   245	    function _localImportsFresh(string memory j) internal view returns (bool) {
   246	        string[] memory keys;
   247	        try vm.parseJsonKeys(j, "$.metadata.sources") returns (string[] memory k) {
   248	            keys = k;
   249	        } catch {
   250	            return false;
   251	        }
   252	        for (uint256 i; i < keys.length; ++i) {
   253	            if (!_startsWith(keys[i], "contracts/src/")) continue;
   254	            (bool ok, string memory body) = _tryRead(keys[i]);
   255	            if (!ok) return false;
   256	            try vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat("$.metadata.sources['", keys[i], "'].keccak256")) returns (bytes32 k) {
   257	                if (k != keccak256(bytes(body))) return false;
   258	            } catch {
   259	                return false;
   260	            }
   261	        }
   262	        return true;
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    function _startsWith(string memory s, string memory p) internal pure returns (bool) {
   266	        bytes memory a = bytes(s);
   267	        bytes memory b = bytes(p);
   268	        if (a.length < b.length) return false;
   269	        for (uint256 i; i < b.length; ++i) if (a[i] != b[i]) return false;
   270	        return true;
   271	    }
   272	
   273	    /// @dev "a/b/c/File.sol" -> ("File.sol", "c", "b").
   274	    function _tail3(string memory path) internal pure returns (string memory f, string memory d1, string memory d2) {
   275	        bytes memory b = bytes(path);
   276	        uint256 end = b.length;
   277	        string[3] memory parts;
   278	        for (uint256 k; k < 3; ++k) {
   279	            uint256 i = end;
   280	            while (i > 0 && b[i - 1] != "/") --i;
   281	            bytes memory seg = new bytes(end - i);
   282	            for (uint256 m; m < seg.length; ++m) seg[m] = b[i + m];
   283	            parts[k] = string(seg);
   284	            if (i == 0) break;
   285	            end = i - 1;
   286	        }
   287	        return (parts[0], parts[1], parts[2]);
   288	    }
   289	
   290	    function _codeEqArtifactImpl(address target, string memory rel) internal view returns (bool) {
   291	        (bool ok, string memory json) = _tryRead(rel);
   292	        if (!ok) return false;
   293	        bytes memory art = vm.parseJsonBytes(json, ".deployedBytecode.object");
   294	        bytes memory code = target.code;
   295	        if (art.length == 0 || art.length != code.length) return false;
   296	        // A contract without immutables has an empty/absent immutableReferences object, which
   297	        // parseJsonKeys rejects — that simply means "nothing to mask".
   298	        string[] memory keys;
   299	        try vm.parseJsonKeys(json, ".deployedBytecode.immutableReferences") returns (string[] memory k) {
   300	            keys = k;
   301	        } catch {
   302	            keys = new string[](0);
   303	        }
   304	        for (uint256 i; i < keys.length; ++i) {
   305	            ImmRef[] memory refs = abi.decode(
   306	                vm.parseJson(json, string.concat(".deployedBytecode.immutableReferences.", keys[i])), (ImmRef[])
   307	            );
   308	            for (uint256 j; j < refs.length; ++j) {
   309	                uint256 end = refs[j].start + refs[j].length;
   310	                require(end <= code.length, "DefaultArtifacts: immutable ref out of range");
   311	                for (uint256 k = refs[j].start; k < end; ++k) {
   312	                    code[k] = 0;
   313	                    art[k] = 0;
   314	                }
   315	            }
   316	        }
   317	        return keccak256(code) == keccak256(art);
   318	    }
   319	
   320	}
   321	
   322	abstract contract DefaultArtifacts is Script {
   323	    /// @dev Simulation-only helper (constructor-time, outside any broadcast); see T4ArtifactReader.
   324	    T4ArtifactReader internal _t4Reader = new T4ArtifactReader();
   325	
   326	    function _defaultArtifact(string memory name) internal view returns (string memory) {
   327	        return _t4Reader.t4DefaultArtifact(name);
   328	    }
   329	
   330	    /// @notice Deploy `name` from its profile.default artifact and require the deployed runtime
   331	    ///         to equal it. Inside a broadcast, vm.deployCode is broadcast as a CREATE.
   332	    function _deployDefault(string memory name, bytes memory args) internal returns (address a) {
   333	        a = vm.deployCode(_defaultArtifact(name), args);
   334	        _requireDefaultArtifact(a, name);
   335	    }
   336	
   337	    /// @notice Hard-fail unless `a`'s runtime equals the profile.default artifact of `name`.
   338	    function _requireDefaultArtifact(address a, string memory name) internal view {
   339	        require(
   340	            _codeEqArtifact(a, _defaultArtifact(name)),
   341	            string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: ", name, " runtime != profile.default artifact (T-4/AUD-4)")
   342	        );
   343	        console.log(string.concat("  [artifact] default artifact OK: ", name), a, a.code.length);
   344	        if (keccak256(bytes(name)) == keccak256("SuperPaymaster")) _requireSPExtensionBinding(a);
   345	    }
   346	
   347	    /// @notice D5b-design §2.2: the SuperPaymaster core's EXTENSION immutable is MASKED by the runtime
   348	    ///         comparison above, so a core bound to a foreign extension would pass it. Require the
   349	    ///         extension (created by the core's constructor) to be the profile.default
   350	    ///         SuperPaymasterAdmin build AND to carry the core's own immutables.
   351	    function _requireSPExtensionBinding(address core) internal view {
   352	        address ext = _addrCall(core, "EXTENSION()");
   353	        require(ext != address(0) && ext.code.length > 0, "DefaultArtifacts: SuperPaymaster.EXTENSION missing");
   354	        require(
   355	            _codeEqArtifact(ext, _defaultArtifact("SuperPaymasterAdmin")),
   356	            "DefaultArtifacts: SuperPaymaster.EXTENSION runtime != profile.default SuperPaymasterAdmin (T-4/AUD-4)"
   357	        );
   358	        string[3] memory imm = ["entryPoint()", "REGISTRY()", "ETH_USD_PRICE_FEED()"];
   359	        for (uint256 i; i < 3; ++i) {
   360	            address c = _addrCall(core, imm[i]);
   361	            require(c != address(0) && c == _addrCall(ext, imm[i]),
   362	                string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: extension/core immutable mismatch: ", imm[i]));
   363	        }
   364	        console.log("  [artifact] SuperPaymasterAdmin extension OK (default build; entryPoint/REGISTRY/feed == core)", ext);
   365	    }
   366	
   367	    function _addrCall(address target, string memory sig) internal view returns (address r) {
   368	        (bool ok, bytes memory ret) = target.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature(sig));
   369	        if (ok && ret.length >= 32) r = abi.decode(ret, (address));
   370	    }
   371	
   372	    /// @notice ERC-1967 proxy: the proxy runtime against ERC1967Proxy's default artifact, the
   373	    ///         implementation (read from the ERC-1967 slot) against `implName`'s.
   374	    function _requireDefaultProxy(address proxy, string memory implName) internal view returns (address impl) {
   375	        _requireDefaultArtifact(proxy, "ERC1967Proxy");
   376	        impl = address(uint160(uint256(vm.load(proxy, ERC1967_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT))));
   377	        _requireDefaultArtifact(impl, implName);
   378	    }
   379	
   380	    bytes32 internal constant ERC1967_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT =
   381	        0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc;
   382	
   383	    /// @notice 1 = default artifact, 2 = registry-size artifact, 0 = neither (diagnostic).
   384	    function _artifactMatch(address target, string memory name) internal view returns (uint8) {
   385	        if (_codeEqArtifact(target, _defaultArtifact(name))) return 1;
   386	        if (_codeEqArtifact(target, string.concat("out/", name, ".sol/", name, ".registry-size.json"))) return 2;
   387	        return 0;
   388	    }
   389	
   390	    /// @notice Runtime code of `target` == `deployedBytecode` of the artifact at `rel`, with every
   391	    ///         range listed in `immutableReferences` zeroed on both sides.
   392	    function _codeEqArtifact(address target, string memory rel) internal view returns (bool) {
   393	        return _t4Reader.t4CodeEqArtifact(target, rel);
   394	    }
   395	
   396	    /// @notice EIP-1167 clone (factory-made token / paymaster): the clone must be a canonical
   397	    ///         minimal proxy and its embedded implementation must be `implName`'s default build.
   398	    function _requireDefaultClone(address clone, string memory implName) internal view returns (address impl) {
   399	        impl = _eip1167Impl(clone);
   400	        require(impl != address(0), string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: not an EIP-1167 clone of ", implName));
   401	        _requireDefaultArtifact(impl, implName);
   402	        console.log(string.concat("  [artifact] EIP-1167 clone OK -> ", implName), clone);
   403	    }
   404	
   405	    /// @notice Implementation embedded in a canonical EIP-1167 minimal proxy (0 if not one).
   406	    function _eip1167Impl(address clone) internal view returns (address) {
   407	        bytes memory c = clone.code;
   408	        if (c.length != 45) return address(0);
   409	        bytes32 w0;
   410	        bytes32 w1;
   411	        assembly {
   412	            w0 := mload(add(c, 0x20))
   413	            w1 := mload(add(c, 0x40))
   414	        }
   415	        if (bytes10(w0) != bytes10(0x363d3d373d3d3d363d73)) return address(0);
   416	        if (bytes2(w0 << 240) != bytes2(0x5af4) || bytes13(w1) != bytes13(0x3d82803e903d91602b57fd5bf3)) {
   417	            return address(0);
   418	        }
   419	        return address(uint160(uint256(w0 >> 16)));
   420	    }
   421	}
     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     2	pragma solidity 0.8.33;
     3	
     4	import {Vm} from "forge-std/Vm.sol";
     5	import {console} from "forge-std/console.sol";
     6	
     7	interface IOwned {
     8	    function owner() external view returns (address);
     9	}
    10	
    11	/// @notice The two Safe methods this gate actually calls. Declared here rather than
    12	///         imported so the gate depends on an INTERFACE SHAPE, not on a Safe release.
    13	interface ISafeCompatibleMofN {
    14	    function getThreshold() external view returns (uint256);
    15	    function getOwners() external view returns (address[] memory);
    16	}
    17	
    18	/**
    19	 * @title GovernanceOwnerGate
    20	 * @notice CC-48 round-7 HIGH-1: the gate now enforces the property it names.
    21	 *
    22	 * @dev WHAT THIS GATE PROVES, STATED EXACTLY. It proves that the owner address
    23	 *      (a) holds code, (b) is NOT an EIP-7702 delegation designator, and (c) answers
    24	 *      `getThreshold()` and `getOwners()` with a Safe-COMPATIBLE M-of-N configuration —
    25	 *      `threshold >= 2`, `owners.length >= threshold`, owners distinct, non-zero and
    26	 *      exactly ABI-encoded. That is an INTERFACE-AND-THRESHOLD assertion.
    27	 *
    28	 *      IT DOES NOT PROVE THE OWNER IS A CANONICAL GNOSIS SAFE. A contract can implement
    29	 *      both methods and return whatever it likes while a single key still controls
    30	 *      execution. Proving canonicity needs something this gate deliberately does not
    31	 *      have: an audited runtime-codehash or proxy-factory allowlist per chain. Until that
    32	 *      exists, every message, log line and document in this repo must say
    33	 *      "Safe-compatible M-of-N", never "is a Safe". Round 6 got this backwards — it
    34	 *      checked `code.length > 0` and printed "owner is a contract (Safe/Timelock)", so an
    35	 *      EIP-7702 delegated EOA (code length 23, private key still signs everything) and a
    36	 *      1-of-1 forwarder both passed while the NatSpec told downstream consumers that a
    37	 *      multisig had been enforced.
    38	 *
    39	 * @dev WHY THE OWNER MATTERS AT ALL. 4.9.0 gave `owner` a new power:
    40	 *      `emergencyDisarmFraudProofVerifier()` clears the fraud-proof verifier in the SAME
    41	 *      block, with no delay and no public notice. A colluding or compromised owner can
    42	 *      front-run an honest watcher's `queueGuardianSlash` out of the mempool for the price
    43	 *      of one transaction, and repeat it indefinitely. That trade is accepted deliberately
    44	 *      — a compromised verifier can slash 100% of every accused guardian's lock inside one
    45	 *      block, so a four-day remedy was not a remedy — but the thing carrying the residual
    46	 *      risk is the owner's M-of-N configuration, and NOTHING ELSE:
    47	 *
    48	 *        • A TimelockController does NOT cover the disarm path. A timelocked emergency
    49	 *          stop is not an emergency stop; the two are semantically exclusive. For that one
    50	 *          path the M-of-N owner is the ONLY governance defence, not defence in depth.
    51	 *        • The in-contract `VERIFIER_ROTATION_DELAY` still governs RE-ARMING (there is no
    52	 *          counterpart that sets a non-zero verifier), so disarm is not a fast path to a
    53	 *          verifier of the owner's choosing.
    54	 *        • Already-queued cases are out of reach either way — execution reads the verdict
    55	 *          frozen at queue time and never reads `fraudProofVerifier`.
    56	 *
    57	 *      A `TimelockController` does NOT satisfy this gate (it has no `getThreshold`), and
    58	 *      that is intentional for `BLSAggregator`. `Registry` may legitimately be owned by a
    59	 *      Timelock instead; see `requireTimelockOwner`, which asserts the Timelock-shaped
    60	 *      property (`getMinDelay() > 0` and `owner == that Timelock`) rather than pretending
    61	 *      one shape fits both.
    62	 *
    63	 * @dev ESCAPE HATCHES, ALL EXPLICIT AND ALL NAMED IN THE LOG.
    64	 *        • `TESTNET_EOA_OWNER_ACK=true` — known testnets only, non-strict variant only.
    65	 *          The RepCredit evidence stack and the CC-89 E2E aggregator genuinely need a hot
    66	 *          owner to drive an experiment; pretending otherwise just gets the gate commented
    67	 *          out. There is NO acknowledgement that works on a production chain.
    68	 *        • `LOCAL_DEV_GOVERNANCE_ACK=true` — chainid 31337 only, AND only on a node that
    69	 *          passes a POSITIVE freshness judgement (`block.number < LOCAL_FRESH_BLOCK_CEILING`).
    70	 *          Round 6 made 31337 an unconditional no-op and `anvil --fork-url <mainnet>`
    71	 *          reports 31337, so a mainnet-fork rehearsal passed the gate silently. Round 7
    72	 *          made the skip require a typed ack — but `deploy-core anvil` then set that ack
    73	 *          for the operator unconditionally, so on a fork rehearsal driven through this
    74	 *          repo's own tooling NOBODY typed it and the skip was automatic again (round-8
    75	 *          LOW-1). Both halves are now real: the shell entry points only pre-set the ack
    76	 *          after PROBING the node (chain id 31337 and a low head block), and the gate
    77	 *          itself refuses a 31337 node whose head block is above the ceiling no matter
    78	 *          who set the ack.
    79	 *
    80	 * @dev GOVERNANCE_OWNER IS A BINDING DECLARATION, NOT A HINT (CC-48 round-8 LOW-2). Round 7
    81	 *      read `GOVERNANCE_OWNER` only to choose between two failure MESSAGES: unset passed,
    82	 *      and set-to-the-wrong-address passed as long as the actual owner happened to satisfy
    83	 *      the gate. It is now checked:
    84	 *        • declared and NOT equal to the live owner  -> REFUSED on every chain. This is the
    85	 *          "the transfer did not land" case, and it is the one that silently produced an
    86	 *          EOA-owned aggregator on a testnet under `TESTNET_EOA_OWNER_ACK`.
    87	 *        • an owner ACCEPTED as Safe-compatible M-of-N while `GOVERNANCE_OWNER` is unset
    88	 *          -> REFUSED. Deployment evidence has to be reconcilable against a declared
    89	 *          intent; "whatever address ended up owning it satisfied the interface" is not a
    90	 *          governance decision anyone made.
    91	 *      The EOA / 7702 acknowledgement paths still work with no declaration, because those
    92	 *      deployments are declaring the opposite — that there is no governance owner.
    93	 *
    94	 *      Scope, stated plainly: this gate covers the OWNER OF THE DISARM AUTHORITY —
    95	 *      `BLSAggregator` — plus the `Registry` owner that the 5.7.0 migration already gated.
    96	 *      It does NOT re-own the rest of the stack; handing GToken / SuperPaymaster / staking
    97	 *      ownership to governance is a separate change with its own blast radius.
    98	 */
    99	library GovernanceOwnerGate {
   100	    Vm private constant vm = Vm(address(uint160(uint256(keccak256("hevm cheat code")))));
   101	
   102	    /// @dev anvil. Still needs `LOCAL_DEV_GOVERNANCE_ACK`; see LOW-4 above.
   103	    uint256 internal constant LOCAL_CHAIN_ID = 31337;
   104	
   105	    /// @dev CC-48 round-8 LOW-1. The POSITIVE half of "this really is a local node".
   106	    ///      `anvil --fork-url <chain>` reports chain id 31337 but inherits the forked
   107	    ///      chain's HEAD BLOCK NUMBER, and every chain this repo targets is far past this
   108	    ///      height (Ethereum ~23M, Sepolia ~9M, OP ~1.4e8). A fresh `anvil` starts at 0 and
   109	    ///      a full `deploy-core anvil` run mines a few hundred blocks, so the gap is four
   110	    ///      orders of magnitude — this is a discriminator, not a coin flip.
   111	    ///
   112	    ///      STATED LIMITATION, because the last three rounds were lost to overstated
   113	    ///      absence proofs: this is a HEURISTIC, not a proof. Forking a chain whose head is
   114	    ///      below the ceiling would still pass, and nothing on-chain can prove a node is
   115	    ///      not a fork. What it does buy is that the realistic mistake — rehearsing against
   116	    ///      a fork of a LIVE chain — can no longer skip the gate, whoever set the ack.
   117	    uint256 internal constant LOCAL_FRESH_BLOCK_CEILING = 1_000_000;
   118	
   119	    /// @dev The env var carrying the operator's DECLARED governance owner. Named once so
   120	    ///      the failure text and the binding check can never drift apart.
   121	    string internal constant GOVERNANCE_OWNER_ENV = "GOVERNANCE_OWNER";
   122	
   123	    /// @dev EIP-7702 delegation designator prefix. A delegated EOA's code is exactly
   124	    ///      `0xef0100 ‖ address` (23 bytes) and its private key still signs everything, so
   125	    ///      it is an EOA for every purpose this gate cares about. Both Sepolia and Ethereum
   126	    ///      mainnet are past Pectra and mainstream "smart EOA" wallets delegate by default,
   127	    ///      so this is the common case, not the exotic one.
   128	    bytes3 internal constant EIP7702_PREFIX = 0xef0100;
   129	    uint256 internal constant EIP7702_CODE_LENGTH = 23;
   130	
   131	    /// @dev Upper bound on `getOwners()` length. A real Safe is far below this; the bound
   132	    ///      exists so a hostile owner cannot return a multi-megabyte array and turn the
   133	    ///      distinctness scan into a denial of service. Over the bound is REFUSED, not
   134	    ///      truncated.
   135	    uint256 internal constant MAX_GOVERNANCE_OWNERS = 64;
   136	
   137	    /// @notice How an owner address classifies before any interface call is made.
   138	    enum OwnerKind {
   139	        Eoa, //           no code
   140	        Delegated7702, // 0xef0100 ‖ address
   141	        Contract //       anything else
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    /// @notice The M-of-N configuration read off a Safe-compatible owner.
   145	    struct MofN {
   146	        uint256 threshold;
   147	        uint256 ownerCount;
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    // =====================================================================
   151	    // Classification
   152	    // =====================================================================
   153	
   154	    /// @notice Classify `addr` without trusting `code.length` alone.
   155	    function ownerKind(address addr) internal view returns (OwnerKind) {
   156	        bytes memory code = addr.code;
   157	        if (code.length == 0) return OwnerKind.Eoa;
   158	        if (code.length == EIP7702_CODE_LENGTH && bytes3(code) == EIP7702_PREFIX) {
   159	            return OwnerKind.Delegated7702;
   160	        }
   161	        return OwnerKind.Contract;
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    /// @notice Read a Safe-compatible M-of-N configuration, fail-closed on ANY deviation.
   165	    /// @dev    Every step is exact: `getThreshold()` must return EXACTLY 32 bytes;
   166	    ///         `getOwners()` must be a canonically-encoded `address[]` (head offset == 32,
   167	    ///         `length` matching the returndata to the byte, every element with a clean
   168	    ///         upper 96 bits). A revert, empty returndata, a wide proxy answer, a
   169	    ///         non-canonical offset and a dirty address word are all REFUSED — those are
   170	    ///         exactly the shapes a fake produces.
   171	    /// @return ok  the owner answered both methods with a decodable, self-consistent M-of-N
   172	    /// @return cfg the configuration; meaningless when `ok` is false
   173	    /// @return why a short reason, for the failure message
   174	    function readMofN(address ownerAddr)
   175	        internal
   176	        view
   177	        returns (bool ok, MofN memory cfg, string memory why)
   178	    {
   179	        (bool tOk, uint256 threshold) =
   180	            _staticUint(ownerAddr, abi.encodeCall(ISafeCompatibleMofN.getThreshold, ()));
   181	        if (!tOk) return (false, cfg, "getThreshold() did not answer with exactly one uint256");
   182	
   183	        (bool oOk, uint256 ownerCount) =
   184	            _staticAddressArray(ownerAddr, abi.encodeCall(ISafeCompatibleMofN.getOwners, ()));
   185	        if (!oOk) {
   186	            return (false, cfg, "getOwners() did not answer with a canonical, distinct address[]");
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        cfg = MofN({threshold: threshold, ownerCount: ownerCount});
   190	        if (threshold < 2) return (false, cfg, "threshold < 2 (a 1-of-N owner is one key)");
   191	        if (ownerCount < threshold) return (false, cfg, "owners.length < threshold (unsatisfiable)");
   192	        return (true, cfg, "");
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    // =====================================================================
   196	    // The gates
   197	    // =====================================================================
   198	
   199	    /// @notice Refuse to leave `subject` owned by anything that is not a Safe-compatible
   200	    ///         M-of-N owner, with a named testnet acknowledgement as the only exception.
   201	    /// @param subject   the contract whose ownership is being gated
   202	    /// @param ownerAddr the owner as it reads RIGHT NOW — pass `IOwned(x).owner()`, never
   203	    ///                  the address the script *intended* to set
   204	    /// @param label     contract name, so a failure names what is mis-owned
   205	    function requireGovernanceOwner(address subject, address ownerAddr, string memory label) internal view {
   206	        _gate(subject, ownerAddr, label, false, GOVERNANCE_OWNER_ENV);
   207	    }
   208	
   209	    /// @notice Convenience: read the owner off-chain and gate it in one call.
   210	    function requireGovernanceOwner(address subject, string memory label) internal view {
   211	        requireGovernanceOwner(subject, IOwned(subject).owner(), label);
   212	    }
   213	
   214	    /// @notice The same gate with NO testnet acknowledgement path.
   215	    /// @dev    Used by the 5.7.0 migration entry point. A governance batch that upgrades a
   216	    ///         live Registry and re-points its aggregator is a production operation even
   217	    ///         when it is rehearsed on Sepolia — a rehearsal whose ownership model differs
   218	    ///         from production rehearses the wrong thing. Experiment DEPLOY scripts use the
   219	    ///         acknowledged variant above; MIGRATION does not get one.
   220	    function requireGovernanceOwnerStrict(address subject, address ownerAddr, string memory label)
   221	        internal
   222	        view
   223	    {
   224	        _gate(subject, ownerAddr, label, true, GOVERNANCE_OWNER_ENV);
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    /// @notice The strict gate, bound to a NAMED declaration env var rather than the
   228	    ///         default `GOVERNANCE_OWNER`.
   229	    /// @dev    CC-48 round-8 LOW-2. The 5.7.0 migration gates TWO subjects with two
   230	    ///         independent owners — the live `Registry` and the freshly deployed
   231	    ///         `BLSAggregator` — and there is no reason they must be the same Safe. One
   232	    ///         global env var could only bind one of them, so the migration names each
   233	    ///         one's declaration separately and both are checked. `declEnv` appears
   234	    ///         verbatim in every failure message, so an operator is told which variable to
   235	    ///         set, not just that something is undeclared.
   236	    function requireGovernanceOwnerStrictDeclaredAs(
   237	        address subject,
   238	        address ownerAddr,
   239	        string memory label,
   240	        string memory declEnv
   241	    ) internal view {
   242	        _gate(subject, ownerAddr, label, true, declEnv);
   243	    }
   244	
   245	    /// @notice Assert that `ownerAddr` is the TimelockController at `timelock`, and that
   246	    ///         the Timelock actually imposes a delay.
   247	    /// @dev    CC-48 round-7, from the archived original checklist: `Registry` governance
   248	    ///         is a different question from `BLSAggregator` governance.
   249	    ///         `Registry.setCreditPolicy` is `immediate onlyOwner`, so if the answer to
   250	    ///         "who owns Registry" is "a Timelock", the deployment evidence has to show
   251	    ///         `getMinDelay() > 0` and `Registry.owner() == that Timelock` — printing
   252	    ///         `scheduleBatch` calldata proves neither. If the answer is "a Safe", use
   253	    ///         `requireGovernanceOwnerStrict` instead; both are accepted, guessing is not.
   254	    function requireTimelockOwner(address subject, address ownerAddr, address timelock, string memory label)
   255	        internal
   256	        view
   257	    {
   258	        require(
   259	            ownerAddr == timelock,
   260	            string.concat(
   261	                "CC-48 round-7: TIMELOCK is set but ",
   262	                label,
   263	                ".owner() is a different address. The batch below would be scheduled into a",
   264	                " Timelock that cannot execute it."
   265	            )
   266	        );
   267	        require(
   268	            ownerKind(timelock) == OwnerKind.Contract,
   269	            string.concat("CC-48 round-7: TIMELOCK ", label, " is not a contract (or is a 7702 delegation)")
   270	        );
   271	        (bool ok, uint256 minDelay) = _staticUint(timelock, abi.encodeWithSignature("getMinDelay()"));
   272	        require(
   273	            ok && minDelay > 0,
   274	            string.concat(
   275	                "CC-48 round-7: TIMELOCK must answer getMinDelay() with a NON-ZERO delay. A",
   276	                " zero-delay Timelock owning ",
   277	                label,
   278	                " makes setCreditPolicy immediate again, which is the property this batch exists",
   279	                " to remove."
   280	            )
   281	        );
   282	        console.log(string.concat("  [gov-gate] ", label, " owner is a Timelock:"), timelock);
   283	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   getMinDelay()      :", minDelay);
   284	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   criterion          : owner == TIMELOCK && getMinDelay() > 0");
   285	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   NOT proven         : that this Timelock's proposers are themselves M-of-N");
   286	        console.log("  [gov-gate] subject:", subject);
   287	    }
   288	
   289	    /// @notice The Safe to hand ownership to, or address(0) if the operator did not name
   290	    ///         one. Deploy scripts transfer ownership to it as their LAST owner-gated
   291	    ///         action, then call `requireGovernanceOwner` on the result.
   292	    /// @dev    Deliberately optional at READ time so local development needs no env var,
   293	    ///         and so a deploy script can tell "hand it to the Safe" from "leave it with
   294	    ///         the deployer". It is NOT optional at GATE time any more (CC-48 round-8
   295	    ///         LOW-2): `_gate` refuses a declared-but-mismatched owner on every path, and
   296	    ///         refuses to ACCEPT an M-of-N owner that was never declared at all.
   297	    function declaredGovernanceOwner() internal view returns (address) {
   298	        return vm.envOr(GOVERNANCE_OWNER_ENV, address(0));
   299	    }
   300	
   301	    // =====================================================================
   302	    // Internals
   303	    // =====================================================================
   304	
   305	    function _gate(
   306	        address subject,
   307	        address ownerAddr,
   308	        string memory label,
   309	        bool strict,
   310	        string memory declEnv
   311	    ) private view {
   312	        if (block.chainid == LOCAL_CHAIN_ID) {
   313	            // CC-48 round-7 LOW-4. `anvil --fork-url <mainnet>` also reports 31337, so an
   314	            // unconditional no-op here let a mainnet-fork rehearsal "pass" a gate that had
   315	            // never run. The distinction is made by a human typing the ack — and it is
   316	            // printed, so a transcript shows the gate was skipped rather than satisfied.
   317	            require(
   318	                vm.envOr("LOCAL_DEV_GOVERNANCE_ACK", false),
   319	                string.concat(
   320	                    "CC-48 round-7 LOW-4: chainid 31337 skips the governance gate for ",
   321	                    label,
   322	                    ", so it must be acknowledged with LOCAL_DEV_GOVERNANCE_ACK=true. Note that",
   323	                    " `anvil --fork-url <mainnet>` ALSO reports 31337: if this is a fork rehearsal",
   324	                    " of a production chain, the gate you are about to skip is the one you came to",
   325	                    " rehearse."
   326	                )
   327	            );
   328	            // CC-48 round-8 LOW-1. The ack alone is not enough, because the shell entry
   329	            // points can set it for the operator. This is the POSITIVE half: a fork of a
   330	            // live chain inherits that chain's head block, so it cannot pass here no
   331	            // matter who typed the ack. See LOCAL_FRESH_BLOCK_CEILING for what this
   332	            // heuristic does and does not buy.
   333	            require(
   334	                block.number < LOCAL_FRESH_BLOCK_CEILING,
   335	                string.concat(
   336	                    "CC-48 round-8 LOW-1: chainid is 31337 and LOCAL_DEV_GOVERNANCE_ACK is set,",
   337	                    " but this node's head block is far above anything a fresh anvil reaches --",
   338	                    " it is a FORK of a live chain. The local-development skip does not apply to a",
   339	                    " fork rehearsal: the gate you would be skipping for ",
   340	                    label,
   341	                    " is the one you came to rehearse. Run against a fresh anvil, or point the",
   342	                    " rehearsal at the real chain and satisfy the gate."
   343	                )
   344	            );
   345	            console.log(string.concat("  [gov-gate] SKIPPED on local chain 31337 for ", label), ownerAddr);
   346	            console.log("  [gov-gate] acknowledged via LOCAL_DEV_GOVERNANCE_ACK, and the node's head");
   347	            console.log("  [gov-gate] block is below the fresh-node ceiling. block.number:", block.number);
   348	            console.log("  [gov-gate] Nothing about the owner was verified. This is a HEURISTIC, not");
   349	            console.log("  [gov-gate] a proof that the node is not a fork.");
   350	            return;
   351	        }
   352	
   353	        // CC-48 round-8 LOW-2: the declaration is BINDING on every path below, including
   354	        // the acknowledged testnet ones. Read here, ENFORCED at the end of each path —
   355	        // deliberately not up front, so a wrong declaration never masks the more specific
   356	        // diagnosis ("this owner is an EIP-7702 delegated EOA", "threshold < 2"), which is
   357	        // the thing the operator actually has to fix first.
   358	        address declared = vm.envOr(declEnv, address(0));
   359	
   360	        OwnerKind kind = ownerKind(ownerAddr);
   361	
   362	        if (kind == OwnerKind.Contract) {
   363	            (bool ok, MofN memory cfg, string memory why) = readMofN(ownerAddr);
   364	            require(
   365	                ok,
   366	                string.concat(
   367	                    "CC-48 round-7 HIGH-1: ",
   368	                    label,
   369	                    " owner holds code but is NOT a Safe-compatible M-of-N owner -- ",
   370	                    why,
   371	                    ". Required: getThreshold() >= 2 and getOwners() a canonical address[] of",
   372	                    " distinct non-zero owners with length >= threshold. A 1-of-1 forwarder is one",
   373	                    " hot key wearing a contract's address, and this owner holds an immediate,",
   374	                    " zero-notice emergencyDisarmFraudProofVerifier(). Set ",
   375	                    declEnv,
   376	                    " to the Safe and re-run."
   377	                )
   378	            );
   379	            // CC-48 round-8 LOW-2, both halves, checked before the owner is ACCEPTED.
   380	            _requireDeclarationMatches(declared, ownerAddr, label, declEnv);
   381	            require(
   382	                declared != address(0),
   383	                string.concat(
   384	                    "CC-48 round-8 LOW-2: ",
   385	                    label,
   386	                    " owner satisfies the Safe-compatible M-of-N interface, but ",
   387	                    declEnv,
   388	                    " is NOT SET, so no governance owner was ever declared for this deployment.",
   389	                    " An owner that merely happens to answer getThreshold()/getOwners() is not a",
   390	                    " governance decision anyone made. Set ",
   391	                    declEnv,
   392	                    " to the address you intend to own this contract and re-run."
   393	                )
   394	            );
   395	            _logAccepted(subject, ownerAddr, label, cfg, declEnv);
   396	            return;
   397	        }
   398	
   399	        // EOA or 7702-delegated EOA from here down.
   400	        string memory kindPhrase = kind == OwnerKind.Delegated7702
   401	            ? " owner is an EIP-7702 DELEGATED EOA (code is 0xef0100 || address, 23 bytes). Its"
   402	                " private key still signs everything, so it is an EOA for governance purposes --"
   403	                " round 6's code.length > 0 check accepted exactly this and printed 'owner is a"
   404	                " contract'."
   405	            : " owner is an EOA.";
   406	
   407	        require(
   408	            _isKnownTestnet(block.chainid),
   409	            string.concat(
   410	                "CC-48 round-7 HIGH-1: ",
   411	                label,
   412	                kindPhrase,
   413	                " A production chain requires a Safe-compatible M-of-N owner: an EOA owner holds",
   414	                " an immediate, zero-notice emergencyDisarmFraudProofVerifier() -- i.e. the power",
   415	                " to censor every future guardian-slash accusation by front-running it.",
   416	                _declarationHint(declEnv)
   417	            )
   418	        );
   419	        require(
   420	            !strict,
   421	            string.concat(
   422	                "CC-48 round-7 HIGH-1: ",
   423	                label,
   424	                kindPhrase,
   425	                " The MIGRATION gate requires a Safe-compatible M-of-N owner and has no testnet",
   426	                " acknowledgement: a migration rehearsal whose ownership model differs from",
   427	                " production rehearses a different system.",
   428	                _declarationHint(declEnv)
   429	            )
   430	        );
   431	        require(
   432	            vm.envOr("TESTNET_EOA_OWNER_ACK", false),
   433	            string.concat(
   434	                "CC-48 round-7 HIGH-1: ",
   435	                label,
   436	                kindPhrase,
   437	                " That is allowed ONLY for experiment stacks and ONLY with",
   438	                " TESTNET_EOA_OWNER_ACK=true, which acknowledges that this deployment has no",
   439	                " governance defence against an immediate verifier disarm. Production chains",
   440	                " cannot set it.",
   441	                _declarationHint(declEnv)
   442	            )
   443	        );
   444	        // CC-48 round-8 LOW-2: an acknowledged hot-key deployment still may not CONTRADICT
   445	        // its own declaration. "GOVERNANCE_OWNER names the Safe, the transfer did not land,
   446	        // TESTNET_EOA_OWNER_ACK waves the EOA through" is exactly how a stack ends up
   447	        // EOA-owned while its transcript names a Safe. Checked last so the ack messages
   448	        // above still explain the primary problem.
   449	        _requireDeclarationMatches(declared, ownerAddr, label, declEnv);
   450	
   451	        console.log(string.concat("  [gov-gate] WARNING: ", label, " owner is a hot key on testnet:"), ownerAddr);
   452	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   kind               :", kind == OwnerKind.Delegated7702 ? "EIP-7702 delegated EOA" : "EOA");
   453	        console.log("  [gov-gate] acknowledged via TESTNET_EOA_OWNER_ACK. This deployment has NO");
   454	        console.log("  [gov-gate] governance defence against an immediate, zero-notice verifier");
   455	        console.log("  [gov-gate] disarm. Never use this configuration for anything but experiments.");
   456	        console.log("  [gov-gate] subject:", subject);
   457	    }
   458	
   459	    /// @dev Deployment evidence (round-7, required by the reviewer): the transcript must
   460	    ///      carry the owner, the threshold, the owner count AND the criterion that was
   461	    ///      applied — including what the criterion does NOT prove, so a downstream reader
   462	    ///      cannot upgrade "Safe-compatible" into "canonical Safe" on their own.
   463	    function _logAccepted(
   464	        address subject,
   465	        address ownerAddr,
   466	        string memory label,
   467	        MofN memory cfg,
   468	        string memory declEnv
   469	    ) private view {
   470	        console.log(string.concat("  [gov-gate] ", label, " owner is Safe-compatible M-of-N:"), ownerAddr);
   471	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   getThreshold()     :", cfg.threshold);
   472	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   getOwners().length :", cfg.ownerCount);
   473	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   criterion          : code, not 0xef0100, threshold>=2, owners>=threshold, distinct");
   474	        // CC-48 round-8 LOW-2: the transcript records that the owner was DECLARED, and
   475	        // under which variable, so the evidence can be reconciled against intent rather
   476	        // than merely against the interface.
   477	        console.log(string.concat("  [gov-gate]   declared via       : ", declEnv, " (checked == owner)"));
   478	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   NOT proven         : that this is a canonical Gnosis Safe (no");
   479	        console.log("  [gov-gate]                        runtime-codehash / factory allowlist exists yet)");
   480	        _logOwners(ownerAddr, cfg.ownerCount);
   481	        console.log("  [gov-gate] subject:", subject);
   482	    }
   483	
   484	    function _logOwners(address ownerAddr, uint256 ownerCount) private view {
   485	        (bool ok, bytes memory ret) = ownerAddr.staticcall(abi.encodeCall(ISafeCompatibleMofN.getOwners, ()));
   486	        if (!ok) return; // unreachable: readMofN already decoded it
   487	        address[] memory owners = abi.decode(ret, (address[]));
   488	        for (uint256 i = 0; i < ownerCount && i < owners.length; ++i) {
   489	            console.log("  [gov-gate]   owner:", owners[i]);
   490	        }
   491	    }
   492	
   493	    /// @dev CC-48 round-8 LOW-2. A declared governance owner that is NOT the live owner is
   494	    ///      a stop condition on every path: either the ownership transfer did not land or
   495	    ///      the declaration is wrong, and in both cases the deployment evidence would name
   496	    ///      an address that does not hold the keys. Round 7 read `GOVERNANCE_OWNER` only to
   497	    ///      choose between two failure MESSAGES, so this case passed whenever the live
   498	    ///      owner happened to satisfy the gate on its own.
   499	    function _requireDeclarationMatches(
   500	        address declared,
   501	        address ownerAddr,
   502	        string memory label,
   503	        string memory declEnv
   504	    ) private pure {
   505	        require(
   506	            declared == address(0) || declared == ownerAddr,
   507	            string.concat(
   508	                "CC-48 round-8 LOW-2: ",
   509	                declEnv,
   510	                " was declared, but ",
   511	                label,
   512	                ".owner() is a DIFFERENT address. Either the ownership transfer did not land or",
   513	                " the declaration is wrong; both are stop conditions, because the deployment",
   514	                " evidence would name an owner that does not hold the keys."
   515	            )
   516	        );
   517	    }
   518	
   519	    /// @dev "not declared" and "declared and wrong" used to be indistinguishable — both
   520	    ///      simply landed on the gate. They are different operator mistakes with different
   521	    ///      fixes, so they get different text.
   522	    /// @dev CC-48 round-8 LOW-2 keeps this as the TEXT half only, and makes it accurate in
   523	    ///      all three states. The BINDING half is `_requireDeclarationMatches`, which runs
   524	    ///      at the end of each path so that the owner-shape diagnosis above is never masked
   525	    ///      by a declaration problem the operator would have to fix second.
   526	    function _declarationHint(string memory declEnv) private view returns (string memory) {
   527	        address declared = vm.envOr(declEnv, address(0));
   528	        if (declared == address(0)) {
   529	            return string.concat(
   530	                " ",
   531	                declEnv,
   532	                " is NOT SET: this deployment never named a governance owner at all. Set it to"
   533	                " the Safe before deploying, not after."
   534	            );
   535	        }
   536	        return string.concat(
   537	            " ",
   538	            declEnv,
   539	            " IS set -- so either the ownership transfer did not land, or it landed on an"
   540	            " address that is not the one declared. Compare the two before re-running; a"
   541	            " mismatch is refused outright once the owner-shape problem above is fixed."
   542	        );
   543	    }
   544	
   545	    /// @dev staticcall returning EXACTLY one uint256 word. Anything else is a refusal, not
   546	    ///      a value: a revert, empty returndata (the shape a call to an EOA produces) and a
   547	    ///      wide proxy answer are all "this address did not answer the question asked".
   548	    function _staticUint(address target, bytes memory callData)
   549	        private
   550	        view
   551	        returns (bool ok, uint256 value)
   552	    {
   553	        (bool s, bytes memory ret) = target.staticcall(callData);
   554	        if (!s || ret.length != 32) return (false, 0);
   555	        return (true, abi.decode(ret, (uint256)));
   556	    }
   557	
   558	    /// @dev staticcall returning a CANONICALLY encoded, distinct, non-zero `address[]`.
   559	    ///      Hand-decoded rather than `abi.decode`d because the failure modes are the point:
   560	    ///      a non-canonical head offset, a length that does not match the returndata, a
   561	    ///      dirty upper-96-bits word and a duplicate owner all have to be REFUSALS, and
   562	    ///      `abi.decode` accepts or reverts unhelpfully on several of them.
   563	    function _staticAddressArray(address target, bytes memory callData)
   564	        private
   565	        view
   566	        returns (bool ok, uint256 count)
   567	    {
   568	        (bool s, bytes memory ret) = target.staticcall(callData);
   569	        if (!s || ret.length < 64) return (false, 0);
   570	
   571	        uint256 offset;
   572	        uint256 n;
   573	        assembly {
   574	            offset := mload(add(ret, 32))
   575	            n := mload(add(ret, 64))
   576	        }
   577	        if (offset != 32) return (false, 0); // non-canonical head
   578	        if (n == 0 || n > MAX_GOVERNANCE_OWNERS) return (false, 0);
   579	        if (ret.length != 64 + 32 * n) return (false, 0); // exact width, no padding games
   580	
   581	        address[] memory owners = new address[](n);
   582	        for (uint256 i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   583	            uint256 word;
   584	            assembly {
   585	                word := mload(add(ret, add(96, mul(32, i))))
   586	            }
   587	            if (word >> 160 != 0) return (false, 0); // dirty address word
   588	            if (word == 0) return (false, 0); // address(0) is not an owner
   589	            address o = address(uint160(word));
   590	            for (uint256 j = 0; j < i; ++j) {
   591	                if (owners[j] == o) return (false, 0); // duplicate inflates N for free
   592	            }
   593	            owners[i] = o;
   594	        }
   595	        return (true, n);
   596	    }
   597	
   598	    /// @dev The same testnet list `DeployLive` uses for its ERC-8004 constants:
   599	    ///      Sepolia, OP Sepolia, Base Sepolia, Arbitrum Sepolia, Polygon Amoy.
   600	    function _isKnownTestnet(uint256 chainId) private pure returns (bool) {
   601	        return chainId == 11155111 || chainId == 11155420 || chainId == 84532 || chainId == 421614
   602	            || chainId == 80002;
   603	    }
   604	}

   218	    /// @notice Artifact metadata == [profile.default]: compilation target, compiler version,
   219	    ///         optimizer enabled + runs, viaIR, evmVersion, and the source keccak256 == the source
   220	    ///         file as it is NOW (a stale artifact of an edited source fails).
   221	    function _isDefaultBuild(string memory j, string memory src, bytes32 srcHash, uint256 runs)
   222	        internal view returns (bool)
   223	    {
   224	        if (!vm.keyExistsJson(j, string.concat("$.metadata.settings.compilationTarget['", src, "']"))) return false;
   225	        if (_runsOf(j) != runs) return false;
   226	        try vm.parseJsonBool(j, ".metadata.settings.optimizer.enabled") returns (bool e) { if (!e) return false; } catch { return false; }
   227	        try vm.parseJsonBool(j, ".metadata.settings.viaIR") returns (bool v) { if (!v) return false; } catch { return false; }
   228	        try vm.parseJsonString(j, ".metadata.settings.evmVersion") returns (string memory ev) {
   229	            if (keccak256(bytes(ev)) != keccak256(bytes(DEFAULT_EVM))) return false;
   230	        } catch { return false; }

   232	            if (!_startsWith(cv, DEFAULT_SOLC_PREFIX)) return false;
   233	        } catch { return false; }

   235	            if (k != srcHash) return false;
   236	        } catch { return false; }
   237	        return _localImportsFresh(j);
   238	    }

   240	    /// @notice Every first-party source in the build (contracts/src/**, i.e. the target's IMPORTS
   241	    ///         too) must equal the file on disk. Checking only the compilation target let a stale
   242	    ///         artifact through when just an imported base changed — e.g. SuperPaymasterStorage.sol
   243	    ///         or Ownable2StepNamespaced.sol under an unchanged SuperPaymaster.sol (Codex D5b
   244	    ///         review, Medium). Dependencies under lib/ are pinned by the submodule commits.
   245	    function _localImportsFresh(string memory j) internal view returns (bool) {
   246	        string[] memory keys;
   247	        try vm.parseJsonKeys(j, "$.metadata.sources") returns (string[] memory k) {
   248	            keys = k;
   249	        } catch {
   250	            return false;
   251	        }
   252	        for (uint256 i; i < keys.length; ++i) {
   253	            if (!_startsWith(keys[i], "contracts/src/")) continue;
   254	            (bool ok, string memory body) = _tryRead(keys[i]);
   255	            if (!ok) return false;
   256	            try vm.parseJsonBytes32(j, string.concat("$.metadata.sources['", keys[i], "'].keccak256")) returns (bytes32 k) {
   257	                if (k != keccak256(bytes(body))) return false;
   258	            } catch {
   259	                return false;
   260	            }
   261	        }
   262	        return true;
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    function _startsWith(string memory s, string memory p) internal pure returns (bool) {
   266	        bytes memory a = bytes(s);
   267	        bytes memory b = bytes(p);
   268	        if (a.length < b.length) return false;
   269	        for (uint256 i; i < b.length; ++i) if (a[i] != b[i]) return false;
   270	        return true;
   271	    }

   273	    /// @dev "a/b/c/File.sol" -> ("File.sol", "c", "b").
   274	    function _tail3(string memory path) internal pure returns (string memory f, string memory d1, string memory d2) {
   275	        bytes memory b = bytes(path);
   276	        uint256 end = b.length;
   277	        string[3] memory parts;
   278	        for (uint256 k; k < 3; ++k) {
   279	            uint256 i = end;
   280	            while (i > 0 && b[i - 1] != "/") --i;
   281	            bytes memory seg = new bytes(end - i);
   282	            for (uint256 m; m < seg.length; ++m) seg[m] = b[i + m];
   283	            parts[k] = string(seg);
   284	            if (i == 0) break;
   285	            end = i - 1;
   286	        }
   287	        return (parts[0], parts[1], parts[2]);
   288	    }
   289	
   290	    function _codeEqArtifactImpl(address target, string memory rel) internal view returns (bool) {
   291	        (bool ok, string memory json) = _tryRead(rel);
   292	        if (!ok) return false;
   293	        bytes memory art = vm.parseJsonBytes(json, ".deployedBytecode.object");
   294	        bytes memory code = target.code;
   295	        if (art.length == 0 || art.length != code.length) return false;
   296	        // A contract without immutables has an empty/absent immutableReferences object, which
   297	        // parseJsonKeys rejects — that simply means "nothing to mask".
   298	        string[] memory keys;
   299	        try vm.parseJsonKeys(json, ".deployedBytecode.immutableReferences") returns (string[] memory k) {
   300	            keys = k;
   301	        } catch {
   302	            keys = new string[](0);
   303	        }
   304	        for (uint256 i; i < keys.length; ++i) {
   305	            ImmRef[] memory refs = abi.decode(
   306	                vm.parseJson(json, string.concat(".deployedBytecode.immutableReferences.", keys[i])), (ImmRef[])
   307	            );
   308	            for (uint256 j; j < refs.length; ++j) {
   309	                uint256 end = refs[j].start + refs[j].length;
   310	                require(end <= code.length, "DefaultArtifacts: immutable ref out of range");
   311	                for (uint256 k = refs[j].start; k < end; ++k) {
   312	                    code[k] = 0;
   313	                    art[k] = 0;
   314	                }
   315	            }
   316	        }
   317	        return keccak256(code) == keccak256(art);
   318	    }

   322	abstract contract DefaultArtifacts is Script {
   323	    /// @dev Simulation-only helper (constructor-time, outside any broadcast); see T4ArtifactReader.
   324	    T4ArtifactReader internal _t4Reader = new T4ArtifactReader();
   325	
   326	    function _defaultArtifact(string memory name) internal view returns (string memory) {
   327	        return _t4Reader.t4DefaultArtifact(name);
   328	    }
   329	
   330	    /// @notice Deploy `name` from its profile.default artifact and require the deployed runtime
   331	    ///         to equal it. Inside a broadcast, vm.deployCode is broadcast as a CREATE.
   332	    function _deployDefault(string memory name, bytes memory args) internal returns (address a) {
   333	        a = vm.deployCode(_defaultArtifact(name), args);
   334	        _requireDefaultArtifact(a, name);
   335	    }
   336	
   337	    /// @notice Hard-fail unless `a`'s runtime equals the profile.default artifact of `name`.
   338	    function _requireDefaultArtifact(address a, string memory name) internal view {
   339	        require(
   340	            _codeEqArtifact(a, _defaultArtifact(name)),
   341	            string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: ", name, " runtime != profile.default artifact (T-4/AUD-4)")
   342	        );
   343	        console.log(string.concat("  [artifact] default artifact OK: ", name), a, a.code.length);
   344	        if (keccak256(bytes(name)) == keccak256("SuperPaymaster")) _requireSPExtensionBinding(a);
   345	    }
   346	
   347	    /// @notice D5b-design §2.2: the SuperPaymaster core's EXTENSION immutable is MASKED by the runtime
   348	    ///         comparison above, so a core bound to a foreign extension would pass it. Require the
   349	    ///         extension (created by the core's constructor) to be the profile.default
   350	    ///         SuperPaymasterAdmin build AND to carry the core's own immutables.
   351	    function _requireSPExtensionBinding(address core) internal view {
   352	        address ext = _addrCall(core, "EXTENSION()");
   353	        require(ext != address(0) && ext.code.length > 0, "DefaultArtifacts: SuperPaymaster.EXTENSION missing");
   354	        require(
   355	            _codeEqArtifact(ext, _defaultArtifact("SuperPaymasterAdmin")),
   356	            "DefaultArtifacts: SuperPaymaster.EXTENSION runtime != profile.default SuperPaymasterAdmin (T-4/AUD-4)"
   357	        );
   358	        string[3] memory imm = ["entryPoint()", "REGISTRY()", "ETH_USD_PRICE_FEED()"];
   359	        for (uint256 i; i < 3; ++i) {
   360	            address c = _addrCall(core, imm[i]);
   361	            require(c != address(0) && c == _addrCall(ext, imm[i]),
   362	                string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: extension/core immutable mismatch: ", imm[i]));
   363	        }
   364	        console.log("  [artifact] SuperPaymasterAdmin extension OK (default build; entryPoint/REGISTRY/feed == core)", ext);
   365	    }
   366	
   367	    function _addrCall(address target, string memory sig) internal view returns (address r) {
   368	        (bool ok, bytes memory ret) = target.staticcall(abi.encodeWithSignature(sig));
   369	        if (ok && ret.length >= 32) r = abi.decode(ret, (address));
   370	    }
   371	
   372	    /// @notice ERC-1967 proxy: the proxy runtime against ERC1967Proxy's default artifact, the
   373	    ///         implementation (read from the ERC-1967 slot) against `implName`'s.
   374	    function _requireDefaultProxy(address proxy, string memory implName) internal view returns (address impl) {
   375	        _requireDefaultArtifact(proxy, "ERC1967Proxy");
   376	        impl = address(uint160(uint256(vm.load(proxy, ERC1967_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT))));
   377	        _requireDefaultArtifact(impl, implName);
   378	    }
   379	
   380	    bytes32 internal constant ERC1967_IMPLEMENTATION_SLOT =
   381	        0x360894a13ba1a3210667c828492db98dca3e2076cc3735a920a3ca505d382bbc;
   382	
   383	    /// @notice 1 = default artifact, 2 = registry-size artifact, 0 = neither (diagnostic).
   384	    function _artifactMatch(address target, string memory name) internal view returns (uint8) {
   385	        if (_codeEqArtifact(target, _defaultArtifact(name))) return 1;
   386	        if (_codeEqArtifact(target, string.concat("out/", name, ".sol/", name, ".registry-size.json"))) return 2;
   387	        return 0;
   388	    }
   389	
   390	    /// @notice Runtime code of `target` == `deployedBytecode` of the artifact at `rel`, with every
   391	    ///         range listed in `immutableReferences` zeroed on both sides.
   392	    function _codeEqArtifact(address target, string memory rel) internal view returns (bool) {
   393	        return _t4Reader.t4CodeEqArtifact(target, rel);
   394	    }
   395	
   396	    /// @notice EIP-1167 clone (factory-made token / paymaster): the clone must be a canonical
   397	    ///         minimal proxy and its embedded implementation must be `implName`'s default build.
   398	    function _requireDefaultClone(address clone, string memory implName) internal view returns (address impl) {
   399	        impl = _eip1167Impl(clone);
   400	        require(impl != address(0), string.concat("DefaultArtifacts: not an EIP-1167 clone of ", implName));
   401	        _requireDefaultArtifact(impl, implName);
   402	        console.log(string.concat("  [artifact] EIP-1167 clone OK -> ", implName), clone);
   403	    }
   404	
   405	    /// @notice Implementation embedded in a canonical EIP-1167 minimal proxy (0 if not one).
   406	    function _eip1167Impl(address clone) internal view returns (address) {
   407	        bytes memory c = clone.code;
   408	        if (c.length != 45) return address(0);
   409	        bytes32 w0;
   410	        bytes32 w1;
   411	        assembly {
   412	            w0 := mload(add(c, 0x20))
   413	            w1 := mload(add(c, 0x40))
   414	        }
   415	        if (bytes10(w0) != bytes10(0x363d3d373d3d3d363d73)) return address(0);
   416	        if (bytes2(w0 << 240) != bytes2(0x5af4) || bytes13(w1) != bytes13(0x3d82803e903d91602b57fd5bf3)) {
   417	            return address(0);
   418	        }
   419	        return address(uint160(uint256(w0 >> 16)));
   420	    }
   421	}
     1	// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
     2	pragma solidity 0.8.33;
