     4	import {Vm} from "forge-std/Vm.sol";
     5	import {console} from "forge-std/console.sol";
     6	
     7	interface IOwned {
     8	    function owner() external view returns (address);
     9	}
    10	
    11	/// @notice The two Safe methods this gate actually calls. Declared here rather than
    12	///         imported so the gate depends on an INTERFACE SHAPE, not on a Safe release.
    13	interface ISafeCompatibleMofN {
    14	    function getThreshold() external view returns (uint256);
    15	    function getOwners() external view returns (address[] memory);
    16	}
    17	
    18	/**
    19	 * @title GovernanceOwnerGate
    20	 * @notice CC-48 round-7 HIGH-1: the gate now enforces the property it names.
    21	 *
    22	 * @dev WHAT THIS GATE PROVES, STATED EXACTLY. It proves that the owner address
    23	 *      (a) holds code, (b) is NOT an EIP-7702 delegation designator, and (c) answers
    24	 *      `getThreshold()` and `getOwners()` with a Safe-COMPATIBLE M-of-N configuration —
    25	 *      `threshold >= 2`, `owners.length >= threshold`, owners distinct, non-zero and
    26	 *      exactly ABI-encoded. That is an INTERFACE-AND-THRESHOLD assertion.
    27	 *
    28	 *      IT DOES NOT PROVE THE OWNER IS A CANONICAL GNOSIS SAFE. A contract can implement
    29	 *      both methods and return whatever it likes while a single key still controls
    30	 *      execution. Proving canonicity needs something this gate deliberately does not
    31	 *      have: an audited runtime-codehash or proxy-factory allowlist per chain. Until that
    32	 *      exists, every message, log line and document in this repo must say
    33	 *      "Safe-compatible M-of-N", never "is a Safe". Round 6 got this backwards — it
    34	 *      checked `code.length > 0` and printed "owner is a contract (Safe/Timelock)", so an
    35	 *      EIP-7702 delegated EOA (code length 23, private key still signs everything) and a
    36	 *      1-of-1 forwarder both passed while the NatSpec told downstream consumers that a
    37	 *      multisig had been enforced.
    38	 *
    39	 * @dev WHY THE OWNER MATTERS AT ALL. 4.9.0 gave `owner` a new power:
    40	 *      `emergencyDisarmFraudProofVerifier()` clears the fraud-proof verifier in the SAME
    41	 *      block, with no delay and no public notice. A colluding or compromised owner can
    42	 *      front-run an honest watcher's `queueGuardianSlash` out of the mempool for the price
    43	 *      of one transaction, and repeat it indefinitely. That trade is accepted deliberately
    44	 *      — a compromised verifier can slash 100% of every accused guardian's lock inside one
    45	 *      block, so a four-day remedy was not a remedy — but the thing carrying the residual
    46	 *      risk is the owner's M-of-N configuration, and NOTHING ELSE:
    47	 *
    48	 *        • A TimelockController does NOT cover the disarm path. A timelocked emergency
    49	 *          stop is not an emergency stop; the two are semantically exclusive. For that one
    50	 *          path the M-of-N owner is the ONLY governance defence, not defence in depth.
    51	 *        • The in-contract `VERIFIER_ROTATION_DELAY` still governs RE-ARMING (there is no
    52	 *          counterpart that sets a non-zero verifier), so disarm is not a fast path to a
    53	 *          verifier of the owner's choosing.
    54	 *        • Already-queued cases are out of reach either way — execution reads the verdict
    55	 *          frozen at queue time and never reads `fraudProofVerifier`.
    56	 *
    57	 *      A `TimelockController` does NOT satisfy this gate (it has no `getThreshold`), and
    58	 *      that is intentional for `BLSAggregator`. `Registry` may legitimately be owned by a
    59	 *      Timelock instead; see `requireTimelockOwner`, which asserts the Timelock-shaped
    60	 *      property (`getMinDelay() > 0` and `owner == that Timelock`) rather than pretending
    61	 *      one shape fits both.
    62	 *
    63	 * @dev ESCAPE HATCHES, ALL EXPLICIT AND ALL NAMED IN THE LOG.
    64	 *        • `TESTNET_EOA_OWNER_ACK=true` — known testnets only, non-strict variant only.
    65	 *          The RepCredit evidence stack and the CC-89 E2E aggregator genuinely need a hot
    66	 *          owner to drive an experiment; pretending otherwise just gets the gate commented
    67	 *          out. There is NO acknowledgement that works on a production chain.
    68	 *        • `LOCAL_DEV_GOVERNANCE_ACK=true` — chainid 31337 only, AND only on a node that
    69	 *          passes a POSITIVE freshness judgement (`block.number < LOCAL_FRESH_BLOCK_CEILING`).
    70	 *          Round 6 made 31337 an unconditional no-op and `anvil --fork-url <mainnet>`
    71	 *          reports 31337, so a mainnet-fork rehearsal passed the gate silently. Round 7
    72	 *          made the skip require a typed ack — but `deploy-core anvil` then set that ack
    73	 *          for the operator unconditionally, so on a fork rehearsal driven through this
    74	 *          repo's own tooling NOBODY typed it and the skip was automatic again (round-8
    75	 *          LOW-1). Both halves are now real: the shell entry points only pre-set the ack
    76	 *          after PROBING the node (chain id 31337 and a low head block), and the gate
    77	 *          itself refuses a 31337 node whose head block is above the ceiling no matter
    78	 *          who set the ack.
    79	 *
    80	 * @dev GOVERNANCE_OWNER IS A BINDING DECLARATION, NOT A HINT (CC-48 round-8 LOW-2). Round 7
    81	 *      read `GOVERNANCE_OWNER` only to choose between two failure MESSAGES: unset passed,
    82	 *      and set-to-the-wrong-address passed as long as the actual owner happened to satisfy
    83	 *      the gate. It is now checked:
    84	 *        • declared and NOT equal to the live owner  -> REFUSED on every chain. This is the
    85	 *          "the transfer did not land" case, and it is the one that silently produced an
    86	 *          EOA-owned aggregator on a testnet under `TESTNET_EOA_OWNER_ACK`.
    87	 *        • an owner ACCEPTED as Safe-compatible M-of-N while `GOVERNANCE_OWNER` is unset
    88	 *          -> REFUSED. Deployment evidence has to be reconcilable against a declared
    89	 *          intent; "whatever address ended up owning it satisfied the interface" is not a
    90	 *          governance decision anyone made.
    91	 *      The EOA / 7702 acknowledgement paths still work with no declaration, because those
    92	 *      deployments are declaring the opposite — that there is no governance owner.
    93	 *
    94	 *      Scope, stated plainly: this gate covers the OWNER OF THE DISARM AUTHORITY —
    95	 *      `BLSAggregator` — plus the `Registry` owner that the 5.7.0 migration already gated.
    96	 *      It does NOT re-own the rest of the stack; handing GToken / SuperPaymaster / staking
    97	 *      ownership to governance is a separate change with its own blast radius.
    98	 */
    99	library GovernanceOwnerGate {
   100	    Vm private constant vm = Vm(address(uint160(uint256(keccak256("hevm cheat code")))));
   101	
   102	    /// @dev anvil. Still needs `LOCAL_DEV_GOVERNANCE_ACK`; see LOW-4 above.
   103	    uint256 internal constant LOCAL_CHAIN_ID = 31337;
   104	
   105	    /// @dev CC-48 round-8 LOW-1. The POSITIVE half of "this really is a local node".
   106	    ///      `anvil --fork-url <chain>` reports chain id 31337 but inherits the forked
   107	    ///      chain's HEAD BLOCK NUMBER, and every chain this repo targets is far past this
   108	    ///      height (Ethereum ~23M, Sepolia ~9M, OP ~1.4e8). A fresh `anvil` starts at 0 and
   109	    ///      a full `deploy-core anvil` run mines a few hundred blocks, so the gap is four
   110	    ///      orders of magnitude — this is a discriminator, not a coin flip.
   111	    ///
   112	    ///      STATED LIMITATION, because the last three rounds were lost to overstated
   113	    ///      absence proofs: this is a HEURISTIC, not a proof. Forking a chain whose head is
   114	    ///      below the ceiling would still pass, and nothing on-chain can prove a node is
   115	    ///      not a fork. What it does buy is that the realistic mistake — rehearsing against
   116	    ///      a fork of a LIVE chain — can no longer skip the gate, whoever set the ack.
   117	    uint256 internal constant LOCAL_FRESH_BLOCK_CEILING = 1_000_000;
   118	
   119	    /// @dev The env var carrying the operator's DECLARED governance owner. Named once so
   120	    ///      the failure text and the binding check can never drift apart.
   121	    string internal constant GOVERNANCE_OWNER_ENV = "GOVERNANCE_OWNER";
   122	
   123	    /// @dev EIP-7702 delegation designator prefix. A delegated EOA's code is exactly
   124	    ///      `0xef0100 ‖ address` (23 bytes) and its private key still signs everything, so
   125	    ///      it is an EOA for every purpose this gate cares about. Both Sepolia and Ethereum
   126	    ///      mainnet are past Pectra and mainstream "smart EOA" wallets delegate by default,
   127	    ///      so this is the common case, not the exotic one.
   128	    bytes3 internal constant EIP7702_PREFIX = 0xef0100;
   129	    uint256 internal constant EIP7702_CODE_LENGTH = 23;
   130	
   131	    /// @dev Upper bound on `getOwners()` length. A real Safe is far below this; the bound
   132	    ///      exists so a hostile owner cannot return a multi-megabyte array and turn the
   133	    ///      distinctness scan into a denial of service. Over the bound is REFUSED, not
   134	    ///      truncated.
   135	    uint256 internal constant MAX_GOVERNANCE_OWNERS = 64;
   136	
   137	    /// @notice How an owner address classifies before any interface call is made.
   138	    enum OwnerKind {
   139	        Eoa, //           no code
   140	        Delegated7702, // 0xef0100 ‖ address
   141	        Contract //       anything else
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    /// @notice The M-of-N configuration read off a Safe-compatible owner.
   145	    struct MofN {
   146	        uint256 threshold;
   147	        uint256 ownerCount;
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    // =====================================================================
   151	    // Classification
   152	    // =====================================================================
   153	
   154	    /// @notice Classify `addr` without trusting `code.length` alone.
   155	    function ownerKind(address addr) internal view returns (OwnerKind) {
   156	        bytes memory code = addr.code;
   157	        if (code.length == 0) return OwnerKind.Eoa;
   158	        if (code.length == EIP7702_CODE_LENGTH && bytes3(code) == EIP7702_PREFIX) {
   159	            return OwnerKind.Delegated7702;
   160	        }
   161	        return OwnerKind.Contract;
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    /// @notice Read a Safe-compatible M-of-N configuration, fail-closed on ANY deviation.
   165	    /// @dev    Every step is exact: `getThreshold()` must return EXACTLY 32 bytes;
   166	    ///         `getOwners()` must be a canonically-encoded `address[]` (head offset == 32,
   167	    ///         `length` matching the returndata to the byte, every element with a clean
   168	    ///         upper 96 bits). A revert, empty returndata, a wide proxy answer, a
   169	    ///         non-canonical offset and a dirty address word are all REFUSED — those are
   170	    ///         exactly the shapes a fake produces.
   171	    /// @return ok  the owner answered both methods with a decodable, self-consistent M-of-N
   172	    /// @return cfg the configuration; meaningless when `ok` is false
   173	    /// @return why a short reason, for the failure message
   174	    function readMofN(address ownerAddr)
   175	        internal
   176	        view
   177	        returns (bool ok, MofN memory cfg, string memory why)
   178	    {
   179	        (bool tOk, uint256 threshold) =
   180	            _staticUint(ownerAddr, abi.encodeCall(ISafeCompatibleMofN.getThreshold, ()));
   181	        if (!tOk) return (false, cfg, "getThreshold() did not answer with exactly one uint256");
   182	
   183	        (bool oOk, uint256 ownerCount) =
   184	            _staticAddressArray(ownerAddr, abi.encodeCall(ISafeCompatibleMofN.getOwners, ()));
   185	        if (!oOk) {
   186	            return (false, cfg, "getOwners() did not answer with a canonical, distinct address[]");
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        cfg = MofN({threshold: threshold, ownerCount: ownerCount});
   190	        if (threshold < 2) return (false, cfg, "threshold < 2 (a 1-of-N owner is one key)");
   191	        if (ownerCount < threshold) return (false, cfg, "owners.length < threshold (unsatisfiable)");
   192	        return (true, cfg, "");
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    // =====================================================================
   196	    // The gates
   197	    // =====================================================================
   198	
   199	    /// @notice Refuse to leave `subject` owned by anything that is not a Safe-compatible
   200	    ///         M-of-N owner, with a named testnet acknowledgement as the only exception.
   201	    /// @param subject   the contract whose ownership is being gated
   202	    /// @param ownerAddr the owner as it reads RIGHT NOW — pass `IOwned(x).owner()`, never
   203	    ///                  the address the script *intended* to set
   204	    /// @param label     contract name, so a failure names what is mis-owned
   205	    function requireGovernanceOwner(address subject, address ownerAddr, string memory label) internal view {
   206	        _gate(subject, ownerAddr, label, false, GOVERNANCE_OWNER_ENV);
   207	    }
   208	
   209	    /// @notice Convenience: read the owner off-chain and gate it in one call.
   210	    function requireGovernanceOwner(address subject, string memory label) internal view {
   211	        requireGovernanceOwner(subject, IOwned(subject).owner(), label);
   212	    }
   213	
   214	    /// @notice The same gate with NO testnet acknowledgement path.
   215	    /// @dev    Used by the 5.7.0 migration entry point. A governance batch that upgrades a
   216	    ///         live Registry and re-points its aggregator is a production operation even
   217	    ///         when it is rehearsed on Sepolia — a rehearsal whose ownership model differs
   218	    ///         from production rehearses the wrong thing. Experiment DEPLOY scripts use the
   219	    ///         acknowledged variant above; MIGRATION does not get one.
   220	    function requireGovernanceOwnerStrict(address subject, address ownerAddr, string memory label)
   221	        internal
   222	        view
   223	    {
   224	        _gate(subject, ownerAddr, label, true, GOVERNANCE_OWNER_ENV);
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    /// @notice The strict gate, bound to a NAMED declaration env var rather than the
   228	    ///         default `GOVERNANCE_OWNER`.
   229	    /// @dev    CC-48 round-8 LOW-2. The 5.7.0 migration gates TWO subjects with two
   230	    ///         independent owners — the live `Registry` and the freshly deployed
   231	    ///         `BLSAggregator` — and there is no reason they must be the same Safe. One
   232	    ///         global env var could only bind one of them, so the migration names each
   233	    ///         one's declaration separately and both are checked. `declEnv` appears
   234	    ///         verbatim in every failure message, so an operator is told which variable to
   235	    ///         set, not just that something is undeclared.
   236	    function requireGovernanceOwnerStrictDeclaredAs(
   237	        address subject,
   238	        address ownerAddr,
   239	        string memory label,
   240	        string memory declEnv
   241	    ) internal view {
   242	        _gate(subject, ownerAddr, label, true, declEnv);
   243	    }
   244	
   245	    /// @notice Assert that `ownerAddr` is the TimelockController at `timelock`, and that
   246	    ///         the Timelock actually imposes a delay.
   247	    /// @dev    CC-48 round-7, from the archived original checklist: `Registry` governance
   248	    ///         is a different question from `BLSAggregator` governance.
   249	    ///         `Registry.setCreditPolicy` is `immediate onlyOwner`, so if the answer to
   250	    ///         "who owns Registry" is "a Timelock", the deployment evidence has to show
   251	    ///         `getMinDelay() > 0` and `Registry.owner() == that Timelock` — printing
   252	    ///         `scheduleBatch` calldata proves neither. If the answer is "a Safe", use
   253	    ///         `requireGovernanceOwnerStrict` instead; both are accepted, guessing is not.
   254	    function requireTimelockOwner(address subject, address ownerAddr, address timelock, string memory label)
   255	        internal
   256	        view
   257	    {
   258	        require(
   259	            ownerAddr == timelock,
   260	            string.concat(
   261	                "CC-48 round-7: TIMELOCK is set but ",
   262	                label,
   263	                ".owner() is a different address. The batch below would be scheduled into a",
   264	                " Timelock that cannot execute it."
   265	            )
   266	        );
   267	        require(
   268	            ownerKind(timelock) == OwnerKind.Contract,
   269	            string.concat("CC-48 round-7: TIMELOCK ", label, " is not a contract (or is a 7702 delegation)")
   270	        );
   271	        (bool ok, uint256 minDelay) = _staticUint(timelock, abi.encodeWithSignature("getMinDelay()"));
   272	        require(
   273	            ok && minDelay > 0,
   274	            string.concat(
   275	                "CC-48 round-7: TIMELOCK must answer getMinDelay() with a NON-ZERO delay. A",
   276	                " zero-delay Timelock owning ",
   277	                label,
   278	                " makes setCreditPolicy immediate again, which is the property this batch exists",
   279	                " to remove."
   280	            )
   281	        );
   282	        console.log(string.concat("  [gov-gate] ", label, " owner is a Timelock:"), timelock);
   283	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   getMinDelay()      :", minDelay);
   284	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   criterion          : owner == TIMELOCK && getMinDelay() > 0");
   285	        console.log("  [gov-gate]   NOT proven         : that this Timelock's proposers are themselves M-of-N");
   286	        console.log("  [gov-gate] subject:", subject);
   287	    }
   288	
   289	    /// @notice The Safe to hand ownership to, or address(0) if the operator did not name
   290	    ///         one. Deploy scripts transfer ownership to it as their LAST owner-gated
   291	    ///         action, then call `requireGovernanceOwner` on the result.
   292	    /// @dev    Deliberately optional at READ time so local development needs no env var,
   293	    ///         and so a deploy script can tell "hand it to the Safe" from "leave it with
   294	    ///         the deployer". It is NOT optional at GATE time any more (CC-48 round-8
   295	    ///         LOW-2): `_gate` refuses a declared-but-mismatched owner on every path, and
   296	    ///         refuses to ACCEPT an M-of-N owner that was never declared at all.
   297	    function declaredGovernanceOwner() internal view returns (address) {
   298	        return vm.envOr(GOVERNANCE_OWNER_ENV, address(0));
   299	    }
   300

   302	    // Internals
   303	    // =====================================================================
   304

   608	def external(path, stage="full"):
   609	    a.need('external_inputs_required', path is not None and Path(path).is_file(), True)
   610	    raw = Path(path).read_bytes()
   611	    data = a.plan_json(raw)
   612	    validate_external(data, stage)
   613	    return dict(sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), data=data)

   616	def validate_external(data, stage="full"):
   617	    a.need("stage", stage in STAGES, True)
   618	    required(data, (() if stage == "a7" else ("pop",)) + (("fraudProofFixture",) if stage == "full" else ())
   619	             + (("phaseC",) if stage in PC_STAGES else ()), "stage_inputs")
   620	    required(data, ('verifier', 'anni', 'dvt', 'committee', 'sampled', 'depositTargetWei',
   621	                    'releaseTag', 'releaseEvidence', 'registryBaselineCommit', 'a3aPlan'), 'external_inputs_complete')
   622	    v = data['verifier']
   623	    a.exact_keys('verifier_provenance', v, ('commit', 'ciSha256', 'ciPath', 'artifactPath', 'buildSha256'))
   624	    verifier_artifact(v)
   625	    a.need('verifier_build_identity', v['buildSha256'], VERIFIER_SHA256)
   626	    evidence_file(dict(path=v['ciPath'], sha256=v['ciSha256']), 'dvt-ci')
   627	    if stage == 'full':
   628	        fraud_fixture(data['fraudProofFixture'])
   629	    if stage in PC_STAGES:
   630	        validate_phase_c_inputs(data['phaseC'])
   631	    a.need('fixed_anni', data['anni'], ANNI)
   632	    required(data['a3aPlan'], ('path', 'sha256', 'receipts'), 'a3a_input')
   633	    reviewed_file(data['a3aPlan']['path'], data['a3aPlan']['sha256'], 'a3a_plan')
   634	    for sid in a.STEP_IDS:
   635	        a.typed(data['a3aPlan']['receipts'].get(sid), a.HASH32)
   636	        a.need('a3a_receipt_nonzero', data['a3aPlan']['receipts'][sid] != a.ZERO32, True)
   637	    a.need('fixed_dvt', data['dvt'], DVT)
   638	    a.need('fixed_committee', data['committee'], COMMITTEE)
   639	    for key in ('ciSha256', 'buildSha256'):
   640	        a.need('verifier_'+key, len(v[key]) == 64 and all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in v[key]) and int(v[key], 16) != 0, True)
   641	    a.need('verifier_commit', len(v['commit']) == 40 and all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in v['commit']), True)
   642	    for key in ('anni', 'dvt', 'committee'):
   643	        a.typed(data[key], a.ADDRESS_RX)
   644	        a.need('canonical_'+key, data[key], data[key].lower())
   645	        a.need('nonzero_'+key, data[key] != a.ZERO, True)
   646	    if stage != 'a7':
   647	        a.need('pop_three', len(data['pop']), 3)
   648	        for p in data['pop']:
   649	            a.exact_keys('pop_fields', p, ('validator', 'slot', 'calldata', 'aggregator', 'pinBlockHash', 'domainSeparator'))
   650	            a.typed(p['validator'], a.ADDRESS_RX)
   651	            a.typed(p['calldata'], a.HEXDATA)
   652	            a.need('pop_selector', p['calldata'][:10], a.keccak(POP)[:10])
   653	            words = u.uints('0x'+p['calldata'][10:])
   654	            a.need('pop_words', len(words), 14)
   655	            a.need('pop_validator', u.addr_word(words[0]), p['validator'])
   656	            a.need('pop_slot', words[5], p['slot'])
   657	            a.need('pop_slot_range', 1 <= p['slot'] <= 13, True)
   658	        for key in ('validator', 'slot'):
   659	            a.need('pop_unique_'+key, len({p[key] for p in data['pop']}), 3)
   660	    a.need('sampled_deployer_only', data['sampled'], [u.DEPLOYER])
   661	    a.need('sampled_unique', data['sampled'], sorted(set(data['sampled'])))
   662	    a.need('sampled_nonempty', bool(data['sampled']), True)
   663	    for who in data['sampled']:
   664	        a.typed(who, a.ADDRESS_RX)
   665	    a.need('deposit_target', type(data['depositTargetWei']) is int and data['depositTargetWei'] > 0, True)
   666	    for kind in EVIDENCE_KINDS:
   667	        evidence_file(data['releaseEvidence'].get(kind), kind)
   668	    ref = data['releaseEvidence'].get('sourceVerificationPlan')
   669	    required(ref, ('path', 'sha256'), 'source_verification_plan')
   670	    a.need('source_verification_nonempty', bool(reviewed_bytes(ref['path'], ref['sha256'], 'source_verification').strip()), True)

   673	@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   674	def rebuild():
   675	    head = subprocess.run(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=u.ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
   676	    return {k: v for k, v in rebuild_commit(head).items() if k not in ('tree', 'treeOwner')}

   679	@lru_cache(maxsize=4)
   680	def rebuild_commit(head):
   681	    version = subprocess.run([str(a.REBUILD_FORGE), '--version'], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
   682	    a.need('forge_version', version.splitlines()[0], 'forge Version: 1.7.1')
   683	    arts, cfg = a.compile_commit(head, version, NAMES, tuple(IMMUTABLES.items()))
   684	    # Inspect the same committed source/configuration in an isolated build tree.
   685	    directory = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='manifest-layout-')
   686	    tree = Path(directory.name)/'tree'
   687	    a.archive_commit(u.ROOT, head, tree)
   688	    env = {k: os.environ[k] for k in ('HOME', 'PATH', 'TMPDIR', 'USER', 'LOGNAME', 'LANG', 'LC_ALL', 'SVM_HOME') if k in os.environ}
   689	    env['FOUNDRY_PROFILE'] = 'default'
   690	    # Retain the pinned tree with the cached build for canonical finding-path resolution.
   691	    from release_evidence import compiled_scope, Stop
   692	    built = subprocess.run([str(a.REBUILD_FORGE), 'build', '--offline', '--ast', '--build-info'],
   693	                           cwd=tree, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True)
   694	    a.need('evidence_scope_build', built.returncode, 0)
   695	    try:
   696	        scope, _, _ = compiled_scope(tree)
   697	    except Stop as error:
   698	        raise a.Gate('evidence_scope_recomputed', str(error), 'valid compiled release scope') from None
   699	    result = subprocess.run([str(a.REBUILD_FORGE), 'inspect', 'BLSAggregator', 'storage-layout', '--offline', '--json'],
   700	        cwd=tree, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True)
   701	    a.need('fraud_layout_inspect', result.returncode, 0)
   702	    layout = a.plan_json(result.stdout)
   703	    a.need('fraud_layout_build_parity', commitment_slot(layout, 1),
   704	           commitment_slot(arts['BLSAggregator']['storageLayout'], 1))
   705	    arts['BLSAggregator']['storageLayout'] = layout
   706	    functions = [n for n in a.ast_nodes(arts['BLSAggregator']['ast']) if n.get('nodeType') == 'FunctionDefinition' and n.get('name') == 'version']
   707	    a.need('built_aggregator_version', [[n.get('value') for n in a.ast_nodes(f['body']) if n.get('nodeType') == 'Literal'] for f in functions], [[AGG_VERSION]])
   708	    return dict(calldataSignatures=isolation.signature_catalogue(tree), oldOwnerInventory=isolation.inventory_source(tree, mode="release", names=tuple(sorted(set(isolation.NAMES) | {"xPNTsFactoryV2", "xPNTsTokenV2", "xPNTsTokenV2Ext"}))), tree=tree, treeOwner=directory, scope=scope, commit=head, aggregatorVersion=AGG_VERSION, forgeVersion=version.strip(), contracts={name: dict(
   709	        bytecode=art['bytecode']['object'], creationHashes=creation_hashes(art['bytecode']['object']), runtimeIdentity=art['runtimeIdentity'],
   710	        storageLayout=art.get('storageLayout', {}), metadata=a.metadata_identity(art, cfg))
   711	        for name, art in arts.items()})
   712

   714	@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   715	def rebuild_dvt():
   716	    a.need('dvt_repo_configured', bool(os.environ.get('MANIFEST_DVT_REPO')), True)
   717	    version = subprocess.run([str(a.REBUILD_FORGE), '--version'], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
   718	    a.need('dvt_forge_version', version.splitlines()[0], 'forge Version: 1.7.1')
   719	    # Local clone of DVT (YetAnotherAA-Validator) containing VERIFIER_COMMIT; never a hardcoded machine path.
   720	    repo = Path(os.environ['MANIFEST_DVT_REPO'])
   721	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='manifest-dvt-') as directory:
   722	        tree = Path(directory)/'tree'
   723	        a.archive_commit(repo, VERIFIER_COMMIT, tree)
   724	        env = {k:os.environ[k] for k in ('HOME','PATH','TMPDIR','USER','LOGNAME','LANG','LC_ALL','SVM_HOME') if k in os.environ}
   725	        env['FOUNDRY_PROFILE'] = 'default'
   726	        result = subprocess.run([str(a.REBUILD_FORGE), 'build', '--offline', '--force', '--skip', 'test', '--skip', 'script', '--use', '0.8.33'],
   727	                                cwd=tree/'contracts', env=env, capture_output=True)
   728	        a.need('dvt_offline_rebuild', result.returncode, 0)
   729	        art = a.raw_artifact(tree/'contracts', 'OverIssueFraudProofVerifier')
   730	        a.need('dvt_rebuilt_creation', creation_hashes(art['bytecode']['object'], 'OverIssueFraudProofVerifier')['strippedCreationKeccak'], VERIFIER_EXECUTABLE)
   731	        mock = a.raw_artifact(tree/'contracts', 'MockOverIssuableToken')
   732	        a.need('dvt_mock_rebuilt_runtime', a.keccak(mock['deployedBytecode']['object']), MOCK_TOKEN_RUNTIME_HASH)
   733	        return dict(bytecode=art['bytecode']['object'], creationHashes=creation_hashes(art['bytecode']['object'], 'OverIssueFraudProofVerifier'),
   734	                    mockRuntimeHash=a.keccak(mock['deployedBytecode']['object']))

   737	def release_gate(inputs, build, pin):
   738	    data = inputs['data']
   739	    state = subprocess.run(['git', 'status', '--porcelain', '--untracked-files=all'], cwd=u.ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
   740	    a.need('release_clean_tree', state.stdout, '')
   741	    a.need('built_aggregator_version', build['aggregatorVersion'], AGG_VERSION)
   742	    head = subprocess.run(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=u.ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
   743	    a.need('release_current_head', head, build['commit'])
   744	    for kind in EVIDENCE_KINDS:
   745	        evidence_file(data['releaseEvidence'].get(kind), kind, build['commit'])
   746	    evidence_file(dict(path=data['verifier']['ciPath'], sha256=data['verifier']['ciSha256']), 'dvt-ci', build['commit'])
   747	    verifier = rebuild_dvt()
   748	    a.need('dvt_artifact_rebuild', verifier_executable(verifier['bytecode']),
   749	           verifier_executable(verifier_artifact(data['verifier'])['bytecode']))
   750	    contracts = dict(build['contracts'], OverIssueFraudProofVerifier=verifier)
   751	    evidence = evidence_file(data['releaseEvidence']['reproducible-build'], 'reproducible-build', build['commit'])
   752	    build['metadataOnlyDifferences'] = compare_build_evidence(evidence, contracts)
   753	    build['verifierCreationHashes'] = verifier['creationHashes']
   754	    tag = subprocess.run(['git', 'rev-parse', '--verify', 'refs/tags/'+data['releaseTag']+'^{commit}'],
   755	                         cwd=u.ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True)
   756	    a.need('release_tag', tag.returncode, 0)
   757	    a.need('release_tag_head', tag.stdout.strip(), build['commit'])
   758	    baseline = subprocess.run(['git', 'rev-parse', '--verify', data['registryBaselineCommit']+'^{commit}'],
   759	                              cwd=u.ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
   760	    a.need('registry_baseline_not_head', baseline != build['commit'], True)
   761	    old, _ = a.compile_commit(baseline, build['forgeVersion'], ('Registry',), (('Registry', ('__self',)),))
   762	    a.need('registry_baseline_runtime', pin['registryImplementationCode'],
   763	           runtime_bytes(old['Registry'], {'__self': int(pin['registryImplementation'], 16)}))
   764	    layout = build['contracts']['Registry']['storageLayout']
   765	    def normalized(value):
   766	        def shape(t):
   767	            info = value['types'][t]
   768	            result = {k: info[k] for k in ('encoding', 'label', 'numberOfBytes')}
   769	            for key in ('key', 'value', 'base'):
   770	                if key in info:
   771	                    result[key] = shape(info[key])
   772	            if 'members' in info:
   773	                result['members'] = [(m['label'], m['slot'], m['offset'], shape(m['type'])) for m in info['members']]
   774	            return result
   775	        return [(x['label'], x['slot'], x['offset'], shape(x['type'])) for x in value['storage']]
   776	    a.need('registry_layout', normalized(layout), normalized(old['Registry']['storageLayout']))
   777	    a.need('registry_storage_count', len(layout['storage']), 32)
   778	    a.need('registry_owner_slot', next(x['slot'] for x in layout['storage'] if x['label'] == '_owner'), '0')
   779

   852	def unchanged_old_activity(rpc, plan, block):
   853	    actual = old_activity(rpc, plan['pin']['oldAggregator'], block,
   854	                          plan['externalInputs']['data'].get('pop'))
   855	    expected = {k:copy.deepcopy(v) for k,v in plan['pin']['oldActivity'].items() if k != 'dvtProposals'}
   856	    # Time/hash advance on the fork; all exemption predicates have just run again.
   857	    for activity in (actual, expected):
   858	        for item in activity.get('exemptions', []):
   859	            item.pop('pinTime', None)
   860	            item.pop('blockHash', None)
   861	    a.need('old_activity_unchanged', actual, expected)

   864	def dvt_activity(rpc, dvt, block):
   865	    # DVT owns proposal creation; aggregation events alone cannot find unsigned proposals.
   866	    # Getters are the inventory; logs must reconcile with them exactly (missing logs are not "none").
   867	    start = u.creation(rpc, dvt, block)
   868	    proposals = u.scan(rpc, dvt, start, block, [a.keccak('ProposalCreated(uint256,address,uint8)')])
   869	    a.need('dvt_stale_next_id', a.word(rpc, dvt, 'nextProposalId()', block=block), STALE_PROPOSAL_NEXT_ID)
   870	    ids = list(range(1, STALE_PROPOSAL_NEXT_ID))
   871	    a.need('dvt_proposal_inventory', sorted(int(log['topics'][1], 16) for log in proposals), ids)
   872	    words = {i: u.uints(a.call(rpc, dvt, 'proposals(uint256)', i, block=block)) for i in ids}
   873	    open_ids = [i for i in ids if words[i][3] != 1]
   874	    for proposal in open_ids:
   875	        a.need('dvt_stale_proposal_listed', proposal in STALE_PROPOSALS, True)
   876	    for proposal in open_ids:
   877	        w = words[proposal]
   878	        sibling, evidence = STALE_PROPOSALS.get(proposal, (None, None))
   879	        a.need('dvt_stale_proposal_shape', (w[0], w[1], w[3], w[4], w[5]), (int(STALE_PROPOSAL_OPERATOR, 16), 1, 0, 1, evidence))
   880	        peer = words.get(sibling, [0]*6)
   881	        a.need('dvt_stale_sibling_executed', (peer[0], peer[1], peer[3], peer[5]), (w[0], w[1], 1, evidence))
   882	    a.need('dvt_stale_open_set', open_ids, sorted(STALE_PROPOSALS))
   883	    return proposals
   884

   886	def old_pointers(rpc, d, old, block):
   887	    bindings = {}
   888	    for target, getter in ((d['dvt'], 'BLS_AGGREGATOR()'), (d['committee'], 'blsAggregator()')):
   889	        a.need('pointer_owner', a.address(rpc, target, 'owner()', block), u.DEPLOYER)
   890	        a.need('old_pointer', a.address(rpc, target, getter, block), old)
   891	        bindings[target] = u.code(rpc, target, block)
   892	    return bindings

   895	def old_timelock_section(pin):
   896	    return pin['oldTimelock']
   897	
   898	
   899	def validate_old_timelock(pin):
   900	    a.need('old_timelock_required', 'oldTimelock' in pin, True)
   901	    section = old_timelock_section(pin)
   902	    a.need('old_timelock_schema', isinstance(section, dict) and set(section) == {'block', 'reads', 'approvalOwners'}
   903	           and isinstance(section.get('approvalOwners'), dict)
   904	           and isinstance(section.get('reads'), list) and all(isinstance(r, dict) and
   905	           ((set(r) == {'method', 'params', 'block', 'result'} and
   906	             r['method'] == 'eth_getLogs' and isinstance(r['params'], list) and
   907	             isinstance(r['block'], str) and isinstance(r['result'], list)) or
   908	           (set(r) == {'target', 'signature', 'args', 'block', 'result'} and
   909	           isinstance(r['target'], str) and isinstance(r['signature'], str) and
   910	           isinstance(r['args'], list) and isinstance(r['block'], str) and
   911	           isinstance(r['result'], str))) for r in section['reads']), True)
   912	    a.need('old_timelock_block', section['block'] == pin['stateBlock'] and
   913	           all(r['block'] == pin['stateBlock'] for r in section['reads']), True)
   914	    reads = iter(section['reads'])
   915	    def replay(method, params):
   916	        row = next(reads, None)
   917	        a.need('old_timelock_replay', row is not None and
   918	               ((row.get('method') == method == 'eth_getLogs' and params == row['params']) or
   919	                ('target' in row and method == 'eth_call' and
   920	                 params == [dict(to=row['target'], data=a.calldata(row['signature'], *row['args'])), row['block']])), True)
   921	        return copy.deepcopy(row['result'])
   922	    actual = old_timelock_holds_nothing(replay, pin, pin['stateBlock'])
   923	    a.need('old_timelock_replay', actual['approvalOwners'], section['approvalOwners'])
   924	    a.need('old_timelock_unused', next(reads, None), None)
   925

   927	# Getter discovery changes only at these reviewed steps. Deployments also expand coverage.
   928	DISCOVERY_STEPS = {
   929	    'A4.factory': 'factory deployment',
   930	    'A6.upgrade': 'Registry implementation; getters unchanged',
   931	    'upgrade_execute_exec': 'factory pointer',
   932	    'C3.deploy': 'factory token enumeration',
   933	    'C3.configure': 'operator token',
   934	}
   935	
   936	
   937	def pin_discovery(pin):
   938	    section = pin['oldOwnerIsolation']
   939	    return dict(targets=copy.deepcopy(section['targets']), xpnts=list(section['xpnts']))
   940	
   941	
   942	def build_expected_discovery(plan):
   943	    state = pin_discovery(plan['pin'])
   944	    expected = {}
   945	    required_ids = set(DISCOVERY_STEPS)
   946	    if plan['stage'] == 'a7':
   947	        required_ids -= {'upgrade_execute_exec', 'C3.deploy', 'C3.configure'}
   948	    elif plan['stage'] == 'b4':
   949	        required_ids -= {'C3.deploy', 'C3.configure'}
   950	    ids = [s['id'] for s in plan['steps']]
   951	    a.need('discovery_step_table', set(ids) & set(DISCOVERY_STEPS) == required_ids and len(ids) == len(set(ids)), True)
   952	    factory = plan['addresses']['xPNTsFactoryV2']
   953	    token = a.create_address(factory, 1)
   954	    sensitive = ('setXPNTsFactory(address)', 'deployxPNTsToken(string,string,string,string,uint256,address)',
   955	                 'configureOperator(address,address)', 'setStaking(address)', 'setMySBT(address)', 'upgradeToAndCall(address,bytes)')
   956	    schedules = {a.keccak(sig)[:10] for sig in plan['rebuild']['calldataSignatures'] if sig.startswith(('schedule(', 'scheduleBatch('))}
   957	    allowed = {sensitive[0]: {'upgrade_execute_exec'}, sensitive[1]: {'C3.deploy'}, sensitive[2]: {'C3.configure'},
   958	               sensitive[5]: {'A6.upgrade', 'upgrade_execute_exec'}}
   959	    for step in plan['steps']:
   960	        sid = step['id']
   961	        # Inspect executed payloads, excluding Safe approvals and timelock scheduling.
   962	        data, effects = step['tx']['data'], []
   963	        if step['tx']['to'] is not None:
   964	            decoded = isolation.assert_manifest_calldata([step], plan['rebuild']['calldataSignatures'])
   965	            outer = decoded[-1] if decoded else {}
   966	            payload = outer.get('arguments', [])[2] if outer.get('signature', '').startswith('execTransaction(') else data
   967	            inactive = data[:10] == a.keccak('approveHash(bytes32)')[:10] or payload[:10] in schedules
   968	            effects = [] if inactive else [r for r in decoded if r['signature'] in sensitive]
   969	            a.need('discovery_unlisted_effect', all(sid in allowed.get(r['signature'], set()) for r in effects), True)
   970	        if sid == 'A4.factory':
   971	            a.need('discovery_factory_prediction', step.get('creates'), factory)
   972	            state['targets']['releaseFactory'] = factory
   973	        elif sid == 'upgrade_execute_exec':
   974	            calls = [r['arguments'][0].lower() for r in effects if r['signature'] == sensitive[0]]
   975	            a.need('discovery_factory_argument', bool(calls) and set(calls) == {factory}, True)
   976	            state['targets']['legacyFactory'] = state['targets']['xPNTsFactory']
   977	            state['targets']['xPNTsFactory'] = factory
   978	        elif sid == 'C3.deploy':
   979	            a.need('discovery_token_prediction', step['tx']['to'] == factory and
   980	                   data == a.calldata('deployxPNTsToken(string,string,string,string,uint256,address)', *PC_TOKEN, PC_RATE, a.ZERO) and
   981	                   any(r.get('kind') == 'code' and r['target'] == token for r in step['readbacks']), True)
   982	            state['xpnts'] = sorted(set(state['xpnts']) | {token})
   983	        elif sid == 'C3.configure':
   984	            a.need('discovery_operator_argument', step['tx']['to'] == u.SP and
   985	                   data == a.calldata('configureOperator(address,address)', token, PC_TREASURY), True)
   986	            state['xpnts'] = sorted(set(state['xpnts']) | {token})
   987	        expected[sid] = copy.deepcopy(state)
   988	    return expected
   989	
   990	
   991	def expected_discovery(plan, passed):
   992	    state = pin_discovery(plan['pin'])
   993	    for step in plan['steps']:
   994	        if step['id'] in passed:
   995	            state = plan['expectedDiscoveryAfter'][step['id']]
   996	    return copy.deepcopy(state)
   997	
   998	
   999	def discovery_coverage(plan, passed):
  1000	    pin = copy.deepcopy(plan['pin'])
  1001	    section = pin['oldOwnerIsolation']
  1002	    state = expected_discovery(plan, passed)
  1003	    section['targets'].update(state['targets'])
  1004	    section['xpnts'] = sorted(set(section['xpnts']) | set(state['xpnts']) | set(isolation.ROUND_CLONES))
  1005	    section['tokens'] = sorted(set(section['tokens']) | set(section['xpnts']))
  1006	    inventory = section['inventory']
  1007	    old_factory = plan['pin']['oldOwnerIsolation']['targets']['xPNTsFactory']
  1008	    if 'A4.factory' in passed:
  1009	        factory = plan['addresses']['xPNTsFactoryV2']
  1010	        section['targets']['legacyFactory'] = old_factory
  1011	        section['targets']['releaseFactory'] = factory
  1012	        pin['discoveryReleaseFactory'] = factory
  1013	        inventory['legacyFactory'] = inventory.get('targetInventories', {}).get(old_factory, inventory['xPNTsFactory'])
  1014	        release = plan['rebuild']['oldOwnerInventory']
  1015	        inventory['releaseFactory'] = release['xPNTsFactoryV2']
  1016	        inventory.setdefault('targetInventories', {})[factory] = release['xPNTsFactoryV2']
  1017	        for token in set(section['xpnts']) - set(plan['pin']['oldOwnerIsolation']['xpnts']):
  1018	            spec = copy.deepcopy(release['xPNTsTokenV2'])
  1019	            for group in ('roles', 'authorities', 'mappings'):
  1020	                rows = {r['signature']: r for r in spec[group]}
  1021	                rows.update({r['signature']: r for r in release['xPNTsTokenV2Ext'][group]})
  1022	                spec[group] = list(rows.values())
  1023	            inventory['targetInventories'][token] = spec
  1024	    pin['discoveryExpected'] = state
  1025	    pin['discoveryOriginalFactory'] = old_factory
  1026	    pin['discoveryRetainedTokens'] = plan['pin']['oldOwnerIsolation']['xpnts']
  1027	    return pin
  1028	
  1029	
  1030	def current_discovery(rpc, pin, block):
  1031	    found = isolation.discover_targets(rpc, dict(pin, stateBlock=block))
  1032	    release_tokens = None
  1033	    if 'discoveryReleaseFactory' in pin and u.code(rpc, pin['discoveryReleaseFactory'], block) != '0x':
  1034	        found['releaseFactory'] = [pin['discoveryReleaseFactory']]
  1035	        release_tokens = u.addr_array(a.call(rpc, pin['discoveryReleaseFactory'], 'getAllTokens()', block=block))
  1036	        found['xPNTsToken'] = sorted(set(found['xPNTsToken']) | set(release_tokens))
  1037	    if 'discoveryOriginalFactory' in pin and found['xPNTsFactory'][0] != pin['discoveryOriginalFactory']:
  1038	        found['legacyFactory'] = [pin['discoveryOriginalFactory']]
  1039	        tokens = u.addr_array(a.call(rpc, pin['discoveryOriginalFactory'], 'getAllTokens()', block=block))
  1040	        a.need('old_owner_original_factory_enumeration', set(isolation.ROUND_CLONES) | set(pin['discoveryRetainedTokens']) <= set(tokens), True)
  1850	    for gap in plan.get('nonceGaps', []):
  1851	        a.need('a3a_gap_sender', gap['from'] in {v['address'] for k,v in pin['senders'].items() if k != 'anni'}, True)
  1852	        a.need('a3a_gap_before_pin', a.qty(gap['nonce']) < next(v['nonce'] for v in pin['senders'].values() if v['address'] == gap['from']), True)
  1853	    return plan
  1854	
  1855	
  1856	def history_gaps(rpc, pin, spec, plan):
  1857	    for label in ('deployer', 'approver', 'executor'):
  1858	        sender = pin['senders'][label]
  1859	        tx = {'from': sender['address'], 'nonce': hex(sender['nonce'])}
  1860	        a.check_gaps(rpc, plan, tx)
  1861	        a.check_gaps(rpc, spec, tx)
  1862	
  1863	
  1864	def validate_nonce_history(pin, inputs):
  1865	    spec = inputs['data']['a3aPlan']
  1866	    plan = history_steps(pin, inputs)
  1867	    required(pin, ('a3aHistory', 'a3aGapReads'), 'a3a_history_required')
  1868	    a.exact_keys('a3a_history', pin['a3aHistory'], a.STEP_IDS)
  1869	    for step in plan['steps']:
  1870	        proof = pin['a3aHistory'][step['id']]
  1871	        receipt, mined = proof['receipt'], proof['transaction']
  1872	        txhash = spec['receipts'][step['id']]
  1873	        a.need('a3a_mined_'+step['id'], isinstance(receipt, dict), True)
  1874	        a.need('a3a_receipt_hash', receipt['transactionHash'], txhash)
  1875	        a.need('a3a_transaction_hash', mined['hash'], txhash)
  1876	        a.need('a3a_status_'+step['id'], a.qty(receipt['status']), 1)
  1877	        a.need('a3a_before_pin', a.qty(receipt['blockNumber']) <= a.qty(pin['stateBlock']), True)
  1878	        a.need('a3a_block', mined['blockNumber'], receipt['blockNumber'])
  1879	        a.need('a3a_block_hash', mined['blockHash'], receipt['blockHash'])
  1880	        a.mined_identity(mined, step['tx'], step['id'])
  1881	    def replay(method, params):
  1882	        matches = [r['result'] for r in pin['a3aGapReads'] if r['method'] == method and r['params'] == params]
  1883	        a.need('gap_proof_read', bool(matches), True)
  1884	        result = matches[0]
  1885	        if method == 'eth_getTransactionByHash' and result is not None:
  1886	            a.need('a3a_gap_mined_before_pin', a.qty(result['blockNumber']) <= a.qty(pin['stateBlock']), True)
  1887	        return result
  1888	    history_gaps(replay, pin, spec, plan)
  1889	    governance_evidence(pin, inputs)
  1890	
  1891	
  1892	def reconcile_nonces(rpc, pin, inputs, governance_rpcs=None):
  1893	    spec = inputs['data']['a3aPlan']
  1894	    plan = history_steps(pin, inputs)
  1895	    pin['a3aHistory'] = {}
  1896	    for step in plan['steps']:
  1897	        txhash = spec['receipts'][step['id']]
  1898	        receipt = rpc('eth_getTransactionReceipt', [txhash])
  1899	        a.need('a3a_mined_'+step['id'], isinstance(receipt, dict), True)
  1900	        block = rpc('eth_getBlockByNumber', [receipt['blockNumber'], False])
  1901	        a.need('a3a_canonical_receipt', receipt['blockHash'], block['hash'])
  1902	        pin['a3aHistory'][step['id']] = dict(receipt=receipt,
  1903	            transaction=rpc('eth_getTransactionByHash', [txhash]))
  1904	    pin['a3aGapReads'] = []
  1905	    def recording(method, params):
  1906	        result = rpc(method, params)
  1907	        if method == 'eth_getTransactionByHash' and result is not None:
  1908	            a.need('a3a_gap_mined_before_pin', a.qty(result['blockNumber']) <= a.qty(pin['stateBlock']), True)
  1909	        pin['a3aGapReads'].append(dict(method=method, params=params, result=result))
  1910	        return result
  1911	    history_gaps(recording, pin, spec, plan)
  1912	    governance_evidence(pin, inputs, governance_rpcs)
  1913	    validate_nonce_history(pin, inputs)
  1914	
  1915	
  1916	def collect(rpc, block, inputs, stage="full", governance_rpcs=None):
  1917	    validate_external(inputs['data'], stage)
  1918	    pin = u.collect(rpc, block)
  1919	    d = inputs['data']
  1920	    pin['isolationControlCases'] = copy.deepcopy(d.get('isolationControlCases',{}))
  1921	    block = pin['stateBlock']
  1922	    who = d['anni']
  1923	    n = a.nonce(rpc, who, block)
  1924	    a.need('anni_pending_nonce', a.nonce(rpc, who, 'pending'), n)
  1925	    a.need('anni_eoa', u.code(rpc, who, block), '0x')
  1926	    pin['senders']['anni'] = dict(address=who, nonce=n, balance=a.qty(rpc('eth_getBalance', [who, block])))
  1927	    a.need('registry_version', a.call(rpc, u.REG, 'version()', block=block), a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(string)', 'Registry-5.8.0'))
  1928	    pin['registryImplementation'] = u.addr_word(int(rpc('eth_getStorageAt', [u.REG, u.IMPL_SLOT, block]), 16))
  1929	    pin['registryImplementationCode'] = u.code(rpc, pin['registryImplementation'], block)
  1930	    reconcile_nonces(rpc, pin, inputs, governance_rpcs)
  1931	    pin['oldAggregator'] = a.address(rpc, u.REG, 'blsAggregator()', block)
  1932	    a.need('fixed_old_aggregator', pin['oldAggregator'], OLD_AGGREGATOR)
  1933	    maximum = a.word(rpc, pin['oldAggregator'], 'MAX_VALIDATORS()', block=block)
  1934	    a.need('guardian_maximum', maximum, 13)
  1935	    pin['guardianInventory'] = [dict(slot=slot, validator=u.addr_word(a.word(rpc, pin['oldAggregator'], 'validatorAtSlot(uint8)', slot, block=block))) for slot in range(1, maximum+1)]
  1936	    pin['oldActivity'], reads = record_old_activity(rpc, pin['oldAggregator'], block, d.get('pop'))
  1937	    pin['oldActivityReads'] = dict(primary=reads)
  1938	    a.need('old_aggregator_owner', a.address(rpc, pin['oldAggregator'], 'owner()', block), u.DEPLOYER)
  1939	    a.need('old_aggregator_slash_policy_admin', a.address(rpc, pin['oldAggregator'], 'slashPolicyAdmin()', block), u.DEPLOYER)
  1940	    pin['oldActivity']['dvtProposals'] = dvt_activity(rpc, d['dvt'], block)
  1941	    pin['bindings'] = old_pointers(rpc, d, pin['oldAggregator'], block)
  1942	    pin.update(balances(rpc, pin['pauseList'], block))
  1943	    drain_gate(pin)
  1944	    ep = pin['immutables']['entryPoint']
  1945	    deposit = u.uints(a.call(rpc, ep, 'getDepositInfo(address)', u.SP, block=block))
  1946	    a.need('deposit_info_shape', len(deposit), 5)
  1947	    pin['entryPointDeposit'], _, pin['stake'], pin['stakeDelay'], pin['withdrawTime'] = deposit
  1948	    a.need('stake_not_unlocking', pin['withdrawTime'], 0)
  1949	    # Isolation records discovery calls, bounded role-event history and canonical blocks
  1950	    # so build_plan can replay every new inventory gate on both endpoint transcripts.
  1951	    callsite_source = isolation.callsite_target_source()
  1952	    pin['oldOwnerIsolation'] = isolation.collect_deployed(rpc, pin, inputs['data'].get('deployedSources'), callsite_source['expected'], fork=isolation.local_control_fork(), pending=inputs['data'].get('pendingDsrRuling'))
  1953	    pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['callsiteTargetSource'] = callsite_source
  1954	    pin['oldTimelock'] = old_timelock_holds_nothing(rpc, pin, block)
  1955	    if stage in PC_STAGES:
  1956	        pin['phaseC'] = collect_phase_c(rpc, block, inputs, pin)
  1957	    return pin
  1958	
  1959	
  1960	def collect_pair(primary, secondary, block, inputs, stage='full'):
  1961	    pin = collect(primary, block, inputs, stage, (primary, secondary))
  1962	    independent = collect(secondary, block, inputs, stage, (primary, secondary))
  1963	    a.need('independent_pin', independent, pin)
  1964	    pin['oldActivityReads']['secondary'] = copy.deepcopy(independent['oldActivityReads']['primary'])
  1965	    pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['endpointResults'] = dict(
  1966	        primary=copy.deepcopy(pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['reads']),
  1967	        secondary=copy.deepcopy(independent['oldOwnerIsolation']['reads']))
  1968	    if 'sourceSelection' in pin['oldOwnerIsolation']:
  1969	        pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['endpointIdentities'] = {label:isolation.endpoint_identity(rpc,label) for label,rpc in (('primary',primary),('secondary',secondary))}
  1970	        isolation.assert_endpoint_identities(pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['endpointIdentities'])
  1971	    return pin
  1972	
  1973	
  1974	def predicate(target, sig, args, value, index=None):
  1975	    p = dict(kind='call', target=target, signature=sig, args=list(args), expected=value)
  1976	    if index is not None:
  1977	        p['index'] = index
  1978	    return p
  1979	
  1980	
  1981	def word_pred(target, sig, value, *args, index=None):
  1982	    return predicate(target, sig, args, value, index)
  1983	
  1984	
  1985	def address_pred(target, sig, value, *args):
  1986	    return word_pred(target, sig, int(value, 16), *args)
  1987	
  1988	
  1989	def runtime_bytes(art, values):
  1990	    identity = art['runtimeIdentity']
  1991	    raw = bytearray.fromhex(identity['artifactRuntime'][2:])
  1992	    for entry in identity['ranges']:
  1993	        a.need('immutable_supplied_'+entry['name'], entry['name'] in values, True)
  1994	        raw[entry['start']:entry['start']+entry['length']] = int(values[entry['name']]).to_bytes(32, 'big')
  1995	    return '0x'+raw.hex()
  1996	
  1997	
  1998	def token_immutables(target, protocol):
  1999	    empty, _ = a.short_string('')
  2000	    one, _ = a.short_string('1')
  2001	    hname, hver = a.keccak('0x'), a.keccak('1')
  2002	    domain = a.keccak(a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(bytes32,bytes32,bytes32,uint256,address)',
  2003	        a.keccak('EIP712Domain(string name,string version,uint256 chainId,address verifyingContract)'), hname, hver, a.CHAIN, target))
  2004	    return dict(PROTOCOL_REGISTRY=int(protocol, 16), _cachedThis=int(target, 16), _cachedChainId=a.CHAIN,
  2005	        _cachedDomainSeparator=int(domain, 16), _hashedName=int(hname, 16), _hashedVersion=int(hver, 16),
  2006	        _name=int.from_bytes(empty, 'big'), _version=int.from_bytes(one, 'big'))
  2007	
  2008	
  2009	def safe_hash(inner, n, target=u.TL):
  2010	    domain = a.keccak(a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(bytes32,uint256,address)',
  2011	                      a.keccak('EIP712Domain(uint256 chainId,address verifyingContract)'), a.CHAIN, u.SAFE))
  2012	    typehash = a.keccak('SafeTx(address to,uint256 value,bytes data,uint8 operation,uint256 safeTxGas,uint256 baseGas,uint256 gasPrice,address gasToken,address refundReceiver,uint256 nonce)')
  2013	    h = a.keccak(a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(bytes32,address,uint256,bytes32,uint8,uint256,uint256,uint256,address,address,uint256)',
  2014	                        typehash, target, 0, a.keccak(inner), 0, 0, 0, 0, a.ZERO, a.ZERO, n))
  2015	    return a.keccak('0x1901'+domain[2:]+h[2:])
  2016	
  2017	
  2018	def safe_payload(inner, target=u.TL):
  2019	    # Approved-hash signatures: each r is an owner, s=0, v=1. Safe requires ascending owners.
  2020	    signatures = '0x'+''.join(who[2:].zfill(64)+'0'*64+'01' for who in sorted((a.APPROVER, a.EXECUTOR)))
  2021	    return a.calldata('execTransaction(address,uint256,bytes,uint8,uint256,uint256,uint256,address,address,bytes)',
  2022	                      target, 0, inner, 0, 0, 0, 0, a.ZERO, a.ZERO, signatures)
  2023	
  2024	
  2025	
  2026	def domain_separator(aggregator):
  2027	    return a.keccak(a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(bytes32,uint256,address,address)',
  2028	        a.keccak('SuperPaymaster.BLSConsensus.v1'), a.CHAIN, aggregator, u.REG))
  2029	
  2030	
  2031	def prefix_payload(plan):
  2032	    steps = copy.deepcopy(plan['steps'])
  2033	    for step in steps:
  2034	        for key in (u.MEASURED_KEYS - {'executionTimestamp'}) | {'skipped', 'gasEvidence', 'variantMeasurements'}:
  2035	            step.pop(key, None)
  2036	        step['tx'].pop('gas', None)
  2037	        for tx in step.get('variants', {}).values():
  2038	            tx.pop('gas', None)
  2039	    # Phase C pin reads never affect the A/B prefix; a7/b4 sources are collected without them.
  2040	    pin = {k: v for k, v in plan['pin'].items() if k != 'phaseC'}
  2041	    return dict(pin=pin, addresses=plan['addresses'], steps=steps,
  2042	                activationScheduleNotBefore=plan['activationScheduleNotBefore'])
  2043	
  2044	
  2045	def bind_timing(plan):
  2046	    """Derive the same mined-block clock used by wait_until and replay."""
  2047	    now = plan['pin']['stateTimestamp']
  2048	    etas, gas_eta, verifier_eta = {}, 0, 0
  2049	    for step in plan['steps']:
  2050	        sid = step['id']
  2051	        target = now
  2052	        if sid == 'activation_schedule_approve':
  2053	            target = max(target, gas_eta, plan['activationScheduleNotBefore'])
  2054	        if sid.endswith('execute_exec'):
  2055	            target = max(target, etas[step['operation']])
  2056	        if sid == 'B4':
  2057	            target = max(target, verifier_eta)
  2058	        step['warpTarget'] = target
  2059	        now = target + 1
  2060	        step['executionTimestamp'] = now
  2061	        if sid.endswith('schedule_exec'):
  2062	            etas[step['operation']] = now + u.DELAY
  2063	        if sid == 'upgrade_execute_exec':
  2064	            gas_eta = now + u.DELAY
  2065	        if sid == 'A12b':
  2066	            verifier_eta = now + 4*DAY
  2067	
  2068	
  2069	def source_plan_bytes(plan):
  2070	    return (json.dumps(plan, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'))+'\n').encode()
  2071	
  2072	
  2073	def stage_source(plan, rpc):
  2074	    # Keep measured gas in stage-<name>.json; source plans are independently
  2075	    # reproducible offline, while their prefix binds the executed fork timing.
  2076	    rebuilt = build_plan(plan['pin'], plan['externalInputs'], plan['stage'], plan['stageSources'])
  2077	    verify_prefix(rebuilt, plan, plan['stage'])
  2078	    return dict(plan=rebuilt, readback=stage_readback(plan, rpc))
  2079	
  2080	
  2081	def prefix_digest(plan):
  2082	    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(prefix_payload(plan), sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode()).hexdigest()
  2083	
  2084	
  2085	def verify_prefix(plan, prior, stage):
  2086	    a.need('prefix_stage', prior['stage'], stage)
  2087	    a.need('prefix_digest', prior['prefixDigest'], prefix_digest(prior))
  2088	    expected = prefix_payload(plan)
  2089	    expected['steps'] = expected['steps'][:len(prior['steps'])]
  2090	    expected['addresses'] = {k: expected['addresses'].get(k) for k in prior['addresses']}
  2091	    if stage == 'c':
  2092	        # Stage c ends with the complete Phase C expansion.
  2093	        a.need('prefix_boundary', [s['id'] for s in prior['steps'] if s.get('phase') == 'C'][-1:],
  2094	               [s['id'] for s in plan['steps'] if s.get('phase') == 'C'][-1:])
  2095	        a.need('prefix_boundary_last', prior['steps'][-1].get('phase'), 'C')
  2096	    else:
  2097	        a.need('prefix_boundary', prior['steps'][-1]['id'], 'A7' if stage == 'a7' else 'B4')
  2098	    a.need('prefix_exact', prefix_payload(prior), expected)
  2099	
  2100	
  2101	def stage_readback(plan, rpc):
  2102	    a.need('stage_local', getattr(rpc, 'local', False), True)
  2103	    domain = a.call(rpc, plan['addresses']['BLSAggregator'], 'domainSeparator()')
  2104	    a.need('stage_domain', domain, plan['expectedDomainSeparator'])
  2105	    return dict(prefixDigest=plan['prefixDigest'], domainSeparator=domain)
  2106	
  2107	
  2108	def stage_pin(pin, stage):
  2109	    """Require Phase C evidence before the builder reads it; strip it for earlier stages."""
  2110	    if stage in PC_STAGES:
  2111	        a.need('stage_pin_phase_c', 'phaseC' in pin, True)
  2112	        return pin
  2113	    return {k: v for k, v in pin.items() if k != 'phaseC'}
  2114	
  2115	
  2116	def build_plan(pin, inputs=None, stage='full', stage_sources=None):
  2117	    a.need('manifest_inputs_required', inputs is not None, True)
  2118	    required(inputs, ('sha256', 'data'), 'external_binding')
  2119	    a.typed('0x'+inputs['sha256'], a.HASH32)
  2120	    a.need('external_digest_nonzero', int(inputs['sha256'], 16) != 0, True)
  2121	    d = inputs['data']
  2122	    validate_external(d, stage)
  2123	    # Phase C pin reads belong only to the stages that build Phase C; a7/b4 prefixes are
  2124	    # byte-identical whether or not the supplied pin carries them.
  2125	    pin = stage_pin(pin, stage)
  2126	    sources = copy.deepcopy(stage_sources or {})
  2127	    a.need("stage_sources", set(sources), set() if stage == "a7" else ({"a7"} if stage == "b4" else ({"a7", "b4"} if sources else set())))
  2128	    salt_digest = sources["b4"]["plan"]["externalInputs"]["sha256"] if "b4" in sources else inputs["sha256"]
  2129	    a.exact_keys('bindings', pin['bindings'], (DVT, COMMITTEE))
  2130	    for code in pin['bindings'].values():
  2131	        a.typed(code, a.HEXDATA)
  2132	        a.need('binding_nonempty', code != '0x', True)
  2133	    a.need('fixed_old_aggregator', pin['oldAggregator'], OLD_AGGREGATOR)
  2134	    validate_nonce_history(pin, inputs)
  2135	    validate_old_activity(pin, d.get('pop'))
  2136	    required(d, ('verifier', 'anni', 'dvt', 'committee', 'sampled', 'depositTargetWei'), 'manifest_external_complete')
  2137	    base_pin = {key: copy.deepcopy(pin[key]) for key in u.PIN_KEYS}
  2138	    base_pin['senders'].pop('anni', None)
  2139	    u.validate_pin(base_pin)
  2140	    a.need('pin_pending_token_eta', pin['pendingAPNTsTokenEta'], PENDING_TOKEN_ETA)
  2141	    drain_gate(pin)
  2142	    a.need('sampled_inventory', set(d['sampled']) <= set(pin['pauseList']), True)
  2143	    a.need('drain_inventory', set(pin['operatorBalances']), set(pin['pauseList']))
  2144	    a.need('anni_binding', pin['senders']['anni']['address'], d['anni'])
  2145	    a.need('sender_labels', set(pin['senders']), {'deployer', 'anni', 'approver', 'executor'})
  2146	    a.need('sender_unique', len({x['address'] for x in pin['senders'].values()}), 4)
  2147	    for label, sender in pin['senders'].items():
  2148	        a.exact_keys('sender_fields_'+label, sender, ('address', 'nonce', 'balance'))
  2149	        a.pin_integer(sender['nonce'], 'nonce_'+label)
  2150	        a.pin_integer(sender['balance'], 'balance_'+label)
  2151	    for field in ('stake', 'stakeDelay', 'entryPointDeposit', 'withdrawTime'):
  2152	        a.pin_integer(pin[field], field)
  2153	    a.need('stake_not_unlocking', pin['withdrawTime'], 0)
  2154	    for label, who in a.SENDERS.items():
  2155	        a.need('sender_'+label, pin['senders'][label]['address'], who)
  2156	    arts = copy.deepcopy(rebuild())
  2157	    isolation.validate_callsite_source(pin['oldOwnerIsolation'], arts['commit'])
  2158	    a.need('old_owner_control_cases_binding',pin.get('isolationControlCases',{}),d.get('isolationControlCases',{}))
  2159	    isolation.validate_deployed(pin.get('oldOwnerIsolation'), pin, d.get('deployedSources'), pending=d.get('pendingDsrRuling'))
  2160	    validate_old_timelock(pin)
  2161	    release_gate(inputs, arts, pin)
  2162	    counters = {label: x['nonce'] for label, x in pin['senders'].items()}
  2163	    sender_of = {label: x['address'] for label, x in pin['senders'].items()}
  2164	    if stage in PC_STAGES:
  2165	        counters['owner'] = pin['phaseC']['owner']['nonce']
  2166	        sender_of['owner'] = pin['phaseC']['owner']['address']
  2167	    phase_c = {}
  2168	    steps, addresses, runtimes = [], {}, {}
  2169	    safe_n = pin['safeNonce']
  2170	    operations = {}
  2171	    def finish():
  2172	        plan = dict(schema='3.1', route='manifest-A-B-D', stage=stage, final=stage == 'full', stageSources=sources,
  2173	            pin=copy.deepcopy(pin), externalInputs=copy.deepcopy(inputs), rebuild=arts,
  2174	            timelockRuntimeIdentity=copy.deepcopy(u.build()['contracts']['TimelockController']['runtime']),

  2176	            addresses=addresses, implementation=impl, extension=extension, operations=operations,
  2177	            steps=steps, controls=CONTROLS+(PC_CONTROLS if stage in PC_STAGES else []),
  2178	            expectedDomainSeparator=domain_separator(agg),
  2179	            b4Disclosure='B4 always consumes its reserved deployer nonce. If already applied, one empty, zero-value self-transfer costs gas only.',
  2180	            gasPolicy=copy.deepcopy(u.GAS_POLICY), gasParams=list(u.GAS),

  2187	        plan['prefixDigest'] = prefix_digest(plan)

  2189	        for name, source in sources.items():
  2190	            rebuilt = build_plan(prefix_pin, source['plan']['externalInputs'], name,
  2191	                                 {'a7': sources['a7']} if name == 'b4' else None)

  2193	            verify_prefix(plan, source['plan'], name)
  2194	            a.need('source_readback_prefix', source['readback']['prefixDigest'], source['plan']['prefixDigest'])
  2195	            a.need('source_domain', source['readback']['domainSeparator'], plan['expectedDomainSeparator'])

  2197	    def add(sid, target, data, checks, sender='deployer', value=0, **extra):
  2198	        a.need('readback_required', bool(checks), True)
  2199	        tx = dict(to=target, data=data, value=hex(value), nonce=hex(counters[sender]),
  2200	            chainId=hex(a.CHAIN), maxFeePerGas=hex(a.MAX_FEE), maxPriorityFeePerGas=hex(a.PRIORITY),
  2201	            **{'from': sender_of[sender]})
  2202	        counters[sender] += 1
  2203	        steps.append(dict(id=sid, sender=sender, tx=tx, readbacks=checks, safeNonce=safe_n, **extra))
  2204	        return steps[-1]
  2205	    def deploy(sid, name, signature='c()', args=(), values=None):
  2206	        target = a.create_address(u.DEPLOYER, counters['deployer'])

  2213	        add(sid, None, art['bytecode']+a.cast('abi-encode', signature, *args)[2:],
  2214	            [dict(kind='code', target=target, expected=rt)], creates=target, constructor=dict(signature=signature,bytecode=art['bytecode']))

  2219	            steps[-1]['readbacks'].append(address_pred(target, 'owner()', u.DEPLOYER))
  2220	        return target
  2221	    def call(sid, target, sig, args, checks, **kw):
  2222	        return add(sid, target, a.calldata(sig, *args), checks, **kw)

  2436	    ticket = ws['endUserStake']+ws['endUserTicket']
  2437	    if not ws['accountMember']:
  2438	        c_call('C6', gtoken, 'approve(address,uint256)', (staking, ticket),
  2439	            [word_pred(gtoken, 'allowance(address,address)', ticket, D, staking)])
  2440	        c_call('C7', u.REG, 'safeMintForRole(bytes32,address,bytes)',
  2441	            (role_id('ENDUSER'), S, a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(address,uint256)', D, ws['endUserStake'])),
  2442	            [word_pred(u.REG, 'hasRole(bytes32,address)', 1, role_id('ENDUSER'), S),
  2443	             word_pred(mysbt, 'verifyCommunityMembership(address,address)', 1, S, D),
  2444	             word_pred(u.SP, 'sbtHolders(address)', 1, S), word_pred(u.SP, 'isEligibleForSponsorship(address)', 1, S),
  2445	             word_pred(u.REG, 'getRoleStake(bytes32,address)', ws['endUserStake'], role_id('ENDUSER'), S),
  2446	             word_pred(gtoken, 'allowance(address,address)', 0, D, staking),
  2447	             word_pred(gtoken, 'balanceOf(address)', p['endUser']['deployerGToken']-ticket, D)])
  2448	    c_call('C8', X, 'mint(address,uint256)', (S, ws['userTarget']),
  2449	        [word_pred(X, 'balanceOf(address)', ws['userTarget'], S), word_pred(X, 'totalSupply()', ws['userTarget']),
  2450	         word_pred(X, 'debts(address)', 0, S), word_pred(X, 'creditPolicy()', 0), word_pred(X, 'pendingPolicyEta()', 0)])
  2451	    if ws['ownerTopUp']:
  2452	        c_add('C20', O, '0x', [dict(kind='balance', target=O, expected=p['owner']['balance']+ws['ownerTopUp'])],
  2453	              value=ws['ownerTopUp'])
  2454	    owner_steps = [('C9.allowance', 'setAutoAllowance(address,uint256)', (u.SP, PC_CAP_L)),
  2455	                   ('C9.total', 'setUserTotalCap(uint256)', (PC_CAP_L,)), ('C9.mode', 'setRenewalMode(uint8)', (0,))]
  2456	    checks = []
  2457	    for sid, sig, args in owner_steps:
  2458	        checks = checks + {'C9.allowance': [predicate(X, 'autoAllowance(address,address)', (S, u.SP), [PC_CAP_L, 0])],
  2459	                           'C9.total': [predicate(X, 'userTotal(address)', (S,), [PC_CAP_L, 0])],
  2460	                           'C9.mode': [word_pred(X, 'renewalMode(address)', 0, S), word_pred(X, 'autoRenewUsed(address)', 0, S),
  2461	                                       word_pred(X, 'creditPolicy()', 0)]}[sid]
  2462	        c_add(sid, S, account_exec(X, a.calldata(sig, *args)), list(checks), sender='owner',
  2463	              gasBound=ws['ownerPreparation'][sid])
  2464	    f1 = lambda target: [address_pred(target, 'MINTER()', D), word_pred(target, 'decimals()', 18), word_pred(target, 'totalSupply()', 0)]
  2465	    task = c_deploy('C10', 'T', base['F1TestToken'], 'c()', (), f1)
  2466	    transfer = a.calldata('transfer(address,uint256)', DEAD, PC_TASK_UNIT)
  2467	    phase_c['taskCallData'] = account_exec(task, transfer)
  2468	    phase_c['callDataHash'] = a.keccak(phase_c['taskCallData'])
  2469	    for sid, who, amount in (('C11.S', S, PC_TASK_SENDER), ('C11.R', DEAD, PC_TASK_UNIT)):
  2470	        c_call(sid, task, 'mint(address,uint256)', (who, amount), [word_pred(task, 'balanceOf(address)', amount, who)])
  2471	    vpm = c_deploy('C12', 'VerifyingPaymaster', base['VerifyingPaymaster'], VPM_CONSTRUCTOR, (ep, c['verifyingSigner']),
  2472	        lambda t: [address_pred(t, 'owner()', D), address_pred(t, 'entryPoint()', ep),
  2473	                   address_pred(t, 'verifyingSigner()', c['verifyingSigner'])])
  2474	    fee = c_deploy('C13.token', 'Q', base['F1TestToken'], 'c()', (), f1)
  2475	    oracle = c_deploy('C13.oracle', 'oracle', base['F1Oracle'], 'c()', (),
  2476	        lambda t: [word_pred(t, 'decimals()', 8), word_pred(t, 'latestRoundData()', 10**8, index=1)])
  2477	    config = (PC_TPM_MARKUP, 0, PC_TPM_REFUND, PC_TPM_AGE)
  2478	    tpm = c_deploy('C14', 'TokenPaymaster', base['TokenPaymaster'], TPM_CONSTRUCTOR,
  2479	        (fee, ep, DEAD, DEAD, '(%d,%d,%d,%d)' % config,
  2480	         '(%d,%d,%s,%s,true,false,false,%d)' % (PC_TPM_AGE, PC_TPM_AGE, oracle, a.ZERO, PC_TPM_THRESHOLD),
  2481	         '(1,3000,5)', D),
  2482	        lambda t: [address_pred(t, 'owner()', D), address_pred(t, 'entryPoint()', ep), address_pred(t, 'token()', fee),
  2483	                   address_pred(t, 'wrappedNative()', DEAD), address_pred(t, 'uniswap()', DEAD),
  2484	                   predicate(t, 'tokenPaymasterConfig()', (), list(config))])
  2485	    c_call('C15', tpm, 'updateCachedPrice(bool)', ('true',),
  2486	        [word_pred(tpm, 'cachedPrice()', PC_TPM_MARKUP), dict(kind='eta', target=tpm, signature='cachedPriceTimestamp()', delay=0)])
  2487	    targets = dict(SP=u.SP, VPM=vpm, TPM=tpm)
  2488	    for arm in ('VPM', 'TPM'):
  2489	        c_call('C16.'+arm.lower(), targets[arm], 'addStake(uint32)', (DAY,),
  2490	            [predicate(ep, 'getDepositInfo(address)', (targets[arm],), [0, 1, PC_STAKE, DAY, 0])], value=PC_STAKE)
  2491	    stakes = dict(SP=pin['stake']+stake, VPM=PC_STAKE, TPM=PC_STAKE)
  2492	    for arm in ARMS:
  2493	        top = ws['entryPointTopUps'][arm]
  2494	        if top:
  2495	            c_call('C17.'+arm.lower(), ep, 'depositTo(address)', (targets[arm],),
  2496	                [word_pred(ep, 'balanceOf(address)', ws['entryPointBefore'][arm]+top, targets[arm]),
  2497	                 word_pred(ep, 'getDepositInfo(address)', stakes[arm], targets[arm], index=2)], value=top)
  2498	    c_call('C18.mint', fee, 'mint(address,uint256)', (S, ws['feeTokenTarget']),
  2499	        [word_pred(fee, 'balanceOf(address)', ws['feeTokenTarget'], S)])
  2500	    c_add('C18.approve', S, account_exec(fee, a.calldata('approve(address,uint256)', tpm, ws['feeTokenTarget'])),
  2501	        [word_pred(fee, 'allowance(address,address)', ws['feeTokenTarget'], S, tpm)], sender='owner',
  2502	        gasBound=ws['ownerPreparation']['C18.approve'])
  2503	    if ws['bundlerTopUp']:
  2504	        c_add('C19', c['bundler'], '0x', [dict(kind='balance', target=c['bundler'],
  2505	              expected=p['bundler']['balance']+ws['bundlerTopUp'])], value=ws['bundlerTopUp'])
  2506	    phase_c['addresses'].update(T=task, Q=fee, oracle=oracle, VPM=vpm, TPM=tpm)
  2507	    phase_c['steps'] = [s['id'] for s in steps if s.get('phase') == 'C']
  2508	    values = sum(a.qty(s['tx']['value']) for s in steps if s['sender'] == 'deployer')
  2509	    # Values alone (stakes, deposits, B/O funding) must fit D before gas is even measured.
  2510	    report = dict(balanceWei=pin['senders']['deployer']['balance'], requiredWei=values,
  2511	                  shortfallWei=positive(values-pin['senders']['deployer']['balance']))
  2512	    a.need('budget_deployer_values', report, dict(report, shortfallWei=0))
  2513	    phase_c['deployerValueWei'] = values
  2514	    if stage == 'c':
  2515	        return finish()
  2516	    call('D.registry.transfer', u.REG, 'transferOwnership(address)', (u.TL,),
  2517	         registry_ownership(u.DEPLOYER, u.TL))
  2518	    op = dict(batch=False, targets=[u.REG], data=[a.calldata('acceptOwnership()')],
  2519	              predecessor=operations['activation']['id'],
  2520	              salt=a.keccak('manifest-v3.1/'+pin['blockHash']+'/'+inputs['sha256']+'/registry_accept'))
  2521	    op['id'] = u.operation_hash(op['targets'], op['data'], op['predecessor'], op['salt'], False)
  2522	    operations['registry_accept'] = op
  2523	    for verb in ('schedule', 'execute'):
  2524	        inner = u.op_call(op, verb)
  2525	        h = safe_hash(inner, safe_n)
  2526	        sid = 'D.registry_'+verb
  2527	        add(sid+'_approve', u.SAFE, a.calldata('approveHash(bytes32)', h),
  2528	            [word_pred(u.SAFE, 'approvedHashes(address,bytes32)', 1, a.APPROVER, h)],
  2529	            sender='approver', safeHash=h, inner=inner, operation='registry_accept')
  2530	        checks = [word_pred(u.TL, 'getOperationState(bytes32)', 1 if verb == 'schedule' else 3, op['id'])]
  2531	        if verb == 'schedule':
  2532	            checks.append(dict(kind='eta', target=u.TL, signature='getTimestamp(bytes32)', args=[op['id']], delay=u.DELAY))
  2533	        else:
  2534	            checks += registry_ownership(u.TL, a.ZERO)
  2535	        add(sid+'_exec', u.SAFE, safe_payload(inner), checks, sender='executor',
  2536	            safeHash=h, inner=inner, operation='registry_accept')
  2537	        safe_n += 1
  2538	    for name, target, owner in handover_contracts(addresses)[1:]:
  2539	        if name in ('aggregator', 'old_aggregator'):
  2540	            call('D.'+name+'.admin', target, 'setSlashPolicyAdmin(address)', (u.SAFE,),
  2541	                 [address_pred(target, 'slashPolicyAdmin()', u.SAFE)])
  2542	        call('D.'+name+'.transfer', target, 'transferOwnership(address)', (owner,),
  2543	             [address_pred(target, 'owner()', owner)])
  2544	    return finish()
  2545
