  2547	def registry_ownership(owner, pending):
  2548	    return [address_pred(u.REG, 'owner()', owner), address_pred(u.REG, 'pendingOwner()', pending),
  2549	            dict(kind='storage', target=u.REG, slot=PENDING_OWNER_SLOT, expected=int(pending, 16))]
  2550	
  2551	
  2552	def handover_contracts(addresses):
  2553	    return [('registry', u.REG, u.TL), ('protocol', addresses['AOAProtocolRegistry'], u.TL),
  2554	            ('factory', addresses['xPNTsFactoryV2'], u.TL),
  2555	            ('aggregator', addresses['BLSAggregator'], u.SAFE), ('dvt', DVT, u.SAFE),
  2556	            ('committee', COMMITTEE, u.SAFE), ('old_aggregator', OLD_AGGREGATOR, u.SAFE)]
  2557	
  2558	
  2559	def handover_gate(rpc, plan, step, context):
  2560	    sid = step['id']
  2561	    if not sid.startswith('D.'):
  2562	        return
  2563	    passed = context.get('passed', set())
  2564	    required_steps = {'activation_execute_exec', 'B4'}
  2565	    if sid.startswith(('D.aggregator.', 'D.old_aggregator.')):
  2566	        required_steps.update(('A10.0', 'A10.1', 'A10.2', 'A11.min', 'A11.default'))
  2567	    if 'phaseC' in plan:
  2568	        # Phase C funding precedes Phase D (§3 ordering, §4 critical path), and every
  2569	        # funding target, including omitted rows, still holds before each handover step.
  2570	        required_steps.update(plan['phaseC']['steps'])
  2571	        if required_steps <= passed:
  2572	            context.setdefault('handoverFunding', []).append(funding_gate(rpc, plan))
  2573	    if sid.startswith('D.registry_'):
  2574	        required_steps.add('D.registry.transfer')
  2575	        for pred in registry_ownership(u.DEPLOYER, u.TL):
  2576	            check_predicate(rpc, pred)
  2577	    if sid == 'D.committee.transfer':
  2578	        required_steps.add('A8.committee')
  2579	        # Require the transferOwnership dispatch bytes and no acceptOwnership or
  2580	        # pendingOwner selector bytes in the pinned runtime. The post-transfer
  2581	        # owner()==SAFE read-back remains the authoritative ownership check.
  2582	        runtime = plan['pin']['bindings'][COMMITTEE].lower()
  2583	        a.need('committee_transfer_selector', '63f2fde38b' in runtime, True)
  2584	        a.need('committee_no_accept_ownership', '79ba5097' in runtime, False)
  2585	        a.need('committee_no_pending_owner', 'e30c3978' in runtime, False)
  2586	    if sid in ('D.aggregator.transfer', 'D.old_aggregator.transfer'):
  2587	        required_steps.add(sid.replace('.transfer', '.admin'))
  2588	        check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(step['tx']['to'], 'slashPolicyAdmin()', u.SAFE))
  2589	    a.need('handover_dependencies', required_steps <= passed, True)
  2590	
  2591	
  2592	def handover_trace(rpc, plan, step, receipt):
  2593	    # The existing SP trace helper is intentionally SP-specific.
  2594	    trace = rpc('debug_traceTransaction', [receipt['transactionHash'], {'tracer':'callTracer'}])
  2595	    a.need('handover_trace_object', isinstance(trace, dict), True)
  2596	    for key, expected in (('from', step['tx']['from']), ('to', u.SAFE), ('input', step['tx']['data'])):
  2597	        a.need('handover_trace_'+key, trace.get(key, '').lower(), expected.lower())
  2598	    a.need('handover_trace_success', bool(trace.get('error')), False)
  2599	    nodes = list(a.trace_nodes(trace))
  2600	    if step['id'].endswith('_exec'):
  2601	        expected = [(u.SAFE, u.TL, step['inner'])]
  2602	        if 'execute' in step['id']:
  2603	            expected.append((u.TL, u.REG, a.calldata('acceptOwnership()')))
  2604	        for sender, target, data in expected:
  2605	            matches = [n for n in nodes if n.get('to', '').lower() == target and n.get('input') == data]
  2606	            a.need('handover_trace_call_count', len(matches), 1)
  2607	            a.need('handover_trace_caller', matches[0].get('from', '').lower(), sender)
  2608	            a.need('handover_trace_call_success', bool(matches[0].get('error')), False)
  2609	
  2610	
  2611	def handover_controls(rpc, plan):
  2612	    """Exercise actual owner checks; all fixture writes/nonces are snapshot-isolated."""
  2613	    a.need('handover_controls_local', getattr(rpc, 'local', False), True)
  2614	    results = []
  2615	    for name, target, owner in handover_contracts(plan['addresses']):
  2616	        snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  2617	        try:
  2618	            check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'owner()', owner))
  2619	            data = a.calldata('transferOwnership(address)', owner)
  2620	            for sender in ((u.DEPLOYER, u.SAFE) if owner == u.TL else (u.DEPLOYER,)):
  2621	                tx = u.fixture_tx(rpc, sender, target, data)
  2622	                results.append(a.negative(rpc, tx, a.calldata('OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address)', sender),
  2623	                                          'handover_'+name+'_'+sender))
  2624	            receipt = u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, owner, target, data))
  2625	            a.need('handover_positive_status', a.qty(receipt['status']), 1)
  2626	            check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'owner()', owner))
  2627	            if name == 'registry':
  2628	                for pred in registry_ownership(owner, owner):
  2629	                    check_predicate(rpc, pred)
  2630	            if name in ('aggregator', 'old_aggregator'):
  2631	                data = a.calldata('setSlashPolicyAdmin(address)', u.SAFE)
  2632	                results.append(a.negative(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.DEPLOYER, target, data),
  2633	                    a.calldata('OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address)', u.DEPLOYER), 'handover_'+name+'_admin'))
  2634	                receipt = u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.SAFE, target, data))
  2635	                a.need('handover_admin_status', a.qty(receipt['status']), 1)
  2636	                check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'slashPolicyAdmin()', u.SAFE))
  2637	            results.append(dict(contract=name, positiveReceipt=receipt, snapshot=snap))
  2638	        finally:
  2639	            a.need('handover_control_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  2640	    # Probe setters without retaining policy, balance or nonce changes.
  2641	    probes = [('sp', u.SP, u.TL, 'setProtocolFee(uint256)', 'protocolFeeBPS()', (),
  2642	               (u.DEPLOYER, u.SAFE), 'OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address)')]
  2643	    for name, target in (('aggregator', plan['addresses']['BLSAggregator']),
  2644	                         ('old_aggregator', OLD_AGGREGATOR)):
  2645	        probes += [(name, target, u.SAFE, setter, getter, args, (u.DEPLOYER,),
  2646	                    'NotSlashPolicyAdmin(address)') for setter, getter, args in (
  2647	                        ('setSlashThreshold(uint8,uint8)', 'slashThresholds(uint8)', (0,)),
  2648	                        ('setGuardianSlashBps(uint16)', 'guardianSlashBps()', ()))]
  2649	    for name, target, admin, setter, getter, args, denied, error in probes:
  2650	        snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  2651	        try:
  2652	            authority = 'owner()' if name == 'sp' else 'slashPolicyAdmin()'
  2653	            check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, authority, admin))
  2654	            current = a.word(rpc, target, getter, *args)
  2655	            data = a.calldata(setter, *args, current)
  2656	            label = 'handover_'+name+'_'+setter.split('(')[0]
  2657	            for sender in denied:
  2658	                results.append(a.negative(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, sender, target, data),
  2659	                    a.calldata(error, sender), label+'_'+sender))
  2660	            receipt = u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, admin, target, data))
  2661	            a.need(label+'_status', a.qty(receipt['status']), 1)
  2662	            check_predicate(rpc, word_pred(target, getter, current, *args))
  2663	            results.append(dict(contract=name, setter=setter, currentValue=current,
  2664	                                positiveReceipt=receipt, snapshot=snap))
  2665	        finally:
  2666	            a.need('handover_setter_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  2667	        check_predicate(rpc, word_pred(target, getter, current, *args))
  2668	    check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(u.SP, 'owner()', u.TL))
  2669	    for name, target, owner in handover_contracts(plan['addresses']):
  2670	        check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'owner()', owner))
  2671	        if name in ('aggregator', 'old_aggregator'):
  2672	            check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'slashPolicyAdmin()', u.SAFE))
  2673	    for pred in registry_ownership(u.TL, a.ZERO):
  2674	        check_predicate(rpc, pred)
  2675	    return dict(control='owner_handover', assertions=results, restored=True)
  2676	
  2677	
  2678	def activation_eta_gate(plan, schedule_timestamp, execute_timestamp):
  2679	    a.need('6c_schedule_floor', schedule_timestamp >= plan['activationScheduleNotBefore'], True)
  2680	    a.need('6d_timelock_eta', execute_timestamp >= schedule_timestamp+u.DELAY, True)
  2681	    a.need('6d_token_eta', execute_timestamp >= plan['pin']['pendingAPNTsTokenEta'], True)
  2682	
  2683	
  2684	def b2_gate(rpc, plan, upgrade_block):
  2685	    a.need('B2_capped_buffer', a.word(rpc, plan['pin']['capped'], 'balanceOf(address)', u.SP), BUFFER)
  2686	    observed = balances(rpc, plan['pin']['pauseList'])
  2687	    a.need('B2_balances', observed['totalTrackedBalance'], observed['protocolRevenue'])
  2688	    a.need('B2_revenue', observed['protocolRevenue'] <= BUFFER, True)
  2689	    a.need('B2_operator_balances', sum(observed['operatorBalances'].values()), 0)
  2690	    for who in plan['pin']['pauseList']:
  2691	        a.need('B2_pending_slash_'+who, a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'isSlashPending(address)', who), 0)
  2692	    # 5.4.2 has no reservation map. B1 creates 5.5's first possible reservation
  2693	    # epoch, atomically paused. Require a continuous pause and no intervening
  2694	    # implementation change; a current pause bit alone cannot prove this.
  2695	    a.need('B2_global_pause', a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'paused()'), 1)
  2696	    end = a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['number']
  2697	    pauses = u.scan(rpc, u.SP, int(upgrade_block, 16), end, [a.keccak('GlobalPauseSet(address,bool)')])
  2698	    a.need('B2_pause_history_present', bool(pauses), True)
  2699	    a.need('B2_no_unpause_since_upgrade', all(u.uints(x['data']) == [1] for x in pauses), True)
  2700	    upgrades = u.scan(rpc, u.SP, int(upgrade_block, 16), end, [a.keccak('Upgraded(address)')])
  2701	    a.need('B2_single_upgrade', len(upgrades), 1)
  2702	    a.need('B2_upgrade_identity', u.addr_word(int(upgrades[0]['topics'][1], 16)), plan['implementation'])
  2703	    observed['inflightProof'] = dict(upgradeBlock=upgrade_block, continuousGlobalPause=True, upgrades=upgrades, pauses=pauses)
  2704	    return observed
  2705	
  2706	
  2707	def b4_needed(rpc, plan):
  2708	    return a.address(rpc, plan['addresses']['BLSAggregator'], 'fraudProofVerifier()') != plan['addresses']['OverIssueFraudProofVerifier']
  2709

  2711	def b4_post_state(rpc, plan, variant, receipt):
  2712	    a.need('B4_variant', variant in ('apply', 'placeholder'), True)
  2713	    status = a.qty(receipt['status'])
  2714	    a.need('B4_status', status == 1 or (variant == 'apply' and status == 0), True)
  2715	    step = next(s for s in plan['steps'] if s['id'] == 'B4')
  2716	    return [check_predicate(rpc, pred) for pred in step['readbacks']]
  2717

  2719	def select_b4(rpc, plan, step):
  2720	    name = 'apply' if b4_needed(rpc, plan) else 'placeholder'
  2721	    return name, copy.deepcopy(step['variants'][name])

  2724	def execute_b4(remote, rpc, plan, step, context, overrides, calibrate, out=None):
  2725	    timestamp = a.qty(a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['timestamp'])+1
  2888	    plan['budgetsWei'] = result

  2891	def validate_plan(plan, inputs, frozen=False, *, expected=None):
  2892	    a.need('external_inputs_match', plan['externalInputs'], inputs)
  2893	    if expected is None:
  2894	        expected = build_plan(plan['pin'], inputs, plan.get('stage', 'full'), plan.get('stageSources'))
  2895	    a.exact_keys('manifest_keys', plan, set(expected) | ({'budgetsWei'} if frozen else set()))
  2896	    for key in set(expected)-{'steps'}:
  2897	        a.need('manifest_'+key, plan[key], expected[key])
  2898	    a.need('manifest_length', len(plan['steps']), len(expected['steps']))
  2899	    for actual, derived in zip(plan['steps'], expected['steps']):
  2900	        extra = u.MEASURED_KEYS | {'skipped', 'gasEvidence'} if frozen else set()
  2901	        if frozen and 'variants' in derived:
  2902	            extra |= {'variantMeasurements'}
  2903	        if frozen:
  2904	            u.validate_gas_evidence(plan, actual)
  2905	            owner_gas_gate(dict(actual, gasBound=derived['gasBound'])) if 'gasBound' in derived else None
  2906	        if frozen:
  2907	            a.need('no_skipped_nonce', actual.get('skipped'), False)
  2908	        a.exact_keys('manifest_step', actual, set(derived) | extra)
  2909	        for key in set(derived)-{'tx', 'variants'}:
  2910	            a.need('manifest_step_'+key, actual[key], derived[key])
  2911	        a.exact_keys('manifest_tx', actual['tx'], set(derived['tx']) | ({'gas'} if frozen and not actual.get('skipped') else set()))
  2912	        for key, value in derived['tx'].items():
  2913	            a.need('manifest_tx_'+key, actual['tx'][key], value)
  2914	        if 'variants' in derived:
  2915	            a.exact_keys('B4_variants', actual['variants'], ('apply', 'placeholder'))
  2916	            for name, tx in derived['variants'].items():
  2917	                variant = actual['variants'][name]
  2918	                a.exact_keys('B4_variant_fields', variant, set(tx) | ({'gas'} if frozen else set()))
  2919	                for key, value in tx.items():
  2920	                    a.need('B4_variant_'+key, variant[key], value)
  2921	            a.need('B4_apply_binding', actual['variants']['apply'], actual['tx'])
  2922	            if frozen:
  2923	                a.need('B4_placeholder_intrinsic', a.qty(actual['variants']['placeholder']['gas']) >= 21000, True)
  2924	                a.tx_quantities(actual['variants']['placeholder'])
  2925	                with u.gas_step(actual):
  2926	                    a.exact_keys('B4_variant_measurements', actual['variantMeasurements'], ('placeholder',))
  2927	                placeholder = dict(id=actual['id'], **actual['variantMeasurements']['placeholder'])
  2928	                placeholder['tx'] = actual['variants']['placeholder']
  2929	                u.validate_gas_evidence(plan, placeholder)
  2930	                with u.gas_step(actual):
  2931	                    a.exact_keys('B4_placeholder_measurements', actual['variantMeasurements']['placeholder'],
  2932	                                 u.MEASURED_KEYS | {'gasEvidence'})
  2933	    if frozen:
  2934	        checked = copy.deepcopy(plan)
  2935	        budget(checked)
  2936	        a.need('manifest_budget', checked['budgetsWei'], plan['budgetsWei'])
  2937	
  2938	    return copy.deepcopy(expected)
  2939	
  2940	
  2941	def owner_gas_gate(step):
  2942	    # O's funding (OD) was computed from this reviewed bound; a larger limit is STOP.
  2943	    if 'gasBound' in step:
  2944	        a.need('C_owner_gas_bound', a.qty(step['tx']['gas']) <= step['gasBound'], True)
  2945	
  2946	
  2947	def price_gate(rpc, block='latest'):
  2948	    price = u.uints(a.call(rpc, u.SP, 'cachedPrice()', block=block))
  2949	    a.need('C_price_decimals', price[3], PC_PRICE_DECIMALS)
  2950	    a.need('C_price_bound', 0 < price[0] <= PC_PRICE_CAP, True)
  2951	    return price
  2952

  2954	def phase_c_state(rpc, plan, context):
  2955	    """§3 C1 preconditions held for every Phase C transaction (B3/B4 complete)."""
  2956	    a.need('C_after_activation', {'activation_execute_exec', 'B4'} <= context.get('passed', set())
  2957	           and bool(context.get('activated')), True)
  2958	    a.need('C_after_token_eta', a.qty(a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['timestamp']) >= plan['pin']['pendingAPNTsTokenEta'], True)
  2959	    a.need('C_token_swapped', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'APNTS_TOKEN()'), plan['pin']['capped'])
  2960	    gas = u.uints(a.call(rpc, u.SP, 'gasParams()'))
  2961	    a.need('C_gas_tuple', [gas[:4], gas[4:]], [u.GAS, [0]*(len(gas)-4)])
  2962	    a.need('C_global_unpaused', a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'paused()'), 0)
  2963	    sampled = set(plan['externalInputs']['data']['sampled'])
  2964	    for who in plan['pin']['pauseList']:
  2965	        a.need('C_sampled_unpaused' if who in sampled else 'C_unsampled_paused',
  2966	               u.uints(a.call(rpc, u.SP, 'operators(address)', who))[OP['isPaused']], int(who not in sampled))
  2967	    price_gate(rpc)
  2968	    pc = plan['phaseC']
  2969	    a.need('C_pricing_unchanged', [a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'aPNTsPriceUSD()'), a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'protocolFeeBPS()')],
  2970	           [pc['aPNTsPriceUSD'], pc['protocolFeeBPS']])
  2971	
  2972	
  2973	def phase_c_pre_send(rpc, plan, step, context):
  2974	    phase_c_state(rpc, plan, context)
  2975	    pc, pin, sid = plan['phaseC'], plan['pin'], step['id']
  3218	def assert_manifest_frozen(rpc, pin, registry_handed_over=False):
  3219	    # Keep the shared role/config checks, but follow the completed owner transition.
  3220	    u.assert_frozen(rpc, dict(pin, registryOwner=u.TL if registry_handed_over else pin['registryOwner']))
  3221

  3223	def pre_send(rpc, plan, step, context, measured=False):
  3224	    a.need('manifest_local_only', getattr(rpc, 'local', False), True)
  3225	    block = a.block_data(rpc,'latest')['number']
  3226	    isolation.registry_callsite_targets(lambda target, sig: a.address(rpc, target, sig, block), {},
  3227	                                       plan['pin']['oldOwnerIsolation']['callsiteTargetSource']['expected'])
  3228	    # Before every send, not only at closing: a post-pin allowance, role or authority of the orphaned timelock.
  3229	    coverage = discovery_coverage(plan, context.get('passed', set()))
  3230	    isolation.never_granted_recheck(rpc, coverage, block)
  3231	    delta = post_pin_delta(rpc, coverage, block)
  3232	    context['oldTimelockDelta'] = delta
  3233	    discovery_old_authority(rpc, coverage, block)
  3234	    old_timelock_holds_nothing(rpc, coverage, block, delta['approvalOwners'])
  3235	    handover_gate(rpc, plan, step, context)
  3236	    if step.get('phase') == 'C':
  3237	        phase_c_pre_send(rpc, plan, step, context)
  3238	    tx = step['tx']
  3239	    a.need('chain', a.qty(rpc('eth_chainId', [])), a.CHAIN)
  3240	    a.need('sender_nonce', a.nonce(rpc, tx['from']), a.qty(tx['nonce']))
  3241	    a.need('pending_nonce', a.nonce(rpc, tx['from'], 'pending'), a.qty(tx['nonce']))
  3242	    a.need('safe_nonce', a.word(rpc, u.SAFE, 'nonce()'), step['safeNonce'])
  3243	    a.need('safe_state', u.safe_state(rpc, 'latest'), plan['pin']['safe'])
  3244	    u.governance(rpc, 'latest', plan['pin']['timelockCreation'], plan['timelockRuntimeIdentity'])
  3245	    for target, expected in plan['pin']['bindings'].items():
  3246	        a.need('bound_runtime', u.code(rpc, target, 'latest'), expected)
  3247	    unchanged_old_activity(rpc, plan, a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['number'])
  3248	    a.need('dvt_activity_unchanged', dvt_activity(rpc, plan['externalInputs']['data']['dvt'],
  3249	        a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['number']), plan['pin']['oldActivity']['dvtProposals'])
  3250	    a.need('registry_owner', a.address(rpc, u.REG, 'owner()'),
  3251	           u.TL if 'D.registry_execute_exec' in context.get('passed', set()) else u.DEPLOYER)
  3252	    a.need('SP_owner', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'owner()'), u.TL if context.get('transferred') else u.DEPLOYER)
  3253	    if context.get('frozen'):
  3254	        assert_manifest_frozen(rpc, plan['pin'], 'D.registry_execute_exec' in context.get('passed', set()))
  3255	    else:
  3256	        a.need('role_config_unchanged', a.call(rpc, u.REG, 'getRoleConfig(bytes32)', u.role()), plan['pin']['roleConfig'])
  3257	    if not context.get('activated'):
  3258	        a.need('pending_token', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'pendingAPNTsToken()'), plan['pin']['capped'])
  3259	        a.need('pending_token_eta', a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'pendingAPNTsTokenEta()'), plan['pin']['pendingAPNTsTokenEta'])
  3260	    if step['id'] == 'A17':
  3261	        u.check_inventory(rpc, plan['pin'])
  3262	        u.require_pauses(rpc, plan['pin'])
  3263	        drain = balances(rpc, plan['pin']['pauseList'])
  3264	        a.need('transfer_drained', drain['totalTrackedBalance'], BUFFER)
  3265	        a.need('transfer_revenue', drain['protocolRevenue'], BUFFER)
  3266	    if step['id'] == 'activation_execute_exec':
  3267	        context['B2'] = b2_gate(rpc, plan, context['upgradeBlock'])
  3268	        eta = a.word(rpc, u.TL, 'getTimestamp(bytes32)', plan['operations']['activation']['id'])
  3269	        activation_eta_gate(plan, context['activationScheduledAt'], eta)
  3270	    if 'safeHash' in step:
  3271	        h = a.call(rpc, u.SAFE, 'getTransactionHash(address,uint256,bytes,uint8,uint256,uint256,uint256,address,address,uint256)',
  3272	            step.get('safeTarget', u.TL), 0, step['inner'], 0, 0, 0, 0, a.ZERO, a.ZERO, step['safeNonce'])
  3273	        a.need('onchain_safe_hash', h, step['safeHash'])
  3274	        if 'operation' in step:
  3275	            u.check_op_hash(rpc, plan['operations'][step['operation']])
  3276	    if measured:
  3277	        u.validate_gas(step)
  3278	        owner_gas_gate(step)
  3279	        probe = {k: v for k, v in a.tx_rpc(tx, gas_cap=u.MAX_CREATE_GAS if tx['to'] is None else a.GAS_CAP).items() if k != 'gas'}
  3280	        a.need('fresh_estimate', a.qty(rpc('eth_estimateGas', [probe, 'latest'])) <= a.qty(tx['gas']), True)
  3281	        a.need('block_gas_limit', a.qty(tx['gas']) <= a.qty(a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['gasLimit']), True)
  3282	    return tx
  3283

  3285	def mint_trace(rpc, step, receipt):
  3286	    trace = rpc('debug_traceTransaction', [receipt['transactionHash'], {'tracer': 'callTracer'}])
  3287	    a.need('mint_trace_top', (trace.get('from', '').lower(), trace.get('to', '').lower(), trace.get('input')),
  3288	           (step['tx']['from'], u.SAFE, step['tx']['data']))
  3289	    a.need('mint_trace_success', bool(trace.get('error')), False)
  3290	    if step['id'].endswith('_exec'):
  3291	        calls = [n for n in a.trace_nodes(trace) if n.get('to', '').lower() == step['safeTarget']]
  3292	        a.need('mint_trace_count', len(calls), 1)
  3293	        a.need('mint_trace_call', (calls[0].get('from', '').lower(), calls[0].get('input'), bool(calls[0].get('error'))),
  3294	               (u.SAFE, step['inner'], False))

  3297	def unbacked_swap_control(rpc, plan, expected=None):
  3298	    """Execute the real 6d operation without B2a on a reverted local snapshot."""
  3299	    snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  3300	    try:
  3301	        a.need('unbacked_initial', a.word(rpc, plan['pin']['capped'], 'balanceOf(address)', u.SP), 0)
  3302	        op = plan['operations']['activation']
  3303	        u.wait_until(rpc, max(plan['activationScheduleNotBefore'],
  3304	                             u.uints(a.call(rpc, u.SP, 'gasParams()'))[8]))
  3305	        u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.SAFE, u.TL, u.op_call(op, 'schedule')))
  3306	        u.wait_until(rpc, a.word(rpc, u.TL, 'getTimestamp(bytes32)', op['id']))
  3307	        # Deliberately bypass pre_send only for the negative control.
  3308	        u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.SAFE, u.TL, u.op_call(op, 'execute')))
  3309	        tracked = a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'totalTrackedBalance()')
  3310	        backing = a.word(rpc, plan['pin']['capped'], 'balanceOf(address)', u.SP)
  3311	        a.need('unbacked_shortfall', tracked-backing, BUFFER)
  3312	        try:
  3313	            b2_gate(rpc, plan, '0x0')
  3314	        except a.Gate as error:
  3315	            a.need('unbacked_refusal_gate', error.record['gate'], 'B2_capped_buffer')
  3316	        else:
  3317	            raise a.Gate('unbacked_build_refusal', 'accepted', 'B2 rejection')
  3318	        expected = expected if expected is not None else build_plan(plan['pin'], plan['externalInputs'])
  3319	        candidate = copy.deepcopy(expected)
  3320	        candidate['steps'] = [s for s in candidate['steps'] if not s['id'].startswith('B2a_')]
  3321	        try:
  3322	            validate_plan(candidate, plan['externalInputs'], expected=expected)
  3323	        except a.Gate as error:
  3324	            a.need('unbacked_plan_refusal_gate', error.record['gate'], 'manifest_length')
  3325	        else:
  3326	            raise a.Gate('unbacked_plan_refusal', 'accepted', 'missing mint rejection')
  3327	        return dict(control='unbacked_swap', tracked=tracked, backing=backing,
  3328	                    shortfall=tracked-backing, buildRefused=True, gate='B2_capped_buffer',
  3329	                    planGate='manifest_length')
  3330	    finally:
  3331	        a.need('unbacked_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  3332	
  3333	
  3334	def early_swap_control(rpc, plan):
  3335	    """Isolate the replacement ETA guard after B1, retaining A15 accounting."""
  3336	    snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  3337	    try:
  3338	        implementation = u.addr_word(int(rpc('eth_getStorageAt', [u.SP, u.IMPL_SLOT, 'latest']), 16))
  3339	        a.need('early_swap_implementation', implementation, plan['implementation'])
  3340	        tracked = a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'totalTrackedBalance()')
  3341	        revenue = a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'protocolRevenue()')
  3342	        a.need('early_swap_accounting', tracked == revenue <= BUFFER, True)
  3343	        token = plan['pin']['capped']
  3344	        a.need('early_swap_token', token != a.ZERO, True)
  3345	        a.need('early_swap_owner', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'owner()'), u.TL)
  3346	        u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.TL, u.SP, a.calldata('setAPNTsToken(address)', token)))
  3347	        a.need('early_swap_pending', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'pendingAPNTsToken()'), token)
  3348	        eta = a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'pendingAPNTsTokenEta()')
  3349	        a.need('early_swap_future_eta', a.qty(a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['timestamp'])+1 < eta, True)
  3350	        tx = u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.TL, u.SP, a.calldata('executeAPNTsTokenChange()'))
  3351	        result = a.negative(rpc, tx, a.calldata('InvalidConfiguration()'), 'early_swap')
  3352	        # The contract shares this error with its accounting guard. Success with
  3353	        # identical state after only advancing time establishes the ETA cause.
  3354	        u.wait_until(rpc, eta)
  3355	        receipt = u.receipt(rpc, tx)
  3356	        a.need('early_swap_mature_token', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'APNTS_TOKEN()'), token)
  3357	        a.need('early_swap_mature_pending', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'pendingAPNTsToken()'), a.ZERO)
  3358	        a.need('early_swap_mature_eta', a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'pendingAPNTsTokenEta()'), 0)
  3359	        return dict(result, eta=eta, tracked=tracked, revenue=revenue, matureReceipt=receipt)
  3360	    finally:
  3361	        a.need('early_swap_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  3362	
  3363	
  3364	def controls_at(rpc, plan, sid, controls, expected=None):
  3365	    agg = plan['addresses']['BLSAggregator']
  3366	    def negative(name, sender, target, sig, args, error, error_args=()):
  3367	        snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  3368	        try:
  3369	            tx = u.fixture_tx(rpc, sender, target, a.calldata(sig, *args))
  3370	            controls.append(a.negative(rpc, tx, a.calldata(error, *error_args), name))
  3371	        finally:
  3372	            a.need('control_fixture_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  3373	    if sid == 'A6.upgrade':
  3374	        negative('registry_nonowner', a.EXECUTOR, u.REG, 'upgradeToAndCall(address,bytes)',
  3375	            (plan['addresses']['Registry'], '0x'), 'OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address)', (a.EXECUTOR,))
  3376	    if sid == 'A10.0':
  3377	        negative('old_reputation', plan['pin']['oldAggregator'], u.REG,
  3378	            'batchUpdateGlobalReputation(uint256,address[],uint256[],uint256,bytes)', (1, '[]', '[]', 1, '0x'), 'UnauthorizedSource()')
  3379	    if sid == 'A13.stake':
  3380	        eta = a.word(rpc, agg, 'pendingFraudProofVerifierReadyAt()')
  3381	        negative('early_verifier', u.DEPLOYER, agg, 'applyFraudProofVerifier()', (), 'VerifierRotationNotReady(uint256)', (eta,))
  3382	    if sid == 'after_upgrade_execute_exec':
  3383	        controls.append(early_swap_control(rpc, plan))
  3384	    if sid == 'upgrade_schedule_approve':
  3385	        # Exercise the new implementation before factory wiring, then restore the
  3386	        # old implementation and every fixture nonce. No mock storage writes.
  3387	        snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  3388	        try:
  3389	            tx = u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.TL, u.SP, a.calldata('upgradeToAndCall(address,bytes)', plan['implementation'], '0x'))
  3390	            u.receipt(rpc, tx)
  3391	            factory_error = 'InvalidConfiguration()' if a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'xpntsFactory()') == a.ZERO else 'InvalidXPNTsToken()'
  3392	            negative('factory_binding', u.DEPLOYER, u.SP, 'configureOperator(address,address)',
  3393	                (plan['addresses']['xPNTsTokenV2'], u.DEPLOYER), factory_error)
  3394	        finally:
  3395	            a.need('factory_control_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  3396	    if sid == 'B2a_approve':
  3397	        controls.append(unbacked_swap_control(rpc, plan, expected))
  3398	    if sid == 'activation_schedule_approve':
  3399	        negative('early_unpause', u.DEPLOYER, u.SP, 'setGlobalPaused(bool)', ('false',), 'Unauthorized()')
  3400	        negative('early_bls', u.TL, u.SP, 'applyBLSAggregator()', (), 'InvalidConfiguration()')
  3401	        snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  3402	        try:
  3403	            # Deposits are legal while paused. A restored operator balance must
  3404	            # stop both the executor gate and the token swap itself.
  3405	            old = a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'APNTS_TOKEN()')
  3406	            for target, sig, args in ((old, 'approve(address,uint256)', (u.SP, 1)), (u.SP, 'deposit(uint256)', (1,))):
  3407	                u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.DEPLOYER, target, a.calldata(sig, *args)))
  3408	            u.wait_until(rpc, plan['pin']['pendingAPNTsTokenEta'])
  3409	            negative('funded_swap', u.TL, u.SP, 'executeAPNTsTokenChange()', (), 'InvalidConfiguration()')
  3410	        finally:
  3411	            a.need('funded_swap_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  3412	    if sid == 'after_B4':
  3413	        negative('repeat_verifier', u.DEPLOYER, agg, 'applyFraudProofVerifier()', (), 'NoPendingVerifierRotation()')
  3414	
  3415	
  3416	def execution_tree_gates(plan):
  3417	    head = subprocess.run(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=u.ROOT,
  3418	                          capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.strip()
  3419	    a.need('execution_head', head, plan['rebuild']['commit'])
  3420	    state = subprocess.run(['git', 'status', '--porcelain', '--untracked-files=all'], cwd=u.ROOT,
  3421	                           capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
  3422	    a.need('execution_clean_tree', state.stdout, '')
  3423	
  3424	
  3425	def run_manifest(remote, rpc, plan, out, calibrate=True):
  3426	    a.need('manifest_local_only', getattr(rpc, 'local', False), True)
  3427	    expected = validate_plan(plan, plan['externalInputs'], frozen=not calibrate)
  3428	    allowlist = execution_reads.reviewed_allowlist()
  3429	    execution_tree_gates(plan)
  3430	    with execution_reads.guard(allowlist):
  3431	        return _run_manifest(remote, rpc, plan, out, calibrate, expected)
  3432	
  3433	
  3434	def _run_manifest(remote, rpc, plan, out, calibrate, expected):
  3435	    for sender in plan['pin']['senders'].values():
  3436	        rpc('anvil_impersonateAccount', [sender['address']])
  3437	    pc = plan.get('phaseC')
  3438	    if pc is not None:
  3439	        rpc('anvil_impersonateAccount', [plan['pin']['phaseC']['owner']['address']])
  3440	    context, records, controls = {'passed': set()}, [], []
  3441	    overrides = copy.deepcopy(getattr(rpc, 'archive_overrides', {}))
  3442	    for step in plan['steps']:
  3443	        a.process_alive(rpc.engine_process)
  3444	        sid = step['id']
  3445	        controls_at(rpc, plan, sid, controls, expected)
  3446	        phase_c_controls(rpc, plan, sid, controls, context)
  3447	        if not calibrate:
  3448	            a.need('frozen_step_not_skipped', step['skipped'], False)
  3449	        if sid == 'activation_schedule_approve':
  3450	            gas_eta = u.uints(a.call(rpc, u.SP, 'gasParams()'))[8]
  3451	            u.wait_until(rpc, max(gas_eta, plan['activationScheduleNotBefore']))
  3452	        if sid.endswith('execute_exec'):
  3453	            u.wait_until(rpc, a.word(rpc, u.TL, 'getTimestamp(bytes32)', plan['operations'][step['operation']]['id']))
  3454	        if sid == 'B4':
  3455	            u.wait_until(rpc, a.word(rpc, plan['addresses']['BLSAggregator'], 'pendingFraudProofVerifierReadyAt()'))
  3456	            controls.extend(b4_controls(rpc, plan, step))
  3457	            record = execute_b4(remote, rpc, plan, step, context, overrides, calibrate, out)
  3458	            records.append(record)
  3459	            u.log(out, record)
  3460	            a.trace_overrides(rpc, record['receipt'], overrides)
  3461	            context['passed'].add(sid)
  3462	            controls_at(rpc, plan, 'after_B4', controls, expected)
  3463	            continue
  3464	        pre_send(rpc, plan, step, context)
  3465	        timestamp = a.qty(a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['timestamp'])+1
  3466	        evidence = u.measure_step(remote, rpc, plan, step, overrides, timestamp, calibrate)
  3467	        pre_send(rpc, plan, step, context, measured=True)
  3468	        evidence['freshGas'] = u.fresh_gas(remote, step, overrides, timestamp, pin_block=plan['pin']['block'])
  3469	        rpc('evm_setNextBlockTimestamp', [timestamp])
  3470	        r = u.receipt(rpc, step['tx'])
  3471	        a.need('manifest_receipt_gas', a.qty(r['gasUsed']), step['forkGasUsed'])
  3472	        a.need('manifest_timestamp', a.qty(a.block_data(rpc, r['blockNumber'])['timestamp']), timestamp)
  3473	        if step['rrRequired']:
  3474	            a.need('manifest_rr_witness', u.reservoir_witness(rpc, step['tx'], r), step['rrWitness'])
  3475	        a.mined_identity(rpc('eth_getTransactionByHash', [r['transactionHash']]), step['tx'], sid)
  3476	        a.need('post_nonce', a.nonce(rpc, step['tx']['from']), a.qty(step['tx']['nonce'])+1)
  3477	        a.need('post_safe_nonce', a.word(rpc, u.SAFE, 'nonce()'), step['safeNonce']+int(sid.endswith('_exec')))
  3478	        observed = []
  3479	        for pred in step['readbacks']:
  3480	            if pred['kind'] in ('upgrade', 'activation'):
  3481	                observed.append(batch_readback(rpc, plan, pred['kind'] == 'activation', timestamp))
  3482	            else:
  3483	                observed.append(check_predicate(rpc, pred, timestamp))
  3484	        if sid == 'A17':
  3485	            context['transferred'] = True
  3486	        if sid == 'activation_execute_exec':
  3487	            context['activated'] = True
  3488	            context['after6d'] = balances(rpc, plan['pin']['pauseList'])
  3489	            context['after6d']['cappedBalance'] = a.word(rpc, plan['pin']['capped'], 'balanceOf(address)', u.SP)
  3490	            observed6d = context['after6d']
  3491	            a.need('after_6d_accounting', [observed6d['totalTrackedBalance'], observed6d['protocolRevenue'],
  3492	                   observed6d['cappedBalance'], sum(observed6d['operatorBalances'].values())], [BUFFER, BUFFER, BUFFER, 0])
  3493	        if sid == 'A16':
  3494	            context['frozen'] = True
  3495	            u.check_inventory(rpc, plan['pin'])
  3496	        if sid == 'upgrade_execute_exec':
  3497	            context['upgradeBlock'] = r['blockNumber']
  3498	        if sid == 'activation_schedule_exec':
  3499	            context['activationScheduledAt'] = timestamp
  3500	            activation_eta_gate(plan, timestamp, a.word(rpc, u.TL, 'getTimestamp(bytes32)', plan['operations']['activation']['id']))
  3501	        if 'safeHash' in step:
  3502	            if 'safeTarget' in step:
  3503	                mint_trace(rpc, step, r)
  3504	            elif step.get('operation') == 'registry_accept':
  3505	                handover_trace(rpc, plan, step, r)
  3506	            else:
  3507	                u.safe_trace(rpc, plan, step, r)
  3508	            if sid.endswith('_exec'):
  3509	                u.safe_exec_events(r['logs'], step['safeHash'])
  3510	        context['passed'].add(sid)
  3511	        step['skipped'] = False
  3512	        record = dict(step=sid, receipt=r, readbacks=observed, **evidence)
  3513	        records.append(record)
  3514	        u.log(out, record)
  3515	        a.trace_overrides(rpc, r, overrides)
  3516	        if sid == 'upgrade_execute_exec':
  3517	            controls_at(rpc, plan, 'after_upgrade_execute_exec', controls, expected)
  3518	        if pc is not None and sid == pc['steps'][-1]:
  3519	            context['afterC'] = phase_c_accounting(rpc, plan)
  3520	            context['funding'] = funding_gate(rpc, plan)
  3521	            context['precheck'] = sp_attempt_precheck(rpc, plan)
  3522	            phase_c_controls(rpc, plan, 'after_phase_c', controls, context)
  3523	    if pc is not None:
  3524	        a.need('phase_c_controls_complete', sorted(x['control'] for x in controls if x['control'] in PC_CONTROLS),
  3525	               sorted(PC_CONTROLS))
  3526	    if plan['stage'] != 'full':
  3527	        a.need('stage_steps_complete', [x['step'] for x in records], [x['id'] for x in plan['steps']])
  3528	        closing = dvt_closing(rpc, plan)
  3529	        readback = dict(stage_readback(plan, rpc), dvtClosing=closing)
  3530	        a.write_json(out/'stage-readback.json', readback)
  3531	        budget(plan)
  3532	        validate_plan(plan, plan['externalInputs'], frozen=True, expected=expected)
  3533	        result = dict(status='PASS', stage=plan['stage'], steps=records, readback=readback)
  3534	        if pc is not None:
  3535	            result.update(phaseCAccounting=context['afterC'], phaseCFunding=context['funding'], spAttemptPrecheck=context['precheck'],
  3536	                          negativeControls=[x for x in controls if x['control'] in PC_CONTROLS])
  3537	        return result
  3538	    controls.append(handover_controls(rpc, plan))
  3539	    a.need('controls_complete', sorted(x['control'] for x in controls), sorted(plan['controls']))
  3540	    a.need('steps_complete', [x['step'] for x in records], [x['id'] for x in plan['steps']])
  3541	    closing = dvt_closing(rpc, plan)
  3542	    assert_manifest_frozen(rpc, plan['pin'], True)
  3543	    accounting = balances(rpc, plan['pin']['pauseList'])
  3544	    accounting['cappedBalance'] = a.word(rpc, plan['pin']['capped'], 'balanceOf(address)', u.SP)
  3545	    funded = BUFFER+plan['phaseC']['operatorTopUp']
  3546	    a.need('post_swap_tracked', accounting['totalTrackedBalance'], funded)
  3547	    a.need('post_swap_revenue', accounting['protocolRevenue'], BUFFER)
  3548	    a.need('post_swap_capped_balance', accounting['cappedBalance'], funded)
  3549	    # Phase C's deposit is the only accounting change after 6d; Phase D changes none.
  3550	    a.need('accounting_unchanged_since_phase_c', accounting, context['afterC'])
  3551	    fraud = fraud_rehearsal(rpc, plan)
  3552	    a.write_json(out/'manifest-fraud-proof.json', fraud)
  3553	    solvency = solvency_probe(rpc, plan, context['after6d'])
  3554	    a.write_json(out/'manifest-solvency.json', solvency)
  3555	    a.need('post_swap_solvency', solvency['allSolvent'], True)
  3556	    budget(plan)
  3557	    validate_plan(plan, plan['externalInputs'], frozen=True, expected=expected)
  3558	    result = dict(status='PASS', scope='Phases A/B/C/D', steps=records, negativeControls=controls,
  3559	                  B2=context['B2'], postSwapAccounting=accounting, phaseCAccounting=context['afterC'],
  3560	                  phaseCFunding=context['funding'], handoverFunding=context.get('handoverFunding', []),
  3561	                  spAttemptPrecheck=context['precheck'], fraudProof=fraud, dvtClosing=closing)
  3562	    a.write_json(out/'manifest-simulate.json', result)
  3563	    return result
  3564	
  3565	
  3566	def phase_c_accounting(rpc, plan):
  3567	    pc = plan['phaseC']
  3568	    state = balances(rpc, plan['pin']['pauseList'])
  3569	    state['cappedBalance'] = a.word(rpc, plan['pin']['capped'], 'balanceOf(address)', u.SP)
  3570	    funded = BUFFER+pc['operatorTopUp']
  3571	    expected = {who: (pc['operatorTarget'] if who == u.DEPLOYER else 0) for who in plan['pin']['pauseList']}
  3572	    a.need('C_accounting', [state['totalTrackedBalance'], state['protocolRevenue'], state['cappedBalance'], state['operatorBalances']],
  3573	           [funded, BUFFER, funded, expected])
  3574	    return state
  3575	
  3576	
  3577	def solvency_probe(rpc, plan, initial):
  3578	    """Observe B2a backing through deposit, real slash accrual and excess withdrawal."""
  3579	    a.need('solvency_probe_local', getattr(rpc, 'local', False), True)
  3580	    snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  3581	    observations = [dict(stage='after_swap', **initial)]
  3582	    token = plan['pin']['capped']
  3583	    def observe(stage):
  3584	        state = balances(rpc, plan['pin']['pauseList'])
  3585	        state['cappedBalance'] = a.word(rpc, token, 'balanceOf(address)', u.SP)
  3586	        observations.append(dict(stage=stage, **state))
  3587	    try:
  3588	        minter = a.address(rpc, token, 'minter()')
  3589	        a.need('capped_minter', minter, u.SAFE)
  3590	        for sender, target, sig, args in (
  3591	            (minter, token, 'mint(address,uint256)', (u.DEPLOYER, 10**18)),
  3592	            (u.DEPLOYER, token, 'approve(address,uint256)', (u.SP, 10**18)),
  3593	            (u.DEPLOYER, u.SP, 'deposit(uint256)', (10**18,))):
  3594	            u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, sender, target, a.calldata(sig, *args)))
  3595	        observe('after_first_deposit')
  3596	        # Slashing transfers deposited accounting to revenue without changing backing.
  3597	        u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.TL, u.SP, a.calldata('queueSlash(address)', u.DEPLOYER)))
  3598	        u.wait_until(rpc, a.qty(a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['timestamp'])+DAY)
  3599	        u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.TL, u.SP,
  3600	            a.calldata('slashOperator(address,uint8,uint256,string)', u.DEPLOYER, 0, BUFFER, 'solvency fixture')))
  3601	        a.need('fixture_revenue_accrued', a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'protocolRevenue()'), 2*BUFFER)
  3602	        u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.TL, u.SP,
  3603	            a.calldata('withdrawProtocolRevenue(address,uint256)', u.DEPLOYER, BUFFER)))
  3604	        observe('after_revenue_withdrawal')
  3605	    finally:
  3606	        a.need('solvency_fixture_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  3607	    for state in observations:
  3608	        state['difference'] = state['cappedBalance']-state['totalTrackedBalance']
  3609	        state['explanation'] = ('equal' if state['difference'] == 0 else
  3610	                                'missing B2a backing' if state['difference'] == -BUFFER else 'unexpected balance difference')
  3611	        a.need('solvency_difference_explained', state['difference'] in (0, -BUFFER), True)
  3612	        state['solvent'] = state['cappedBalance'] >= state['totalTrackedBalance']
  3613	        state['shortfall'] = max(0, state['totalTrackedBalance']-state['cappedBalance'])
  3614	        a.need('solvency_accounting_sum', state['totalTrackedBalance'],
  3615	               state['protocolRevenue']+sum(state['operatorBalances'].values()))
  3616	    return dict(observations=observations, allSolvent=all(x['solvent'] for x in observations),
  3617	                invariant='APNTsCapped.balanceOf(SP) >= totalTrackedBalance', restored=True)
  3618	
  3619	
  3620	def require_final(plan):
  3621	    # a3a's check predates stage c; a renamed or relabelled stage artifact is never final.
  3622	    a.require_final_artifact(plan)
  3623	    a.need('final_stage', plan.get('stage', 'full'), 'full')
  3624	
  3625	
  3626	def save_artifact(out, plan):
  3627	    if plan['stage'] in STAGES[:-1]:
  3628	        a.need('stage_not_final', plan['final'], False)
  3629	        path = out/('stage-'+plan['stage']+'.json')
  3630	        a.write_json(path, plan)
  3631	        digest = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
  3632	        path.with_name(path.name+'.sha256').write_text(digest+'  '+path.name+'\n')
  3633	    else:
  3634	        require_final(plan)
  3635	        u.save_plan(out, plan)
  3636	
  3637	
  3638	def load_artifact(path):
  3639	    # Integrity is independent of release eligibility and of the filename.
  3640	    plan = a.load_plan(path)
  3641	    if plan.get('stage') in STAGES[:-1]:
  3642	        a.need('stage_not_final', plan.get('final'), False)
  3643	    else:
  3644	        require_final(plan)
  3645	    return plan
  3646	
  3647	
  3648	def main(argv=None):
  3649	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  3650	    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', required=True)
  3651	    for name in ('plan', 'simulate'):
  3652	        p = sub.add_parser(name)
  3653	        p.add_argument('--stage', choices=STAGES, default='full')
  3654	        p.add_argument('--a7-source', type=Path, help='JSON containing plan and readback from A7 fork')
  3655	        p.add_argument('--b4-source', type=Path, help='JSON containing plan and readback from B4 fork')
  3656	        p.add_argument('--dump-state', type=Path)
  3657	        p.add_argument('--hold-fork', action='store_true', help='Keep stage fork alive until Enter')
  3658	        p.add_argument('--inputs', type=Path, required=True)
  3659	        p.add_argument('--out', type=Path, required=True)
  3660	        if name == 'plan':
  3661	            p.add_argument('--pin', type=Path, required=True, help='Pinned JSON from collect; offline build only')
  3662	        else:
  3663	            p.add_argument('--rpc-file', type=Path, required=True)
  3664	            p.add_argument('--rpc-file-secondary', type=Path, required=True)
  3665	            p.add_argument('--block', type=lambda x: hex(int(x, 0)), required=True)
  3666	            p.add_argument('--plan', type=Path)
  3667	    for name in ('sign', 'send'):
  3668	        sub.add_parser(name).add_argument('--plan', type=Path)
  3669	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
  3670	    out = getattr(args, 'out', None)
  3671	    try:
  3672	        if args.command in ('sign', 'send'):
  3673	            if args.plan:
  3674	                try:
  3675	                    require_final(a.plan_json(args.plan.read_bytes()))
  3676	                except a.Gate as error:
  3677	                    print('STOP: '+error.record['gate'], file=sys.stderr)
  3678	                    return 1
  3679	            print('STOP: manifest signing and sending are not implemented', file=sys.stderr)
  3680	            return 3
  3681	        a.need('output_empty', not out.exists() or (out.is_dir() and not any(out.iterdir())), True)
  3682	        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  3683	        inputs = external(args.inputs, args.stage)
  3684	        sources = {name: a.plan_json(path.read_bytes()) for name, path in
  3685	                   (('a7', args.a7_source), ('b4', args.b4_source)) if path}
  3686	        if args.command == 'plan':
  3687	            pin = a.plan_json(args.pin.read_bytes())
  3688	            plan = build_plan(pin, inputs, args.stage, sources)
  3689	            # A skeleton is explicitly not a calibrated/reviewable release packet.
  3690	            if args.stage == 'full':
  3691	                a.write_json(out/'manifest-draft.json', plan)
  3692	            else:
  3693	                save_artifact(out, plan)
  3694	        else:
  3695	            remote = a.RPC(args.rpc_file.read_text().strip())
  3696	            secondary = a.RPC(args.rpc_file_secondary.read_text().strip())
  3697	            pin = collect_pair(remote, secondary, args.block, inputs, args.stage)
  3698	            plan = load_artifact(args.plan) if args.plan else build_plan(pin, inputs, args.stage, sources)
  3699	            replay_frozen = bool(args.plan) and (plan['stage'] == 'full' or 'budgetsWei' in plan)
  3700	            a.need('simulation_pin', plan['pin'], pin)
  3701	            validate_plan(plan, inputs, frozen=replay_frozen)
  3702	            a.need('simulation_stage', plan['stage'], args.stage)
  3703	            frozen = copy.deepcopy(plan)
  3704	            with a.fork(remote, pin, out) as local:
  3705	                run_manifest(remote, local, plan, out, calibrate=not replay_frozen)
  3706	                if args.stage != 'full':
  3707	                    a.write_json(out/'stage-source.json', stage_source(plan, local))
  3708	                    if args.dump_state:
  3709	                        a.write_json(args.dump_state, local('anvil_dumpState', []))
  3710	                    print(json.dumps(dict(stage=args.stage, A7=plan['addresses']['BLSAggregator'], forkRPC=local.url)), flush=True)
  3711	                    if args.hold_fork:
  3712	                        input('Stage stopped. Press Enter to terminate the fork: ')
  3713	            a.relay_evidence(out)
  3714	            a.need('engine_stderr', (out/'engine.stderr').stat().st_size, 0)
  3715	            a.need('pin_recheck', collect_pair(remote, secondary, args.block, inputs, args.stage), pin)

  1960	def collect_pair(primary, secondary, block, inputs, stage='full'):
  1961	    pin = collect(primary, block, inputs, stage, (primary, secondary))
  1962	    independent = collect(secondary, block, inputs, stage, (primary, secondary))
  1963	    a.need('independent_pin', independent, pin)
  1964	    pin['oldActivityReads']['secondary'] = copy.deepcopy(independent['oldActivityReads']['primary'])
  1965	    pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['endpointResults'] = dict(
  1966	        primary=copy.deepcopy(pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['reads']),
  1967	        secondary=copy.deepcopy(independent['oldOwnerIsolation']['reads']))
  1968	    if 'sourceSelection' in pin['oldOwnerIsolation']:
  1969	        pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['endpointIdentities'] = {label:isolation.endpoint_identity(rpc,label) for label,rpc in (('primary',primary),('secondary',secondary))}
  1970	        isolation.assert_endpoint_identities(pin['oldOwnerIsolation']['endpointIdentities'])
  1971	    return pin

  1974	def predicate(target, sig, args, value, index=None):
  1975	    p = dict(kind='call', target=target, signature=sig, args=list(args), expected=value)
  1976	    if index is not None:
  1977	        p['index'] = index
  1978	    return p
  1979

  1981	def word_pred(target, sig, value, *args, index=None):
  1982	    return predicate(target, sig, args, value, index)
  1983	
  1984	
  1985	def address_pred(target, sig, value, *args):
  1986	    return word_pred(target, sig, int(value, 16), *args)

  1989	def runtime_bytes(art, values):
  1990	    identity = art['runtimeIdentity']
  1991	    raw = bytearray.fromhex(identity['artifactRuntime'][2:])
  1992	    for entry in identity['ranges']:
  1993	        a.need('immutable_supplied_'+entry['name'], entry['name'] in values, True)
  1994	        raw[entry['start']:entry['start']+entry['length']] = int(values[entry['name']]).to_bytes(32, 'big')
  1995	    return '0x'+raw.hex()

  1998	def token_immutables(target, protocol):
  1999	    empty, _ = a.short_string('')
  2000	    one, _ = a.short_string('1')
  2001	    hname, hver = a.keccak('0x'), a.keccak('1')
  2002	    domain = a.keccak(a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(bytes32,bytes32,bytes32,uint256,address)',
  2003	        a.keccak('EIP712Domain(string name,string version,uint256 chainId,address verifyingContract)'), hname, hver, a.CHAIN, target))
  2004	    return dict(PROTOCOL_REGISTRY=int(protocol, 16), _cachedThis=int(target, 16), _cachedChainId=a.CHAIN,
  2005	        _cachedDomainSeparator=int(domain, 16), _hashedName=int(hname, 16), _hashedVersion=int(hver, 16),
  2006	        _name=int.from_bytes(empty, 'big'), _version=int.from_bytes(one, 'big'))

  2009	def safe_hash(inner, n, target=u.TL):
  2010	    domain = a.keccak(a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(bytes32,uint256,address)',
  2011	                      a.keccak('EIP712Domain(uint256 chainId,address verifyingContract)'), a.CHAIN, u.SAFE))
  2012	    typehash = a.keccak('SafeTx(address to,uint256 value,bytes data,uint8 operation,uint256 safeTxGas,uint256 baseGas,uint256 gasPrice,address gasToken,address refundReceiver,uint256 nonce)')
  2013	    h = a.keccak(a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(bytes32,address,uint256,bytes32,uint8,uint256,uint256,uint256,address,address,uint256)',
  2014	                        typehash, target, 0, a.keccak(inner), 0, 0, 0, 0, a.ZERO, a.ZERO, n))
  2015	    return a.keccak('0x1901'+domain[2:]+h[2:])
  2016	
  2017	
  2018	def safe_payload(inner, target=u.TL):
  2019	    # Approved-hash signatures: each r is an owner, s=0, v=1. Safe requires ascending owners.
  2020	    signatures = '0x'+''.join(who[2:].zfill(64)+'0'*64+'01' for who in sorted((a.APPROVER, a.EXECUTOR)))
  2021	    return a.calldata('execTransaction(address,uint256,bytes,uint8,uint256,uint256,uint256,address,address,bytes)',
  2022	                      target, 0, inner, 0, 0, 0, 0, a.ZERO, a.ZERO, signatures)
  2023	
  2024	
  2025	
  2026	def domain_separator(aggregator):
  2027	    return a.keccak(a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(bytes32,uint256,address,address)',
  2028	        a.keccak('SuperPaymaster.BLSConsensus.v1'), a.CHAIN, aggregator, u.REG))
  2029	
  2030	
  2031	def prefix_payload(plan):
  2032	    steps = copy.deepcopy(plan['steps'])
  2033	    for step in steps:
  2034	        for key in (u.MEASURED_KEYS - {'executionTimestamp'}) | {'skipped', 'gasEvidence', 'variantMeasurements'}:
  2035	            step.pop(key, None)
  2036	        step['tx'].pop('gas', None)
  2037	        for tx in step.get('variants', {}).values():
  2038	            tx.pop('gas', None)
  2039	    # Phase C pin reads never affect the A/B prefix; a7/b4 sources are collected without them.
  2040	    pin = {k: v for k, v in plan['pin'].items() if k != 'phaseC'}
  2041	    return dict(pin=pin, addresses=plan['addresses'], steps=steps,
  2042	                activationScheduleNotBefore=plan['activationScheduleNotBefore'])
  2043	
  2044	
  2045	def bind_timing(plan):
  2046	    """Derive the same mined-block clock used by wait_until and replay."""
  2047	    now = plan['pin']['stateTimestamp']
  2048	    etas, gas_eta, verifier_eta = {}, 0, 0
  2049	    for step in plan['steps']:
  2050	        sid = step['id']
  2051	        target = now
  2052	        if sid == 'activation_schedule_approve':
  2053	            target = max(target, gas_eta, plan['activationScheduleNotBefore'])
  2054	        if sid.endswith('execute_exec'):
  2055	            target = max(target, etas[step['operation']])
  2056	        if sid == 'B4':
  2057	            target = max(target, verifier_eta)
  2058	        step['warpTarget'] = target
  2059	        now = target + 1
  2060	        step['executionTimestamp'] = now
  2061	        if sid.endswith('schedule_exec'):
  2062	            etas[step['operation']] = now + u.DELAY
  2063	        if sid == 'upgrade_execute_exec':
  2064	            gas_eta = now + u.DELAY
  2065	        if sid == 'A12b':
  2066	            verifier_eta = now + 4*DAY
  2067	
  2068	
  2069	def source_plan_bytes(plan):
  2070	    return (json.dumps(plan, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'))+'\n').encode()
  2071	
  2072	
  2073	def stage_source(plan, rpc):
  2074	    # Keep measured gas in stage-<name>.json; source plans are independently
  2075	    # reproducible offline, while their prefix binds the executed fork timing.
  2076	    rebuilt = build_plan(plan['pin'], plan['externalInputs'], plan['stage'], plan['stageSources'])
  2077	    verify_prefix(rebuilt, plan, plan['stage'])
  2078	    return dict(plan=rebuilt, readback=stage_readback(plan, rpc))
  2079	
  2080	
  2081	def prefix_digest(plan):
  2082	    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(prefix_payload(plan), sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode()).hexdigest()
  2083	
  2084	
  2085	def verify_prefix(plan, prior, stage):
  2086	    a.need('prefix_stage', prior['stage'], stage)
  2087	    a.need('prefix_digest', prior['prefixDigest'], prefix_digest(prior))
  2088	    expected = prefix_payload(plan)
  2089	    expected['steps'] = expected['steps'][:len(prior['steps'])]
  2090	    expected['addresses'] = {k: expected['addresses'].get(k) for k in prior['addresses']}
  2091	    if stage == 'c':
  2092	        # Stage c ends with the complete Phase C expansion.
  2093	        a.need('prefix_boundary', [s['id'] for s in prior['steps'] if s.get('phase') == 'C'][-1:],
  2094	               [s['id'] for s in plan['steps'] if s.get('phase') == 'C'][-1:])
  2095	        a.need('prefix_boundary_last', prior['steps'][-1].get('phase'), 'C')
  2096	    else:
  2097	        a.need('prefix_boundary', prior['steps'][-1]['id'], 'A7' if stage == 'a7' else 'B4')
  2098	    a.need('prefix_exact', prefix_payload(prior), expected)
  2099	
  2100	
  2101	def stage_readback(plan, rpc):
  2102	    a.need('stage_local', getattr(rpc, 'local', False), True)
  2103	    domain = a.call(rpc, plan['addresses']['BLSAggregator'], 'domainSeparator()')
  2104	    a.need('stage_domain', domain, plan['expectedDomainSeparator'])
  2105	    return dict(prefixDigest=plan['prefixDigest'], domainSeparator=domain)
  2106	
  2107	
  2108	def stage_pin(pin, stage):
  2109	    """Require Phase C evidence before the builder reads it; strip it for earlier stages."""
  2110	    if stage in PC_STAGES:
  2111	        a.need('stage_pin_phase_c', 'phaseC' in pin, True)
  2112	        return pin
  2113	    return {k: v for k, v in pin.items() if k != 'phaseC'}
  2114	
  2115	
  2116	def build_plan(pin, inputs=None, stage='full', stage_sources=None):
  2117	    a.need('manifest_inputs_required', inputs is not None, True)
  2118	    required(inputs, ('sha256', 'data'), 'external_binding')
  2116	def build_plan(pin, inputs=None, stage='full', stage_sources=None):
  2117	    a.need('manifest_inputs_required', inputs is not None, True)
  2118	    required(inputs, ('sha256', 'data'), 'external_binding')
  2119	    a.typed('0x'+inputs['sha256'], a.HASH32)
  2120	    a.need('external_digest_nonzero', int(inputs['sha256'], 16) != 0, True)
  2121	    d = inputs['data']
  2122	    validate_external(d, stage)
  2123	    # Phase C pin reads belong only to the stages that build Phase C; a7/b4 prefixes are
  2124	    # byte-identical whether or not the supplied pin carries them.
  2125	    pin = stage_pin(pin, stage)
  2126	    sources = copy.deepcopy(stage_sources or {})
  2127	    a.need("stage_sources", set(sources), set() if stage == "a7" else ({"a7"} if stage == "b4" else ({"a7", "b4"} if sources else set())))
  2128	    salt_digest = sources["b4"]["plan"]["externalInputs"]["sha256"] if "b4" in sources else inputs["sha256"]
  2129	    a.exact_keys('bindings', pin['bindings'], (DVT, COMMITTEE))
  2130	    for code in pin['bindings'].values():
  2131	        a.typed(code, a.HEXDATA)
  2132	        a.need('binding_nonempty', code != '0x', True)
  2133	    a.need('fixed_old_aggregator', pin['oldAggregator'], OLD_AGGREGATOR)
  2134	    validate_nonce_history(pin, inputs)
  2135	    validate_old_activity(pin, d.get('pop'))
  2136	    required(d, ('verifier', 'anni', 'dvt', 'committee', 'sampled', 'depositTargetWei'), 'manifest_external_complete')
  2137	    base_pin = {key: copy.deepcopy(pin[key]) for key in u.PIN_KEYS}
  2138	    base_pin['senders'].pop('anni', None)
  2139	    u.validate_pin(base_pin)
  2140	    a.need('pin_pending_token_eta', pin['pendingAPNTsTokenEta'], PENDING_TOKEN_ETA)
  2141	    drain_gate(pin)
  2142	    a.need('sampled_inventory', set(d['sampled']) <= set(pin['pauseList']), True)
  2143	    a.need('drain_inventory', set(pin['operatorBalances']), set(pin['pauseList']))
  2144	    a.need('anni_binding', pin['senders']['anni']['address'], d['anni'])
  2145	    a.need('sender_labels', set(pin['senders']), {'deployer', 'anni', 'approver', 'executor'})
  2146	    a.need('sender_unique', len({x['address'] for x in pin['senders'].values()}), 4)
  2147	    for label, sender in pin['senders'].items():
  2148	        a.exact_keys('sender_fields_'+label, sender, ('address', 'nonce', 'balance'))
  2149	        a.pin_integer(sender['nonce'], 'nonce_'+label)
  2150	        a.pin_integer(sender['balance'], 'balance_'+label)
  2151	    for field in ('stake', 'stakeDelay', 'entryPointDeposit', 'withdrawTime'):
  2152	        a.pin_integer(pin[field], field)
  2153	    a.need('stake_not_unlocking', pin['withdrawTime'], 0)
  2154	    for label, who in a.SENDERS.items():
  2155	        a.need('sender_'+label, pin['senders'][label]['address'], who)
  2156	    arts = copy.deepcopy(rebuild())
  2157	    isolation.validate_callsite_source(pin['oldOwnerIsolation'], arts['commit'])
  2158	    a.need('old_owner_control_cases_binding',pin.get('isolationControlCases',{}),d.get('isolationControlCases',{}))
  2159	    isolation.validate_deployed(pin.get('oldOwnerIsolation'), pin, d.get('deployedSources'), pending=d.get('pendingDsrRuling'))
  2160	    validate_old_timelock(pin)
  2161	    release_gate(inputs, arts, pin)
  2162	    counters = {label: x['nonce'] for label, x in pin['senders'].items()}
  2163	    sender_of = {label: x['address'] for label, x in pin['senders'].items()}
  2164	    if stage in PC_STAGES:
  2165	        counters['owner'] = pin['phaseC']['owner']['nonce']
  2166	        sender_of['owner'] = pin['phaseC']['owner']['address']
  2167	    phase_c = {}
  2168	    steps, addresses, runtimes = [], {}, {}
  2169	    safe_n = pin['safeNonce']
  2170	    operations = {}
  2171	    def finish():
  2172	        plan = dict(schema='3.1', route='manifest-A-B-D', stage=stage, final=stage == 'full', stageSources=sources,
  2173	            pin=copy.deepcopy(pin), externalInputs=copy.deepcopy(inputs), rebuild=arts,
  2174	            timelockRuntimeIdentity=copy.deepcopy(u.build()['contracts']['TimelockController']['runtime']),
  2175	            creationMetadataLayouts={'OverIssueFraudProofVerifier': copy.deepcopy(VERIFIER_METADATA_LAYOUT)},
  2176	            addresses=addresses, implementation=impl, extension=extension, operations=operations,
  2177	            steps=steps, controls=CONTROLS+(PC_CONTROLS if stage in PC_STAGES else []),
  2178	            expectedDomainSeparator=domain_separator(agg),
  2179	            b4Disclosure='B4 always consumes its reserved deployer nonce. If already applied, one empty, zero-value self-transfer costs gas only.',
  2180	            gasPolicy=copy.deepcopy(u.GAS_POLICY), gasParams=list(u.GAS),
  2181	            activationScheduleNotBefore=max(0, pin['pendingAPNTsTokenEta']-u.DELAY))
  2182	        isolation.assert_manifest_calldata(steps, arts['calldataSignatures'])
  2183	        if stage in PC_STAGES:
  2184	            plan['phaseC'] = phase_c
  2185	        plan['expectedDiscoveryAfter'] = build_expected_discovery(plan)
  2186	        bind_timing(plan)
  2187	        plan['prefixDigest'] = prefix_digest(plan)
  2188	        prefix_pin = {k: v for k, v in pin.items() if k != 'phaseC'}
  2189	        for name, source in sources.items():
  2190	            rebuilt = build_plan(prefix_pin, source['plan']['externalInputs'], name,
  2191	                                 {'a7': sources['a7']} if name == 'b4' else None)
  2192	            a.need('source_plan_bytes', source_plan_bytes(source['plan']) == source_plan_bytes(rebuilt), True)
  2193	            verify_prefix(plan, source['plan'], name)
  2194	            a.need('source_readback_prefix', source['readback']['prefixDigest'], source['plan']['prefixDigest'])
  2195	            a.need('source_domain', source['readback']['domainSeparator'], plan['expectedDomainSeparator'])
  2196	        return plan
  2197	    def add(sid, target, data, checks, sender='deployer', value=0, **extra):
  2198	        a.need('readback_required', bool(checks), True)
  2199	        tx = dict(to=target, data=data, value=hex(value), nonce=hex(counters[sender]),
  2200	            chainId=hex(a.CHAIN), maxFeePerGas=hex(a.MAX_FEE), maxPriorityFeePerGas=hex(a.PRIORITY),
  2201	            **{'from': sender_of[sender]})
  2202	        counters[sender] += 1
  2203	        steps.append(dict(id=sid, sender=sender, tx=tx, readbacks=checks, safeNonce=safe_n, **extra))
  2204	        return steps[-1]
  2205	    def deploy(sid, name, signature='c()', args=(), values=None):
  2206	        target = a.create_address(u.DEPLOYER, counters['deployer'])
  2207	        addresses[name] = target
  2208	        art = verifier_artifact(d['verifier']) if name == 'OverIssueFraudProofVerifier' else arts['contracts'][name]
  2209	        vals = values(target) if callable(values) else (values or {})
  2210	        rt = runtime_bytes(art, vals)
  2211	        a.need('nonempty_runtime_'+name, rt != '0x', True)
  2212	        runtimes[target] = rt
  2213	        add(sid, None, art['bytecode']+a.cast('abi-encode', signature, *args)[2:],
  2214	            [dict(kind='code', target=target, expected=rt)], creates=target, constructor=dict(signature=signature,bytecode=art['bytecode']))
  2215	        if name == 'OverIssueFraudProofVerifier':
  2216	            alternate = runtime_bytes(art, {'AGGREGATOR': vals['REGISTRY'], 'REGISTRY': vals['AGGREGATOR']})
  2217	            steps[-1]['readbacks'][0] = dict(kind='verifierCode', target=target, expected=[rt, alternate])
  2218	        if name in ('AOAProtocolRegistry', 'xPNTsFactoryV2', 'BLSAggregator'):
  2219	            steps[-1]['readbacks'].append(address_pred(target, 'owner()', u.DEPLOYER))
  2220	        return target
  2221	    def call(sid, target, sig, args, checks, **kw):
  2222	        return add(sid, target, a.calldata(sig, *args), checks, **kw)
  2223	    imm = {k: int(v, 16) for k, v in pin['immutables'].items()}
  2224	    impl = deploy('A1', 'SuperPaymaster', 'c(address,address,address)', tuple(pin['immutables'][k] for k in
  2225	        ('entryPoint', 'REGISTRY', 'ETH_USD_PRICE_FEED')), lambda t: {**imm, '__self': int(t, 16), 'EXTENSION': int(a.create_address(t, 1), 16)})
  2226	    extension = a.create_address(impl, 1)
  2227	    rt = runtime_bytes(arts['contracts']['SuperPaymasterAdmin'], {**imm, '__self': int(extension, 16)})
  2228	    steps[-1]['readbacks'].append(dict(kind='code', target=extension, expected=rt))
  2229	    tier = deploy('A2', 'GlobalTierSource', 'c(address)', (u.REG,), {'REGISTRY': int(u.REG, 16)})
  2230	    protocol = deploy('A3.create', 'AOAProtocolRegistry', 'c(address)', (u.DEPLOYER,))
  2231	    for suffix, kind, key in (('sp', 0, '0x'+u.SP[2:].zfill(64)), ('tier', 2, a.keccak(runtimes[tier]))):
  2232	        call('A3.'+suffix, protocol, 'bootstrapApprove(uint8,bytes32)', (kind, key),
  2233	            [word_pred(protocol, 'approved(uint8,bytes32)', 1, kind, key)])
  2234	    call('A3.seal', protocol, 'seal()', (), [word_pred(protocol, 'sealed_()', 1)])
  2235	    ext = deploy('A4.ext', 'xPNTsTokenV2Ext', 'c(address)', (protocol,), lambda t: token_immutables(t, protocol))
  2236	    token = deploy('A4.token', 'xPNTsTokenV2', 'c(address,address)', (protocol, ext),
  2237	        lambda t: {**token_immutables(t, protocol), 'EXTENSION': int(ext, 16)})
  2238	    factory = deploy('A4.factory', 'xPNTsFactoryV2', 'c(address,address,address,address)', (u.SP, u.REG, token, tier),
  2239	        dict(REGISTRY=int(u.REG, 16), implementation=int(token, 16), implementationCodehash=int(a.keccak(runtimes[token]), 16),
  2240	             extensionCodehash=int(a.keccak(runtimes[ext]), 16)))
  2241	    steps[-1]['readbacks'] += [address_pred(factory, 'SUPERPAYMASTER()', u.SP),
  2242	        address_pred(factory, 'defaultTierSource()', tier)]
  2243	    deploy('A5', 'SuperPaymasterLens')
  2244	    reg = deploy('A6.create', 'Registry', values=lambda t: {'__self': int(t, 16)})
  2245	    call('A6.upgrade', u.REG, 'upgradeToAndCall(address,bytes)', (reg, '0x'),
  2246	        [dict(kind='storage', target=u.REG, slot=u.IMPL_SLOT, expected=int(reg, 16)),
  2247	         predicate(u.REG, 'version()', (), a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(string)', 'Registry-5.9.0'))])
  2248	    agg = deploy('A7', 'BLSAggregator', 'c(address,address,address)', (u.REG, u.SP, d['dvt']), {'REGISTRY': int(u.REG, 16)})
  2249	    steps[-1]['readbacks'] += [word_pred(agg, 'minThreshold()', 3), word_pred(agg, 'defaultThreshold()', 7),
  2250	        address_pred(agg, 'SUPERPAYMASTER()', u.SP), address_pred(agg, 'DVT_VALIDATOR()', d['dvt']),
  2251	        *[word_pred(agg, 'slashThresholds(uint8)', threshold, severity) for severity, threshold in enumerate((2, 3, 3))]]
  2252	    steps[-1]['readbacks'].append(predicate(agg, 'version()', (), a.cast('abi-encode', 'c(string)', AGG_VERSION)))
  2253	    steps[-1]['readbacks'].append(predicate(agg, 'domainSeparator()', (), domain_separator(agg)))
  2254	    if stage == 'a7':
  2255	        return finish()
  2256	    for suffix, target, setter, getter in (('registry', u.REG, 'setBLSAggregator', 'blsAggregator'),
  2257	        ('dvt', d['dvt'], 'setBLSAggregator', 'BLS_AGGREGATOR'), ('committee', d['committee'], 'setBlsAggregator', 'blsAggregator')):
  2258	        call('A8.'+suffix, target, setter+'(address)', (agg,), [address_pred(target, getter+'()', agg)])
  2259	    for suffix, who, active in (('new', agg, True), ('old', pin['oldAggregator'], False)):
  2260	        call('A9.'+suffix, u.REG, 'setReputationSource(address,bool)', (who, str(active).lower()),
  2261	            [word_pred(u.REG, 'isReputationSource(address)', int(active), who)])
  2262	    inventory = pin['guardianInventory']
  2263	    a.need('guardian_slots', [x['slot'] for x in inventory], list(range(1, 14)))
  2264	    a.need('guardian_inventory', {(x['validator'], x['slot']) for x in inventory if x['validator'] != a.ZERO}, {(x['validator'], x['slot']) for x in d['pop']})
  2265	    a.need('pop_count', len(d['pop']), 3)
  2266	    for i, pop in enumerate(d['pop']):
  2267	        a.need('pop_domain', pop['domainSeparator'], domain_separator(agg))
  2268	        a.need('pop_aggregator', pop['aggregator'], agg)
  2269	        a.need('pop_pin', pop['pinBlockHash'], pin['blockHash'])
  2270	        words = u.uints('0x'+pop['calldata'][10:])
  2271	        add('A10.'+str(i), agg, pop['calldata'],
  2272	            [word_pred(agg, 'validatorAtSlot(uint8)', int(pop['validator'], 16), pop['slot']),
  2273	             predicate(agg, 'getBLSPublicKey(address)', (pop['validator'],), words[1:5]+[pop['slot'], 1])])
  2274	    for suffix, setter, getter in (('min', 'setMinThreshold', 'minThreshold'), ('default', 'setDefaultThreshold', 'defaultThreshold')):
  2275	        call('A11.'+suffix, agg, setter+'(uint256)', (2,), [word_pred(agg, getter+'()', 2)])
  2276	    verifier = deploy('A12a', 'OverIssueFraudProofVerifier', 'c(address,address)', (agg, u.REG),
  2277	        {'AGGREGATOR': int(agg, 16), 'REGISTRY': int(u.REG, 16)})
  2278	    steps[-1]['readbacks'] += [address_pred(verifier, 'AGGREGATOR()', agg), address_pred(verifier, 'REGISTRY()', u.REG), address_pred(agg, 'REGISTRY()', u.REG)]
  2279	    if stage == 'full':
  2280	        fixture = fraud_fixture(d['fraudProofFixture'], pin, agg, verifier, arts['contracts']['BLSAggregator']['storageLayout'])
  2281	        a.need('fixture_domain', fixture['domainSeparator'], domain_separator(agg))
  2282	    call('A12b', agg, 'proposeFraudProofVerifier(address)', (verifier,),
  2283	        [address_pred(agg, 'pendingFraudProofVerifier()', verifier),
  2284	         dict(kind='eta', target=agg, signature='pendingFraudProofVerifierReadyAt()', delay=4*DAY)])
  2285	    ep = pin['immutables']['entryPoint']
  2286	    stake = max(0, 10**18-pin['stake'])
  2287	    call('A13.stake', u.SP, 'addStake(uint32)', (max(DAY, pin['stakeDelay']),),
  2288	        [word_pred(ep, 'getDepositInfo(address)', pin['stake']+stake, u.SP, index=2),
  2289	         word_pred(ep, 'getDepositInfo(address)', max(DAY, pin['stakeDelay']), u.SP, index=3)], value=stake)
  2290	    deposit = max(0, d['depositTargetWei']-pin['entryPointDeposit'])
  2291	    call('A13.deposit', ep, 'depositTo(address)', (u.SP,),
  2292	        [word_pred(ep, 'balanceOf(address)', pin['entryPointDeposit']+deposit, u.SP)], value=deposit)
  2293	    for i, who in enumerate(pin['pauseList']):
  2294	        call('A14.'+str(i), u.SP, 'setOperatorPaused(address,bool)', (who, 'true'),
  2295	            [word_pred(u.SP, 'operators(address)', 1, who, index=2)])
  2296	    remaining = pin['totalTrackedBalance']
  2297	    for label in ('deployer', 'anni'):
  2298	        who = pin['senders'][label]['address']
  2299	        amount = pin['operatorBalances'].get(who, 0)
  2300	        a.need('drain_operator_present_'+label, who in pin['operatorBalances'], True)
  2301	        remaining -= amount
  2302	        call('A15.'+label, u.SP, 'withdraw(uint256)', (amount,),
  2303	            [word_pred(u.SP, 'operators(address)', 0, who, index=0), word_pred(u.SP, 'totalTrackedBalance()', remaining)], sender=label)
  2304	    call('A15.revenue', u.SP, 'withdrawProtocolRevenue(address,uint256)', (u.DEPLOYER, pin['protocolRevenue']-BUFFER),
  2305	        [word_pred(u.SP, 'protocolRevenue()', BUFFER), word_pred(u.SP, 'totalTrackedBalance()', BUFFER)])
  2306	    add('A16', u.REG, u.freeze_calldata(pin['roleConfig']),
  2307	        [predicate(u.REG, 'getRoleConfig(bytes32)', (u.role(),), u.frozen_config(pin['roleConfig']))])
  2308	    call('A17', u.SP, 'transferOwnership(address)', (u.TL,), [address_pred(u.SP, 'owner()', u.TL)])
  2309	    upgrade_data = [a.calldata('upgradeToAndCall(address,bytes)', impl, a.calldata('setGlobalPaused(bool)', 'true')),
  2310	        a.calldata('queueGasParams(uint32,uint32,uint32,uint32)', *u.GAS),
  2311	        a.calldata('setXPNTsFactory(address)', factory), a.calldata('queueBLSAggregator(address)', agg)]
  2312	    activation_data = [a.calldata(x+'()') for x in ('executeGasParams', 'applyBLSAggregator', 'executeAPNTsTokenChange')]
  2313	    activation_data += [a.calldata('setOperatorPaused(address,bool)', who, 'false') for who in d['sampled']]
  2314	    activation_data += [a.calldata('setGlobalPaused(bool)', 'false')]
  2315	    operations = {}

  2547	def registry_ownership(owner, pending):
  2548	    return [address_pred(u.REG, 'owner()', owner), address_pred(u.REG, 'pendingOwner()', pending),
  2549	            dict(kind='storage', target=u.REG, slot=PENDING_OWNER_SLOT, expected=int(pending, 16))]

  2552	def handover_contracts(addresses):
  2553	    return [('registry', u.REG, u.TL), ('protocol', addresses['AOAProtocolRegistry'], u.TL),
  2554	            ('factory', addresses['xPNTsFactoryV2'], u.TL),
  2555	            ('aggregator', addresses['BLSAggregator'], u.SAFE), ('dvt', DVT, u.SAFE),
  2556	            ('committee', COMMITTEE, u.SAFE), ('old_aggregator', OLD_AGGREGATOR, u.SAFE)]

  2559	def handover_gate(rpc, plan, step, context):
  2560	    sid = step['id']
  2561	    if not sid.startswith('D.'):
  2562	        return
  2563	    passed = context.get('passed', set())
  2564	    required_steps = {'activation_execute_exec', 'B4'}
  2565	    if sid.startswith(('D.aggregator.', 'D.old_aggregator.')):
  2566	        required_steps.update(('A10.0', 'A10.1', 'A10.2', 'A11.min', 'A11.default'))
  2567	    if 'phaseC' in plan:
  2568	        # Phase C funding precedes Phase D (§3 ordering, §4 critical path), and every
  2550	
  2551	
  2552	def handover_contracts(addresses):
  2553	    return [('registry', u.REG, u.TL), ('protocol', addresses['AOAProtocolRegistry'], u.TL),
  2554	            ('factory', addresses['xPNTsFactoryV2'], u.TL),
  2555	            ('aggregator', addresses['BLSAggregator'], u.SAFE), ('dvt', DVT, u.SAFE),
  2556	            ('committee', COMMITTEE, u.SAFE), ('old_aggregator', OLD_AGGREGATOR, u.SAFE)]
  2557	
  2558	
  2559	def handover_gate(rpc, plan, step, context):
  2560	    sid = step['id']
  2561	    if not sid.startswith('D.'):
  2562	        return
  2563	    passed = context.get('passed', set())
  2564	    required_steps = {'activation_execute_exec', 'B4'}
  2565	    if sid.startswith(('D.aggregator.', 'D.old_aggregator.')):
  2566	        required_steps.update(('A10.0', 'A10.1', 'A10.2', 'A11.min', 'A11.default'))
  2567	    if 'phaseC' in plan:
  2568	        # Phase C funding precedes Phase D (§3 ordering, §4 critical path), and every
  2569	        # funding target, including omitted rows, still holds before each handover step.
  2570	        required_steps.update(plan['phaseC']['steps'])
  2571	        if required_steps <= passed:
  2572	            context.setdefault('handoverFunding', []).append(funding_gate(rpc, plan))
  2573	    if sid.startswith('D.registry_'):
  2574	        required_steps.add('D.registry.transfer')
  2575	        for pred in registry_ownership(u.DEPLOYER, u.TL):
  2576	            check_predicate(rpc, pred)
  2577	    if sid == 'D.committee.transfer':
  2578	        required_steps.add('A8.committee')
  2579	        # Require the transferOwnership dispatch bytes and no acceptOwnership or
  2580	        # pendingOwner selector bytes in the pinned runtime. The post-transfer
  2581	        # owner()==SAFE read-back remains the authoritative ownership check.
  2582	        runtime = plan['pin']['bindings'][COMMITTEE].lower()
  2583	        a.need('committee_transfer_selector', '63f2fde38b' in runtime, True)
  2584	        a.need('committee_no_accept_ownership', '79ba5097' in runtime, False)
  2585	        a.need('committee_no_pending_owner', 'e30c3978' in runtime, False)
  2586	    if sid in ('D.aggregator.transfer', 'D.old_aggregator.transfer'):
  2587	        required_steps.add(sid.replace('.transfer', '.admin'))
  2588	        check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(step['tx']['to'], 'slashPolicyAdmin()', u.SAFE))
  2589	    a.need('handover_dependencies', required_steps <= passed, True)
