  2592	def handover_trace(rpc, plan, step, receipt):
  2593	    # The existing SP trace helper is intentionally SP-specific.
  2594	    trace = rpc('debug_traceTransaction', [receipt['transactionHash'], {'tracer':'callTracer'}])
  2595	    a.need('handover_trace_object', isinstance(trace, dict), True)
  2596	    for key, expected in (('from', step['tx']['from']), ('to', u.SAFE), ('input', step['tx']['data'])):
  2597	        a.need('handover_trace_'+key, trace.get(key, '').lower(), expected.lower())
  2598	    a.need('handover_trace_success', bool(trace.get('error')), False)
  2599	    nodes = list(a.trace_nodes(trace))
  2600	    if step['id'].endswith('_exec'):
  2601	        expected = [(u.SAFE, u.TL, step['inner'])]
  2602	        if 'execute' in step['id']:
  2603	            expected.append((u.TL, u.REG, a.calldata('acceptOwnership()')))
  2604	        for sender, target, data in expected:
  2605	            matches = [n for n in nodes if n.get('to', '').lower() == target and n.get('input') == data]
  2606	            a.need('handover_trace_call_count', len(matches), 1)
  2607	            a.need('handover_trace_caller', matches[0].get('from', '').lower(), sender)
  2608	            a.need('handover_trace_call_success', bool(matches[0].get('error')), False)

  2611	def handover_controls(rpc, plan):
  2612	    """Exercise actual owner checks; all fixture writes/nonces are snapshot-isolated."""
  2613	    a.need('handover_controls_local', getattr(rpc, 'local', False), True)
  2614	    results = []
  2615	    for name, target, owner in handover_contracts(plan['addresses']):
  2616	        snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  2617	        try:
  2618	            check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'owner()', owner))
  2619	            data = a.calldata('transferOwnership(address)', owner)
  2620	            for sender in ((u.DEPLOYER, u.SAFE) if owner == u.TL else (u.DEPLOYER,)):
  2621	                tx = u.fixture_tx(rpc, sender, target, data)
  2622	                results.append(a.negative(rpc, tx, a.calldata('OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address)', sender),
  2623	                                          'handover_'+name+'_'+sender))
  2624	            receipt = u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, owner, target, data))
  2625	            a.need('handover_positive_status', a.qty(receipt['status']), 1)
  2626	            check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'owner()', owner))
  2627	            if name == 'registry':
  2628	                for pred in registry_ownership(owner, owner):
  2629	                    check_predicate(rpc, pred)
  2630	            if name in ('aggregator', 'old_aggregator'):
  2631	                data = a.calldata('setSlashPolicyAdmin(address)', u.SAFE)
  2632	                results.append(a.negative(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.DEPLOYER, target, data),
  2633	                    a.calldata('OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address)', u.DEPLOYER), 'handover_'+name+'_admin'))
  2634	                receipt = u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, u.SAFE, target, data))
  2635	                a.need('handover_admin_status', a.qty(receipt['status']), 1)
  2636	                check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'slashPolicyAdmin()', u.SAFE))
  2637	            results.append(dict(contract=name, positiveReceipt=receipt, snapshot=snap))
  2638	        finally:
  2639	            a.need('handover_control_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  2640	    # Probe setters without retaining policy, balance or nonce changes.
  2641	    probes = [('sp', u.SP, u.TL, 'setProtocolFee(uint256)', 'protocolFeeBPS()', (),
  2642	               (u.DEPLOYER, u.SAFE), 'OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address)')]
  2643	    for name, target in (('aggregator', plan['addresses']['BLSAggregator']),
  2644	                         ('old_aggregator', OLD_AGGREGATOR)):
  2645	        probes += [(name, target, u.SAFE, setter, getter, args, (u.DEPLOYER,),
  2646	                    'NotSlashPolicyAdmin(address)') for setter, getter, args in (
  2647	                        ('setSlashThreshold(uint8,uint8)', 'slashThresholds(uint8)', (0,)),
  2648	                        ('setGuardianSlashBps(uint16)', 'guardianSlashBps()', ()))]
  2649	    for name, target, admin, setter, getter, args, denied, error in probes:
  2650	        snap = rpc('evm_snapshot', [])
  2651	        try:
  2652	            authority = 'owner()' if name == 'sp' else 'slashPolicyAdmin()'
  2653	            check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, authority, admin))
  2654	            current = a.word(rpc, target, getter, *args)
  2655	            data = a.calldata(setter, *args, current)
  2656	            label = 'handover_'+name+'_'+setter.split('(')[0]
  2657	            for sender in denied:
  2658	                results.append(a.negative(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, sender, target, data),
  2659	                    a.calldata(error, sender), label+'_'+sender))
  2660	            receipt = u.receipt(rpc, u.fixture_tx(rpc, admin, target, data))
  2661	            a.need(label+'_status', a.qty(receipt['status']), 1)
  2662	            check_predicate(rpc, word_pred(target, getter, current, *args))
  2663	            results.append(dict(contract=name, setter=setter, currentValue=current,
  2664	                                positiveReceipt=receipt, snapshot=snap))
  2665	        finally:
  2666	            a.need('handover_setter_restore', rpc('evm_revert', [snap]), True)
  2667	        check_predicate(rpc, word_pred(target, getter, current, *args))
  2668	    check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(u.SP, 'owner()', u.TL))
  2669	    for name, target, owner in handover_contracts(plan['addresses']):
  2670	        check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'owner()', owner))
  2671	        if name in ('aggregator', 'old_aggregator'):
  2672	            check_predicate(rpc, address_pred(target, 'slashPolicyAdmin()', u.SAFE))
  2673	    for pred in registry_ownership(u.TL, a.ZERO):
  2674	        check_predicate(rpc, pred)
  2675	    return dict(control='owner_handover', assertions=results, restored=True)

  2678	def activation_eta_gate(plan, schedule_timestamp, execute_timestamp):
  2679	    a.need('6c_schedule_floor', schedule_timestamp >= plan['activationScheduleNotBefore'], True)
  2680	    a.need('6d_timelock_eta', execute_timestamp >= schedule_timestamp+u.DELAY, True)
  2681	    a.need('6d_token_eta', execute_timestamp >= plan['pin']['pendingAPNTsTokenEta'], True)

  2684	def b2_gate(rpc, plan, upgrade_block):
  2685	    a.need('B2_capped_buffer', a.word(rpc, plan['pin']['capped'], 'balanceOf(address)', u.SP), BUFFER)
  2686	    observed = balances(rpc, plan['pin']['pauseList'])
  2687	    a.need('B2_balances', observed['totalTrackedBalance'], observed['protocolRevenue'])
  2688	    a.need('B2_revenue', observed['protocolRevenue'] <= BUFFER, True)
  2689	    a.need('B2_operator_balances', sum(observed['operatorBalances'].values()), 0)
  2690	    for who in plan['pin']['pauseList']:
  2691	        a.need('B2_pending_slash_'+who, a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'isSlashPending(address)', who), 0)
  2692	    # 5.4.2 has no reservation map. B1 creates 5.5's first possible reservation
  2693	    # epoch, atomically paused. Require a continuous pause and no intervening
  2694	    # implementation change; a current pause bit alone cannot prove this.
  2695	    a.need('B2_global_pause', a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'paused()'), 1)

  2891	def validate_plan(plan, inputs, frozen=False, *, expected=None):
  2892	    a.need('external_inputs_match', plan['externalInputs'], inputs)
  2893	    if expected is None:
  2894	        expected = build_plan(plan['pin'], inputs, plan.get('stage', 'full'), plan.get('stageSources'))
  2895	    a.exact_keys('manifest_keys', plan, set(expected) | ({'budgetsWei'} if frozen else set()))
  2896	    for key in set(expected)-{'steps'}:
  2897	        a.need('manifest_'+key, plan[key], expected[key])
  2898	    a.need('manifest_length', len(plan['steps']), len(expected['steps']))
  2899	    for actual, derived in zip(plan['steps'], expected['steps']):
  2900	        extra = u.MEASURED_KEYS | {'skipped', 'gasEvidence'} if frozen else set()
  2901	        if frozen and 'variants' in derived:
  2902	            extra |= {'variantMeasurements'}
  2903	        if frozen:
  2904	            u.validate_gas_evidence(plan, actual)
  2905	            owner_gas_gate(dict(actual, gasBound=derived['gasBound'])) if 'gasBound' in derived else None
  2906	        if frozen:
  2907	            a.need('no_skipped_nonce', actual.get('skipped'), False)
  2908	        a.exact_keys('manifest_step', actual, set(derived) | extra)
  2909	        for key in set(derived)-{'tx', 'variants'}:
  2910	            a.need('manifest_step_'+key, actual[key], derived[key])
  2911	        a.exact_keys('manifest_tx', actual['tx'], set(derived['tx']) | ({'gas'} if frozen and not actual.get('skipped') else set()))
  2912	        for key, value in derived['tx'].items():
  2913	            a.need('manifest_tx_'+key, actual['tx'][key], value)
  2914	        if 'variants' in derived:
  2915	            a.exact_keys('B4_variants', actual['variants'], ('apply', 'placeholder'))
  2916	            for name, tx in derived['variants'].items():
  2917	                variant = actual['variants'][name]
  2918	                a.exact_keys('B4_variant_fields', variant, set(tx) | ({'gas'} if frozen else set()))
  2919	                for key, value in tx.items():
  2920	                    a.need('B4_variant_'+key, variant[key], value)
  2921	            a.need('B4_apply_binding', actual['variants']['apply'], actual['tx'])
  2922	            if frozen:
  2923	                a.need('B4_placeholder_intrinsic', a.qty(actual['variants']['placeholder']['gas']) >= 21000, True)
  2924	                a.tx_quantities(actual['variants']['placeholder'])
  2925	                with u.gas_step(actual):
  2926	                    a.exact_keys('B4_variant_measurements', actual['variantMeasurements'], ('placeholder',))
  2927	                placeholder = dict(id=actual['id'], **actual['variantMeasurements']['placeholder'])
  2928	                placeholder['tx'] = actual['variants']['placeholder']
  2929	                u.validate_gas_evidence(plan, placeholder)
  2930	                with u.gas_step(actual):
  2931	                    a.exact_keys('B4_placeholder_measurements', actual['variantMeasurements']['placeholder'],
  2932	                                 u.MEASURED_KEYS | {'gasEvidence'})
  2933	    if frozen:
  2934	        checked = copy.deepcopy(plan)
  2935	        budget(checked)
  2936	        a.need('manifest_budget', checked['budgetsWei'], plan['budgetsWei'])
  2937	
  2938	    return copy.deepcopy(expected)
  2939

  2941	def owner_gas_gate(step):

  2943	    if 'gasBound' in step:
  2944	        a.need('C_owner_gas_bound', a.qty(step['tx']['gas']) <= step['gasBound'], True)

  3223	def pre_send(rpc, plan, step, context, measured=False):
  3224	    a.need('manifest_local_only', getattr(rpc, 'local', False), True)
  3225	    block = a.block_data(rpc,'latest')['number']
  3226	    isolation.registry_callsite_targets(lambda target, sig: a.address(rpc, target, sig, block), {},
  3227	                                       plan['pin']['oldOwnerIsolation']['callsiteTargetSource']['expected'])
  3228	    # Before every send, not only at closing: a post-pin allowance, role or authority of the orphaned timelock.
  3229	    coverage = discovery_coverage(plan, context.get('passed', set()))
  3230	    isolation.never_granted_recheck(rpc, coverage, block)
  3231	    delta = post_pin_delta(rpc, coverage, block)
  3232	    context['oldTimelockDelta'] = delta
  3233	    discovery_old_authority(rpc, coverage, block)
  3234	    old_timelock_holds_nothing(rpc, coverage, block, delta['approvalOwners'])
  3235	    handover_gate(rpc, plan, step, context)
  3236	    if step.get('phase') == 'C':
  3237	        phase_c_pre_send(rpc, plan, step, context)
  3238	    tx = step['tx']
  3239	    a.need('chain', a.qty(rpc('eth_chainId', [])), a.CHAIN)
  3240	    a.need('sender_nonce', a.nonce(rpc, tx['from']), a.qty(tx['nonce']))
  3241	    a.need('pending_nonce', a.nonce(rpc, tx['from'], 'pending'), a.qty(tx['nonce']))
  3242	    a.need('safe_nonce', a.word(rpc, u.SAFE, 'nonce()'), step['safeNonce'])
  3243	    a.need('safe_state', u.safe_state(rpc, 'latest'), plan['pin']['safe'])
  3244	    u.governance(rpc, 'latest', plan['pin']['timelockCreation'], plan['timelockRuntimeIdentity'])
  3245	    for target, expected in plan['pin']['bindings'].items():
  3246	        a.need('bound_runtime', u.code(rpc, target, 'latest'), expected)
  3247	    unchanged_old_activity(rpc, plan, a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['number'])
  3248	    a.need('dvt_activity_unchanged', dvt_activity(rpc, plan['externalInputs']['data']['dvt'],
  3249	        a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['number']), plan['pin']['oldActivity']['dvtProposals'])
  3250	    a.need('registry_owner', a.address(rpc, u.REG, 'owner()'),
  3251	           u.TL if 'D.registry_execute_exec' in context.get('passed', set()) else u.DEPLOYER)
  3252	    a.need('SP_owner', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'owner()'), u.TL if context.get('transferred') else u.DEPLOYER)
  3253	    if context.get('frozen'):
  3254	        assert_manifest_frozen(rpc, plan['pin'], 'D.registry_execute_exec' in context.get('passed', set()))
  3255	    else:
  3256	        a.need('role_config_unchanged', a.call(rpc, u.REG, 'getRoleConfig(bytes32)', u.role()), plan['pin']['roleConfig'])
  3257	    if not context.get('activated'):
  3258	        a.need('pending_token', a.address(rpc, u.SP, 'pendingAPNTsToken()'), plan['pin']['capped'])
  3259	        a.need('pending_token_eta', a.word(rpc, u.SP, 'pendingAPNTsTokenEta()'), plan['pin']['pendingAPNTsTokenEta'])
  3260	    if step['id'] == 'A17':
  3261	        u.check_inventory(rpc, plan['pin'])
  3262	        u.require_pauses(rpc, plan['pin'])
  3263	        drain = balances(rpc, plan['pin']['pauseList'])
  3264	        a.need('transfer_drained', drain['totalTrackedBalance'], BUFFER)
  3265	        a.need('transfer_revenue', drain['protocolRevenue'], BUFFER)
  3266	    if step['id'] == 'activation_execute_exec':
  3267	        context['B2'] = b2_gate(rpc, plan, context['upgradeBlock'])
  3268	        eta = a.word(rpc, u.TL, 'getTimestamp(bytes32)', plan['operations']['activation']['id'])
  3269	        activation_eta_gate(plan, context['activationScheduledAt'], eta)
  3270	    if 'safeHash' in step:
  3271	        h = a.call(rpc, u.SAFE, 'getTransactionHash(address,uint256,bytes,uint8,uint256,uint256,uint256,address,address,uint256)',
  3272	            step.get('safeTarget', u.TL), 0, step['inner'], 0, 0, 0, 0, a.ZERO, a.ZERO, step['safeNonce'])
  3273	        a.need('onchain_safe_hash', h, step['safeHash'])
  3274	        if 'operation' in step:
  3275	            u.check_op_hash(rpc, plan['operations'][step['operation']])
  3276	    if measured:
  3277	        u.validate_gas(step)
  3278	        owner_gas_gate(step)
  3279	        probe = {k: v for k, v in a.tx_rpc(tx, gas_cap=u.MAX_CREATE_GAS if tx['to'] is None else a.GAS_CAP).items() if k != 'gas'}
  3280	        a.need('fresh_estimate', a.qty(rpc('eth_estimateGas', [probe, 'latest'])) <= a.qty(tx['gas']), True)
  3281	        a.need('block_gas_limit', a.qty(tx['gas']) <= a.qty(a.block_data(rpc, 'latest')['gasLimit']), True)
  3282	    return tx

  2510	    report = dict(balanceWei=pin['senders']['deployer']['balance'], requiredWei=values,
  2511	                  shortfallWei=positive(values-pin['senders']['deployer']['balance']))
  2512	    a.need('budget_deployer_values', report, dict(report, shortfallWei=0))
  2513	    phase_c['deployerValueWei'] = values
  2514	    if stage == 'c':
  2515	        return finish()
  2516	    call('D.registry.transfer', u.REG, 'transferOwnership(address)', (u.TL,),
  2517	         registry_ownership(u.DEPLOYER, u.TL))
  2518	    op = dict(batch=False, targets=[u.REG], data=[a.calldata('acceptOwnership()')],
  2519	              predecessor=operations['activation']['id'],
  2520	              salt=a.keccak('manifest-v3.1/'+pin['blockHash']+'/'+inputs['sha256']+'/registry_accept'))
  2521	    op['id'] = u.operation_hash(op['targets'], op['data'], op['predecessor'], op['salt'], False)
  2522	    operations['registry_accept'] = op
  2523	    for verb in ('schedule', 'execute'):
  2524	        inner = u.op_call(op, verb)
  2525	        h = safe_hash(inner, safe_n)
  2526	        sid = 'D.registry_'+verb
  2527	        add(sid+'_approve', u.SAFE, a.calldata('approveHash(bytes32)', h),
  2528	            [word_pred(u.SAFE, 'approvedHashes(address,bytes32)', 1, a.APPROVER, h)],
  2529	            sender='approver', safeHash=h, inner=inner, operation='registry_accept')
  2530	        checks = [word_pred(u.TL, 'getOperationState(bytes32)', 1 if verb == 'schedule' else 3, op['id'])]
  2531	        if verb == 'schedule':
  2532	            checks.append(dict(kind='eta', target=u.TL, signature='getTimestamp(bytes32)', args=[op['id']], delay=u.DELAY))
  2533	        else:
  2534	            checks += registry_ownership(u.TL, a.ZERO)
  2535	        add(sid+'_exec', u.SAFE, safe_payload(inner), checks, sender='executor',
  2536	            safeHash=h, inner=inner, operation='registry_accept')
  2537	        safe_n += 1
  2538	    for name, target, owner in handover_contracts(addresses)[1:]:
  2539	        if name in ('aggregator', 'old_aggregator'):
  2540	            call('D.'+name+'.admin', target, 'setSlashPolicyAdmin(address)', (u.SAFE,),
  2541	                 [address_pred(target, 'slashPolicyAdmin()', u.SAFE)])
  2542	        call('D.'+name+'.transfer', target, 'transferOwnership(address)', (owner,),
  2543	             [address_pred(target, 'owner()', owner)])
  2544	    return finish()
  2545

    11	/**
    12	 * @title xPNTsFactoryV2
    13	 * @notice xPNTs v2 (XPNTs-4.0.1) factory for the AOA balance-mode stack.
    14	 * @dev    Differences from 3.x (spec 03 A-9, A-10, R4-H5, §2.5):
    15	 *         - the token template is deployed SEPARATELY and passed in (EIP-3860: core + extension
    16	 *           creation code would not fit in this constructor);
    17	 *         - the genesis SP is passed to `initialize` and is NOT made a spender (A-3);
    18	 *         - `paymasterAOA` becomes a genesis spender with a per-user default cap of 0 (Q5);
    19	 *         - a default credit tier source is passed to every new token (R4-H5);
    20	 *         - `propagateSuperPaymaster` only PROPOSES (48 h, community-cancellable, S-1).
    21	 *
    22	 * (3.x description follows)
    23	 * @notice Factory for deploying xPNTs tokens with AI-powered deposit predictions
    24	 * @dev Provides standardized deployment and intelligent deposit recommendations
    25	 *
    26	 * Key Features:
    27	 * - One-click xPNTs token deployment
    28	 * - Automatic pre-authorization setup (SuperPaymaster, Factory)
    29	 * - AI prediction for optimal aPNTs deposit amounts
    30	 * - Industry-specific multipliers (DeFi=2.0, Gaming=1.5, Social=1.0)
    31	 * - Safety factor adjustments
    32	 *
    33	 * Prediction Formula:
    34	 * suggestedAmount = avgDailyTx * avgGasCost * 30 days * industryMultiplier * safetyFactor / 1e18
    35	 *
    36	 * Example:
    37	 * - DeFi community: 100 tx/day * 0.001 ETH * 30 * 2.0 * 1.5 = 9 ETH
    38	 * - Gaming: 200 tx/day * 0.0005 ETH * 30 * 1.5 * 1.5 = 6.75 ETH
    39	 * - Social: 50 tx/day * 0.0003 ETH * 30 * 1.0 * 1.5 = 0.675 ETH
    40	 */

   194	    /**
   195	     * @notice Initialize factory
   196	     * @param _superPaymaster SuperPaymaster v2.0 address
   197	     * @param _registry Registry contract address
   198	     */
   199	    constructor(address _superPaymaster, address _registry, address _implementation, address _defaultTierSource)
   200	        Ownable(msg.sender)
   201	    {
   202	        if (_registry == address(0) || _implementation == address(0) || _implementation.code.length == 0) {
   203	            revert InvalidAddress(address(0));
   204	        }
   205	
   206	        address extension;
   207	        AOAProtocolRegistry protocolRegistry;
   208	        try xPNTsTokenV2(_implementation).BALANCE_MODE_VERSION() returns (uint16 v) {
   209	            if (v != 1) revert InvalidTemplate();
   210	        } catch {
   211	            revert InvalidTemplate();
   212	        }
   213	        try xPNTsTokenV2(_implementation).version() returns (string memory v) {
   214	            if (keccak256(bytes(v)) != keccak256("XPNTs-4.0.1")) revert InvalidTemplate();
   215	        } catch {
   216	            revert InvalidTemplate();
   217	        }
   218	        try xPNTsTokenV2(_implementation).EXTENSION() returns (address e) {
   219	            extension = e;
   220	        } catch {
   221	            revert InvalidTemplate();
   222	        }
   223	        try xPNTsTokenV2(_implementation).PROTOCOL_REGISTRY() returns (AOAProtocolRegistry r) {
   224	            protocolRegistry = r;
   225	        } catch {
   226	            revert InvalidTemplate();
   227	        }
   228	        if (extension.code.length == 0 || address(protocolRegistry) == address(0)) revert InvalidTemplate();
   229	        try xPNTsTokenV2(extension).PROTOCOL_REGISTRY() returns (AOAProtocolRegistry r) {
   230	            if (address(r) != address(protocolRegistry)) revert InvalidTemplate();
   231	        } catch {
   232	            revert InvalidTemplate();
   233	        }
   234	        if (_defaultTierSource == address(0)
   235	            || !protocolRegistry.isApprovedImpl(protocolRegistry.KIND_TIER_SOURCE(), _defaultTierSource)) {
   236	            revert InvalidAddress(_defaultTierSource);
   237	        }
   238	
   239	        implementation = _implementation;
   240	        implementationCodehash = _implementation.codehash;
   241	        extensionCodehash = extension.codehash;
   242	        defaultTierSource = _defaultTierSource;
   243	
   244	        SUPERPAYMASTER = _superPaymaster; // Can be address(0) initially
   245	        REGISTRY = _registry;
   246	
   247	        // Initialize aPNTs price (default: $0.02)

   274	    // ====================================
   275	    // Core Functions
   276	    // ====================================
   277	
   278	    /**
   279	     * @notice Deploy new xPNTs token
   280	     * @param name Token name (e.g., "MyDAO Points")
   281	     * @param symbol Token symbol (e.g., "xMDAO")
   282	     * @param communityName Community display name
   283	     * @param communityENS Community ENS domain
   284	     * @param exchangeRate Exchange rate with aPNTs (18 decimals, e.g., 1e18 = 1:1)
   285	     * @param paymasterAOA Paymaster address for AOA mode (optional, use address(0) for AOA+ only)
   286	     * @return token Deployed token address
   287	     */
   288	    function deployxPNTsToken(
   289	        string memory name,
   290	        string memory symbol,
   291	        string memory communityName,
   292	        string memory communityENS,
   293	        uint256 exchangeRate,
   294	        address paymasterAOA
   295	    ) external returns (address token) {
   296	        if (implementation.codehash != implementationCodehash
   297	            || xPNTsTokenV2(implementation).EXTENSION().codehash != extensionCodehash) {
   298	            revert TemplateCodehashChanged();
   299	        }
   300	        if (!IRegistry(REGISTRY).hasRole(keccak256("COMMUNITY"), msg.sender)) {
   301	            revert CallerNotCommunity();
   302	        }
   303	        if (communityToToken[msg.sender] != address(0)) {
   304	            revert AlreadyDeployed(msg.sender);
   305	        }
   306	        if (exchangeRate == 0) revert InvalidParameters();
   307	
   308	        // Deploy new xPNTs token proxy using clone pattern
   309	        address newTokenAddress = implementation.clone();
   310	        token = newTokenAddress;
   311	        xPNTsTokenV2 configuredToken = xPNTsTokenV2(newTokenAddress);
   312	        configuredToken.initialize(xPNTsTokenV2.InitConfig({
   313	            name: name,
   314	            symbol: symbol,
   315	            communityOwner: msg.sender,
   316	            community: msg.sender,
   317	            communityName: communityName,
   318	            communityENS: communityENS,
   319	            exchangeRate: exchangeRate,
   320	            superPaymaster: SUPERPAYMASTER,   // S-0: genesis SP, NOT a spender (A-3)
   321	            genesisSpender: paymasterAOA,     // A-10 ②: per-user default cap 0
   322	            tierSource: defaultTierSource     // R4-H5
   323	        }));
   324	        if (configuredToken.FACTORY() != address(this)
   325	            || configuredToken.communityOwner() != msg.sender
   326	            || configuredToken.community() != msg.sender
   327	            || configuredToken.exchangeRate() != exchangeRate
   328	            || configuredToken.SUPERPAYMASTER_ADDRESS() != SUPERPAYMASTER
   329	            || configuredToken.creditTierSource() != defaultTierSource
   330	            || configuredToken.creditPolicy() != 0
   331	            || (paymasterAOA != address(0) && !configuredToken.autoApprovedSpenders(paymasterAOA))) {
   332	            revert InitializationFailed();
   333	        }
   334	
   335	        // Record deployment
   336	        communityToToken[msg.sender] = token;
   337	        deployedTokens.push(token);
   338	
   339	        // P0-12a: register this token as an xPNTs in the factory whitelist so
   340	        // SuperPaymaster.settleX402PaymentDirect can gate on it. Without this
   341	        // gate, any ERC20 (e.g. USDC for which the user has done a standard
   342	        // infinite approve to the facilitator) could be drained via Direct.
   343	        isXPNTs[token] = true;
   344	
   345	        emit xPNTsTokenDeployed(msg.sender, token, name, symbol);
   346	    }

    90	    function initialize(address _owner, address _gtokenStaking, address _mysbt) external initializer {
    91	        // GOV-2 B.5: a fresh proxy must never start ownerless.
    92	        if (_owner == address(0)) revert OwnableInvalidOwner(address(0));
    93	        _transferOwnership(_owner);
    94	        GTOKEN_STAKING = IGTokenStaking(_gtokenStaking);
    95	        MYSBT = IMySBT(_mysbt);
    96	
    97	        // CC-48 round-9 (EIP-170): these seven bootstrap rows used to be seven separate
    98	        // `_initRole(...)` call sites. `via_ir` inlined the callee at every one of them,
    99	        // costing ~552 runtime bytes EACH -- about 3.8 KB of the contract, spent on writing
   100	        // seven rows of a table. Driving them through ONE call site from a memory array
   101	        // keeps exactly the same values and the same writes (asserted field-by-field by
   102	        // `test_RoleBootstrapMatrixUnchanged`) and hands the headroom back to the credit
   103	        // subsystem this round grows. The rows read in the same order as before.
   104	        bytes32[7] memory ids = [
   105	            ROLE_PAYMASTER_AOA, ROLE_PAYMASTER_SUPER, ROLE_DVT, ROLE_ANODE,
   106	            ROLE_KMS, ROLE_COMMUNITY, ROLE_ENDUSER
   107	        ];
   108	        uint256[7] memory mins = [uint256(30 ether), 50 ether, 30 ether, 20 ether, 100 ether, 0, 0];
   109	        uint256[7] memory tickets =
   110	            [uint256(3 ether), 5 ether, 3 ether, 2 ether, 10 ether, 30 ether, 0.3 ether];
   111	        uint32[7] memory threshs = [uint32(10), 10, 10, 15, 5, 0, 0];
   112	        uint32[7] memory bases = [uint32(2), 2, 2, 1, 5, 0, 0];
   113	        uint32[7] memory incs = [uint32(1), 1, 1, 1, 2, 0, 0];
   114	        uint32[7] memory maxes = [uint32(10), 10, 10, 5, 20, 0, 0];
   115	        uint16[7] memory exitFees = [uint16(1000), 1000, 1000, 1000, 1000, 0, 0];
   116	        uint256[7] memory minExitFees = [uint256(1 ether), 2 ether, 1 ether, 1 ether, 5 ether, 0, 0];
   117	        uint256[7] memory locks = [uint256(30 days), 30 days, 30 days, 30 days, 30 days, 0, 0];
   118	        for (uint256 r = 0; r < 7; r++) {
   119	            _initRole(
   120	                ids[r], mins[r], tickets[r], threshs[r], bases[r], incs[r], maxes[r],
   121	                exitFees[r], minExitFees[r], _owner, locks[r]
   122	            );
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        // AUDIT H-1: level-1 (new / zero-reputation users) MUST default to a NON-ZERO
   126	        // credit ceiling. SuperPaymaster now enforces the ceiling in validation
   127	        // regardless of xPNTs balance (Plan A), so a level-1 default of 0 would reject
   128	        // every fresh user even when they hold tokens. The defaults below keep the tier
   129	        // monotonic (level 1 ≤ 2 ≤ … ≤ 6) so higher reputation never *lowers* credit.
   130	        // NOTE: these are FRESH-INIT bootstrap values only — they apply when a new
   131	        // Registry is initialized, NOT to an already-initialized UUPS proxy, whose stored
   132	        // tiers survive an upgrade untouched (measured across the 5.4.2 -> 5.8.0 swap on
   133	        // 2026-08-30: the six-level schedule read identical either side of the impl swap).
   134	        // A live proxy's schedule is therefore THESE values as amended by whatever
   135	        // `setCreditTier` calls have since been made — and levels nobody has amended still
   136	        // hold exactly what is written below. (On the Sepolia proxy as of 2026-08-31 that
   137	        // is levels 4/5/6: every `CreditTierUpdated` ever emitted there touches only 1, 2,
   138	        // 3 and 7.) So the live schedule is neither "the constants below" nor "whatever
   139	        // setCreditTier says" — it is per level, and no comment can track it.
   140	        // DO NOT state one here — read it:
   141	        //   cast call 0xf5Bf37ca83AfdAab73691bA7eCcDfA69b8708E71 \
   142	        //     "creditTierConfig(uint256)(uint256)" 1 --rpc-url "$SEPOLIA_RPC_URL"
   143	        // (This rule is not hypothetical: the figure removed from this note had never
   144	        // matched the chain at any block. See git blame and PR #396 for the trace.)
   145	        //
   146	        // The 100 ether floor below covers the MEASURED ~38 aPNTs per-op charge
   147	        // with margin, but a high-maxFeePerGas op can exceed it — every deployment MUST
   148	        // call setCreditTier() to cover its own worst-case charge + economic model.
   149	        // Final tier economics are a team decision (tracked in audit issue #245).
   150	        creditTierConfig[1] = 100 ether;
   151	        creditTierConfig[2] = 100 ether;
   152	        creditTierConfig[3] = 300 ether;
   153	        creditTierConfig[4] = 600 ether;
   154	        creditTierConfig[5] = 1000 ether;
   155	        creditTierConfig[6] = 2000 ether;
   156	
   157	        // Fresh deployments fail closed at a finite proposal-level issuance
   158	        // budget. Existing UUPS proxies read zero after upgrade, which also
   159	        // fails closed for positive uplifts until governance configures a cap.
   160	        maxAggregateCreditUpliftPerProposal = 2000 ether;
   161	        // CC-48 HIGH-1: fresh deployments start with a finite protocol-wide ceiling
   162	        // (100x the per-proposal guard). Governance is expected to set the real number
   163	        // via setCreditPolicy before opening the reputation path to production traffic.
   164	        maxTotalCreditExposure = 200_000 ether;

   172	        isReputationSource[_owner] = true;

   174	        // CC-48 round-9 MEDIUM-HIGH-B3: a FRESH Registry has no users, so its credit
   175	        // population is empty and its derived exposure is zero -- provably, not by
   176	        // operator assertion. Marking it seeded here is what keeps the fail-closed gate in
   177	        // `updateGlobalReputation` aimed exclusively at UPGRADED proxies, whose population
   178	        // slots read empty while real promoted users exist on-chain.
   179	        creditPopulationSeededAt = block.timestamp;

   387	    /// @dev Reverts unless `account` has the role and its live stake meets the current minimum.
   388	    function _requireSuperOperatorRoleFor(address account) internal view {
   389	        if (!_hasEffectiveSuperStake(account)) revert Unauthorized();
   390	    }
   391	
   392	    function _hasEffectiveSuperStake(address account) internal view returns (bool) {
   393	        if (!REGISTRY.hasRole(ROLE_PAYMASTER_SUPER, account)) return false;
   394	        IRegistry.RoleConfig memory config = REGISTRY.getRoleConfig(ROLE_PAYMASTER_SUPER);
   395	        return REGISTRY.getEffectiveStake(account, ROLE_PAYMASTER_SUPER) >= config.minStake;
   396	    }
   397	
   398	    /// @dev The only deletion path for an in-flight record. Only 5.5.1 records entered the aggregate.
   399	    function _clearInflight(bytes32 opHash) internal {
   400	        Inflight memory f = _inflight[opHash];
   401	        if (f.operator == address(0)) return;
   402	        if (f.counted) inflightReserved[f.operator] -= f.a0;
   403	        delete _inflight[opHash];
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    function _inflightSlot(bytes32 opHash) private pure returns (bytes32) {
   407	        return keccak256(abi.encode(opHash, INFLIGHT_SEED));
   408	    }
   409	
   410	    function _setInflightLive(bytes32 opHash, bool on) internal {
   411	        bytes32 slot = _inflightSlot(opHash);
   412	        uint256 v = on ? 1 : 0;
   413	        assembly { tstore(slot, v) }
   414	    }
   415	
   416	    function _isInflightLive(bytes32 opHash) private view returns (bool live) {
   417	        bytes32 slot = _inflightSlot(opHash);
   418	        assembly { live := tload(slot) }
   419	    }
   420	
   421	    function _releaseStaleSponsorship(bytes32 opHash) internal {
   422	        Inflight memory f = _inflight[opHash];
   423	        if (f.operator == address(0)) return;
   424	        if (_isInflightLive(opHash)) revert SponsorshipInFlight();
   425	        _clearInflight(opHash);
   426	        operators[f.operator].aPNTsBalance += uint128(f.a0);
   427	        emit SponsorshipReleased(opHash, f.operator, f.a0);
   428	    }

   106	    /**
   107	     * @notice Initialize the UUPS proxy state
   108	     * @param _owner Contract owner
   109	     * @param _apntsToken aPNTs token address
   110	     * @param _protocolTreasury Treasury address for protocol fees
   111	     * @param _priceStalenessThreshold Oracle staleness threshold in seconds
   112	     */
   113	    function initialize(
   114	        address _owner,
   115	        address _apntsToken,
   116	        address _protocolTreasury,
   117	        uint256 _priceStalenessThreshold
   118	    ) external initializer {
   119	        if (_owner == address(0)) revert InvalidOwner();
   120	        __BasePaymaster_init(_owner);
   121	        // Note: _apntsToken can be address(0) during staged deployment
   122	        // (deployed later via setAPNTsToken which has its own zero-address check)
   123	        APNTS_TOKEN = _apntsToken;
   124	        treasury = _protocolTreasury != address(0) ? _protocolTreasury : _owner;
   125	        uint256 staleness = _priceStalenessThreshold > 0 ? _priceStalenessThreshold : 3600;
   126	        // Same range as PaymasterBase.setPriceStalenessThreshold. There is no setter here, so this is
   127	        // the only write: an oversized value underflows `block.timestamp - threshold` in updatePrice
   128	        // and truncates `uint48(updatedAt + threshold)` (validUntil).
   129	        if (staleness < 60 || staleness > 86400) revert InvalidConfiguration();
   130	        priceStalenessThreshold = staleness;
   131	        // Default values must be set explicitly (proxy storage doesn't inherit implementation defaults)
   132	        aPNTsPriceUSD = 0.02 ether;
   133	        protocolFeeBPS = 1000;
   134	    }

   140	    /// @dev Every selector this core does not define is executed by EXTENSION against the proxy's
   141	    ///      storage. NON-payable: plain ETH transfers are still rejected (there is no receive()).
   142	    fallback() external {
   143	        address ext = EXTENSION;
   144	        assembly {
   145	            calldatacopy(0, 0, calldatasize())
   146	            let ok := delegatecall(gas(), ext, 0, calldatasize(), 0, 0)
   147	            returndatacopy(0, 0, returndatasize())
   148	            switch ok
   149	            case 0 { revert(0, returndatasize()) }
   150	            default { return(0, returndatasize()) }
   151	        }
   152	    }

   158	    /// @notice Deposit aPNTs from msg.sender into their own operator balance (pull mode).
   159	    function deposit(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {
   160	        _requireSuperOperatorRoleFor(msg.sender);
   161	        // This might revert if Token blocks transferFrom (Secure Token)
   162	        IERC20(APNTS_TOKEN).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);
   163	        _creditDeposit(msg.sender, amount);
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    /// @dev Shared deposit accounting: overflow-check, credit operator balance,
   167	    ///      bump totalTrackedBalance, emit. Used by deposit/onTransferReceived/depositFor.
   168	    function _creditDeposit(address operator, uint256 amount) internal {
   169	        // Every later credit back into aPNTsBalance (cancel/expire of escrow, postOp refund or stale
   170	        // release of a reservation) must still fit uint128, so the cap covers all three buckets.
   171	        uint256 collateral = uint256(operators[operator].aPNTsBalance) + pendingWithdrawals[operator].amount
   172	            + inflightReserved[operator];
   173	        if (collateral > type(uint128).max || amount > type(uint128).max - collateral) {
   174	            revert AmountExceedsUint128();
   175	        }
   176	        // forge-lint: disable-next-line(unsafe-typecast)
   177	        operators[operator].aPNTsBalance += uint128(amount);
   178	        totalTrackedBalance += amount;
   179	        emit ISuperPaymaster.OperatorDeposited(operator, amount);
   180	    }

   162	    /// @param firstProposalId Non-zero lower bound for this deployment's IDs, below 2^128.
   163	    ///        Allocation uses max(this, aggregator.proposalFloor()) (nextProposalId), so a
   164	    ///        value below the floor is lifted, not rejected.
   165	    constructor(address _registry, uint256 firstProposalId, bytes32[] memory categories, address[] memory checkers)
   166	        Ownable(msg.sender)
   167	    {
   168	        if (firstProposalId == 0 || firstProposalId >> 128 != 0) {
   169	            revert InvalidFirstProposalId(firstProposalId);
   170	        }
   171	        if (categories.length != checkers.length) revert EvidenceConfigLengthMismatch();
   172	        for (uint256 i; i < categories.length; ++i) {
   173	            bytes32 category = categories[i];
   174	            address checker = checkers[i];
   175	            if (category == bytes32(0)) revert ZeroEvidenceCategory();
   176	            if (checker == address(0)) revert ZeroEvidenceChecker();
   177	            if (checkerOf[category] != address(0)) revert DuplicateEvidenceCategory(category);
   178	            checkerOf[category] = checker;
   179	            _categories.push(category);
   180	        }
   100	    function setRule(bytes32 ruleId, uint256 base, uint256 bonus, uint256 max, string calldata desc) external {
   101	        address community = msg.sender;
   102	        // Verify msg.sender has the COMMUNITY role (multi-tenant: each community manages its own rules)
   103	        if (!REGISTRY.hasRole(keccak256("COMMUNITY"), msg.sender) && owner() != msg.sender) revert NotAuthorized();
   104	        // Bounds constrain rule parameters, not arbitrary activity inputs or entropy factors;
   105	        // multiplication of those inputs may still overflow and revert in computeScore.
   106	        // base/bonus capped at 10_000 points per rule; max capped at 100_000 (max cumulative bonus).
   107	        if (base > 10_000 || bonus > 10_000 || max > 100_000) revert InvalidInput();
   108	
   109	        _ruleListed[community][ruleId] = true;
   110	        bytes32[] storage activeRules = communityActiveRules[community];
   111	        uint256 index = _activeRuleIndex[community][ruleId];
   112	        if (base > 0 && index == 0) {
   113	            activeRules.push(ruleId);
   114	            _activeRuleIndex[community][ruleId] = activeRules.length;
   115	        } else if (base == 0 && index != 0) {
   116	            uint256 last = activeRules.length;
   117	            if (index != last) {
   118	                bytes32 moved = activeRules[last - 1];
   119	                activeRules[index - 1] = moved;
   120	                _activeRuleIndex[community][moved] = index;
   121	            }
   122	            activeRules.pop();
   123	            delete _activeRuleIndex[community][ruleId];
   124	        }
   125	        _ruleActive[community][ruleId] = base > 0;
   126	        communityRules[community][ruleId] = Rule(base, bonus, max, desc);
   127	        emit RuleUpdated(community, ruleId, base, bonus);
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    function setNFTBoost(address collection, uint256 boost) external onlyOwner {
   131	        if (collection == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress();
   132	        if (!_collectionListed[collection]) {
   133	            if (boostedCollections.length >= MAX_BOOSTED_COLLECTIONS) revert MaxBoostedReached();
   134	            _collectionListed[collection] = true;
   135	            boostedCollections.push(collection);
   136	        }
   137	        nftCollectionBoost[collection] = boost;
   138	        emit NFTBoostAdded(collection, boost);
   139	    }

   141	    /**
   142	     * @notice Compute reputation for a user based on their community activities.
   143	     * @dev Activities are now mapped to rules.
   144	     */
   145	    function computeScore(
   146	        address user,
   147	        address[] memory communities,
   148	        bytes32[][] memory ruleIds,
   149	        uint256[][] memory activities
   150	    ) public view returns (uint256 totalScore) {
   151	        if (ruleIds.length != communities.length || activities.length != communities.length) revert InvalidInput();
   152	        for (uint256 i = 0; i < communities.length; i++) {
   153	            if (ruleIds[i].length != activities[i].length) revert InvalidInput();
   154	            address community = communities[i];
   155	            uint256 communityScore = 0;
   156	
   157	            for (uint256 j = 0; j < ruleIds[i].length; j++) {
   158	                bytes32 rId = ruleIds[i][j];
   159	                Rule memory r = communityRules[community][rId];
   160	
   161	                // A configured zero-base rule is disabled, not missing.
   162	                if (_ruleListed[community][rId] && !_ruleActive[community][rId]) continue;
   163	
   164	                // If rule was never configured, use the default for the first rule.
   165	                // P1-26 resolved: fallback uses constructor-initialized defaultRule; no setter changes it
   166	                if (!_ruleListed[community][rId] && j == 0) {
   167	                    r = defaultRule;
   168	                }
   169	
   170	                uint256 bonus = activities[i][j] * r.activityBonus;
    70	    );

   110	    /// @dev Initialize a newly deployed paymaster and verify owner is set correctly
   111	    function _initAndVerify(address paymaster, address operator, bytes memory initData) internal {
   112	        if (initData.length < 4) revert InvalidInitData();
   113	        (bool success, bytes memory returnData) = paymaster.call(initData);
   114	        if (!success) revert InitFailed(returnData);
   115	        (bool ownerSuccess, bytes memory ownerData) = paymaster.staticcall(
   116	            abi.encodeWithSignature("owner()")
   117	        );
   118	        if (!ownerSuccess || ownerData.length < 32) revert InitFailed(ownerData);
   119	        address actualOwner = abi.decode(ownerData, (address));
   120	        if (actualOwner != operator) revert OwnerMismatch(operator, actualOwner);
   121	    }

   141	    /// @dev Ensures the channel exists and has not been finalized.
   142	    modifier channelActive(bytes32 channelId) {
   143	        Channel storage ch = _channels[channelId];
   144	        if (ch.payer == address(0)) revert ChannelNotFound();
   145	        if (ch.finalized) revert ChannelFinalized();
   170	
   171	        // (b) ASSET dimension — enforced ONLY when configured (else asset is UNRESTRICTED).
   172	        AssetPolicy storage ap = _assetPolicy[sender][asset];
   173	        if (ap.configured) {
   174	            // per-tx hard cap.
   175	            if (amount > ap.perTxHardCap) return (PolicyDecision.REJECT, 0);
   176	            // daily (asset) window: would posting `amount` exceed dailyLimit?
   177	            uint256 assetSpent = _effectiveSpent(_assetSpend[sender][asset], ap.windowSeconds);
   178	            uint256 projected = assetSpent + amount;
   179	            if (projected > ap.dailyLimit) return (PolicyDecision.REJECT, 0);
   180	            remainingDaily = ap.dailyLimit - projected;
   181	            // DVT trigger by amount: `dvtTriggerAmount == 0` ⇒ DISABLED (no amount-based trigger).
   182	            if (ap.dvtTriggerAmount != 0 && amount >= ap.dvtTriggerAmount) {
   183	                requireDVT = true;
   184	            }
   185	        }
   186	
   187	        // (c) CONTRACT-SCOPE dimension — enforced ONLY when configured (else target UNRESTRICTED).
   188	        TargetScope storage cs = _contractScope[sender][target];
   189	        if (cs.configured) {
   190	            // target must be on the call-target allowlist, and the selector allowed.
   191	            if (!cs.allowed) return (PolicyDecision.REJECT, 0);
   192	            if (!_selectorAllowed[sender][target][selector]) return (PolicyDecision.REJECT, 0);
   193	            // Per-target velocity window (0 window ⇒ no velocity limit). A missing per-asset
   194	            // limit fails closed so switching assets cannot bypass the target scope.
   195	            if (cs.velocityWindow != 0) {
   196	                TargetVelocity storage velocity = _targetVelocity[sender][target][asset];
   197	                if (!velocity.configured || velocity.epoch != cs.velocityEpoch) {
   198	                    return (PolicyDecision.REJECT, 0);
   199	                }
   200	                uint256 targetSpent = _effectiveSpent(_targetSpend[sender][target][asset], cs.velocityWindow);
   201	                if (targetSpent + amount > velocity.limit) return (PolicyDecision.REJECT, 0);
   202	            }
   203	            // target flagged requireDVTAlways ⇒ REQUIRE_DVT regardless of amount.
   204	            if (cs.requireDVTAlways) {
   205	                requireDVT = true;
   206	            }
   207	        }
   208	
   209	        if (requireDVT) return (PolicyDecision.REQUIRE_DVT, remainingDaily);
   210	        return (PolicyDecision.ALLOW, remainingDaily);
   211	    }

   668	    /// @dev CC-48 round-3: the key-to-owner binding may only be released while NO active
   669	    ///      slot holds that key. Otherwise releasing it would let a second address claim
   670	    ///      the key of a live signer.
   671	    error KeyBindingStillActive(bytes32 keyHash, address boundTo);
   672	    /// @dev CC-48 round-3: a proposal that carries no reputation batch, no operator and
   673	    ///      no severity does nothing but burn a proposalId in two contracts. It still
   674	    ///      requires a real quorum, so it is not an attack — but a consensus path with a
   675	    ///      no-op shape is a place for future divergence, so the shape is rejected.
   676	    error EmptyProposalNotSupported();
   677	    /// @dev CC-48 round-2: the same G1 public key may never be bound to two
   678	    ///      validator addresses. N slots holding one key make pkAgg = N*pk, which a
   679	    ///      single secret-key holder can sign for — a 1-of-N quorum forgery.
   680	    error DuplicatePublicKey(bytes32 keyHash, address boundTo);
   681	    /// @dev CC-48 round-2: the combined (reputation + slash) proposal shape is
   682	    ///      rejected outright. Its aggregator pre-image committed to the real
   683	    ///      operator/slashLevel while Registry re-derived the reputation pre-image
   684	    ///      with address(0)/0, so the two verifications could never agree on the same
   685	    ///      signature. Rather than invent a second reputation schema, the shape is
   686	    ///      forbidden: submit the slash and the reputation update as separate proposals.
   687	    error CombinedProposalNotSupported();

   693	    constructor(address _registry, address _superPaymaster, address _dvtValidator) Ownable(msg.sender) {
   694	        if (_registry == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(_registry);
   695	        if (_superPaymaster == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(_superPaymaster);
   696	        if (_dvtValidator == address(0)) revert InvalidAddress(_dvtValidator);
   697	        REGISTRY = IRegistry(_registry);

   699	        DVT_VALIDATOR = _dvtValidator;
   700	
   701	        // Bootstrap slash thresholds for N=3: WARNING 2-of-3 (no funds moved,
   702	        // reputation ding only), MINOR/MAJOR 3-of-3 (real aPNTs at stake).
   703	        // Governance raises these via setSlashThreshold as the validator set grows.
   704	        slashThresholds[uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.WARNING)] = 2;
   705	        slashThresholds[uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MINOR)] = 3;
   706	        slashThresholds[uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MAJOR)] = 3;
   707	
   708	        // Owner is the initial policy admin; rotate to a multisig / timelock post-deploy.
   709	        slashPolicyAdmin = msg.sender;
   710	        // 30%, mirroring SuperPaymaster's automated-slash hardcap. That cap protects the
   711	        // operator's aPNTs balance; this one protects the guardian's GToken lock, which is
   712	        // the asset the committee-eligibility gate actually reads.
   713	        guardianSlashBps = 3000;
   714	    }
   715	
   716	    /// @notice Update the per-severity slash consensus threshold.
   717	    /// @dev    Gated to `slashPolicyAdmin` (a multisig, or a TimelockController for
   718	    ///         timelocked changes). Floored at SLASH_THRESHOLD_FLOOR and capped at
   719	    ///         MAX_VALIDATORS. Operationally the value should stay <= the active
   720	    ///         validator count, otherwise that severity becomes unslashable.
   721	    function setSlashThreshold(uint8 slashLevel, uint8 threshold) external {
   722	        if (msg.sender != slashPolicyAdmin) revert NotSlashPolicyAdmin(msg.sender);
   723	        if (slashLevel > uint8(ISuperPaymasterSlash.SlashLevel.MAJOR)) revert InvalidParameter("slashLevel");
   724	        if (threshold < SLASH_THRESHOLD_FLOOR || threshold > MAX_VALIDATORS) {
   725	            revert SlashThresholdOutOfRange(threshold);
   726	        }
   727	        emit SlashThresholdUpdated(slashLevel, slashThresholds[slashLevel], threshold);
   728	        slashThresholds[slashLevel] = threshold;
   729	    }
   730	
   731	    /// @notice Set the fraction of a colluding guardian's lock burned per slash.
   732	    /// @dev    Gated to `slashPolicyAdmin` exactly like `setSlashThreshold`: both decide how
   733	    ///         hard an automated path can hit someone's stake, so they share one admin.
   734	    function setGuardianSlashBps(uint16 newBps) external {
   735	        if (msg.sender != slashPolicyAdmin) revert NotSlashPolicyAdmin(msg.sender);
   736	        if (newBps < GUARDIAN_SLASH_BPS_MIN || newBps > GUARDIAN_SLASH_BPS_MAX) {
   737	            revert InvalidParameter("guardianSlashBps");
   738	        }
   739	        emit GuardianSlashBpsUpdated(guardianSlashBps, newBps);
   740	        guardianSlashBps = newBps;

    79	    /// @notice OZ {TimelockController}; its `minDelay` (= 2 days) gates loosening. Immutable so the

    83	    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    84	    // Construction
    85	    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    86	
    87	    /// @param timelock_ the OZ TimelockController (loosening + admin authority).
    88	    /// @param guardian_ the AirAccount 2-of-3 RecoveryService (immediate tighten/freeze authority).
    89	    /// @param initialConsumer optional staked consumer to authorize at deploy (address(0) ⇒ none).
    90	    constructor(address timelock_, address guardian_, address initialConsumer) {
    91	        if (timelock_ == address(0) || guardian_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();
    92	        timelock = timelock_;
    93	        guardian = guardian_;
    94	        emit GuardianUpdated(address(0), guardian_);
    95	        if (initialConsumer != address(0)) {
    96	            _authorizedConsumer[initialConsumer] = true;
    97	            emit ConsumerAuthorizationSet(initialConsumer, true);
    98	        }
    99	    }

   120	    /// @dev Normalizes/validates an asset address: `address(0)` is invalid (Q4).
   121	    function _requireValidAsset(address asset) private pure {
   122	        if (asset == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();
   123	    }

   125	    /// @dev Effective window length for a config value (0 ⇒ DEFAULT_WINDOW).
   126	    function _window(uint64 windowSeconds) private pure returns (uint64) {
   127	        return windowSeconds == 0 ? DEFAULT_WINDOW : windowSeconds;
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    /// @dev View-safe window roll: returns the spend that COUNTS right now without writing.
   131	    ///      If the stored window has elapsed, the effective spend is 0 (a fresh window).
   132	    function _effectiveSpent(SpendCounter storage c, uint64 windowSeconds) private view returns (uint256) {
   133	        uint64 w = _window(windowSeconds);
   134	        // windowStart == 0 (never spent) ⇒ block.timestamp >= w ⇒ fresh window ⇒ 0.
   135	        if (block.timestamp >= uint256(c.windowStart) + w) return 0;
   136	        return c.spentInWindow;
   137	    }

    70	        sealed_ = true;

    74	    function proposeApproval(uint8 kind, bytes32 key) external onlyOwner {
    75	        _checkKind(kind);
    76	        _checkApprovalKey(kind, key);
    77	        // `pendingAt` is uint64. Reject rather than truncate: wrapping the ETA would make a
    78	        // nominally time-locked approval immediately executable at sufficiently large timestamps.
    79	        if (block.timestamp > type(uint64).max - TIMELOCK) {
    80	            revert TimelockTimestampOverflow(block.timestamp);
    81	        }
    82	        uint64 eta = uint64(block.timestamp + TIMELOCK);
    83	        pendingAt[kind][key] = eta;
    84	        emit ApprovalProposed(kind, key, eta);
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    function executeApproval(uint8 kind, bytes32 key) external {
    88	        uint64 eta = pendingAt[kind][key];
    89	        if (eta == 0) revert NotPending();
    90	        if (block.timestamp < eta) revert TimelockActive(eta);
    91	        delete pendingAt[kind][key];
    92	        approved[kind][key] = true;
    93	        emit ApprovalExecuted(kind, key);
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    function revokeApproval(uint8 kind, bytes32 key) external onlyOwner {
    97	        _checkKind(kind);
    98	        delete pendingAt[kind][key];
    99	        approved[kind][key] = false;
   100	        emit ApprovalRevoked(kind, key);
   101	    }

   107	    function spKey(address sp) public pure returns (bytes32) {
   108	        return bytes32(uint256(uint160(sp)));
   109	    }

   111	    function isApprovedSP(address sp) external view returns (bool) {
   112	        return approved[KIND_SP][spKey(sp)];
   113	    }

   115	    /// @notice True when the codehash resolved by `implCodehash(target)` is approved for `kind`.
   116	    function isApprovedImpl(uint8 kind, address target) external view returns (bool) {
   117	        if (kind == KIND_SP) return false; // SP is address-keyed, never codehash-keyed
   118	        return approved[kind][implCodehash(target)];
   119	    }

   140	    /**
   141	     * @notice Deploy a new Paymaster using minimal proxy pattern
   142	     * @param _version Version of Paymaster implementation to use
   143	     * @param initData Initialization data for the Paymaster
   144	     * @return paymaster Address of the newly deployed Paymaster
   145	     */
   146	    function deployPaymaster(
   147	        string memory _version,
   148	        bytes memory initData
   149	    ) public nonReentrant returns (address paymaster) {
   150	        address operator = msg.sender;
   151	
   152	        // M-7: Require ROLE_COMMUNITY and ROLE_PAYMASTER_AOA in Registry.
   153	        // Both checks enforced only when registry is configured.
   154	        // ROLE_COMMUNITY: operator is a registered community.
   155	        // ROLE_PAYMASTER_AOA: operator is authorized for AOA paymaster mode —
   156	        //   isActiveInRegistry() checks this role on the owner, so a deploy
   157	        //   without it produces an immediately-inactive paymaster.
   158	        IRegistry reg = registry;
   159	        if (address(reg) != address(0)) {
   160	            if (!reg.hasRole(ROLE_COMMUNITY, operator)) revert NotRegisteredCommunity();
   161	            if (!reg.hasRole(ROLE_PAYMASTER_AOA, operator)) revert NotRegisteredPaymasterAOA();
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        // Check if operator already has a Paymaster
   165	        if (paymasterByOperator[operator] != address(0)) {
   166	            revert OperatorAlreadyHasPaymaster(operator);
   167	        }
   168	
   169	        // Get implementation address
   170	        address implementation = implementations[_version];
   171	        if (implementation == address(0)) {
   172	            revert ImplementationNotFound(_version);
   173	        }
   174	
   175	        // Deploy minimal proxy using OpenZeppelin Clones
   176	        paymaster = implementation.clone();
   177	
   178	        // CEI: record state before external calls so any re-entry sees the mapping populated.
   179	        // nonReentrant already blocks re-entry; this aligns with the pattern for hygiene.
   180	        paymasterByOperator[operator] = paymaster;
   181	        operatorByPaymaster[paymaster] = operator;
   182	        paymasterList.push(paymaster);
   183	        totalDeployed++;
   184	
   185	        _initAndVerify(paymaster, operator, initData);
   186	
   187	        emit PaymasterDeployed(operator, paymaster, _version, block.timestamp);
   188	    }
   189

   192	     * @param _version Version of Paymaster implementation

   194	     * @param initData Initialization data
   195	     * @return paymaster Deterministically deployed Paymaster address

   197	    function deployPaymasterDeterministic(
   198	        string memory _version,

   208	            if (!reg.hasRole(ROLE_PAYMASTER_AOA, operator)) revert NotRegisteredPaymasterAOA();

docs/API_SUPERPAYMASTER.md:444:`nonce = keccak256(abi.encode(to, salt))`. The payer signs the EIP-3009 `TransferWithAuthorization`
   190	    /**
   191	     * @notice Deploy Paymaster using deterministic address (CREATE2)
   192	     * @param _version Version of Paymaster implementation
   193	     * @param salt Salt for deterministic deployment
   194	     * @param initData Initialization data
   195	     * @return paymaster Deterministically deployed Paymaster address
   196	     */
   197	    function deployPaymasterDeterministic(
   198	        string memory _version,
   199	        bytes32 salt,
   200	        bytes memory initData
   201	    ) external nonReentrant returns (address paymaster) {
   202	        address operator = msg.sender;
   203	
   204	        // M-7: Require ROLE_COMMUNITY and ROLE_PAYMASTER_AOA (mirrors deployPaymaster check)
   205	        IRegistry reg = registry;
   206	        if (address(reg) != address(0)) {
   207	            if (!reg.hasRole(ROLE_COMMUNITY, operator)) revert NotRegisteredCommunity();
   208	            if (!reg.hasRole(ROLE_PAYMASTER_AOA, operator)) revert NotRegisteredPaymasterAOA();
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        if (paymasterByOperator[operator] != address(0)) {
   212	            revert OperatorAlreadyHasPaymaster(operator);
   213	        }
   214	
   215	        address implementation = implementations[_version];
   216	        if (implementation == address(0)) {
   217	            revert ImplementationNotFound(_version);
   218	        }
   219	
   220	        // Deploy with deterministic address
   221	        paymaster = implementation.cloneDeterministic(salt);
   222	
   223	        // CEI: record state before external calls (mirrors deployPaymaster ordering).
   224	        paymasterByOperator[operator] = paymaster;
   225	        operatorByPaymaster[paymaster] = operator;
   226	        paymasterList.push(paymaster);
   227	        totalDeployed++;
   228	
   229	        _initAndVerify(paymaster, operator, initData);
   230	
   231	        emit PaymasterDeployed(operator, paymaster, _version, block.timestamp);
   232	    }
   233	
   234	    /**
   235	     * @notice Predict deterministic Paymaster address
   236	     * @param _version Version to use
   237	     * @param salt Salt for CREATE2
   238	     * @return predicted Predicted Paymaster address
   239	     */
   240	    function predictPaymasterAddress(string memory _version, bytes32 salt)
   241	        external
   242	        view
   243	        returns (address predicted)
   244	    {
   245	        address implementation = implementations[_version];

    53	def role():
    54	    return a.keccak('PAYMASTER_SUPER')

    57	def uints(raw):
    58	    a.typed(raw, a.HEXDATA)
    59	    a.need('abi_word_alignment', len(raw[2:]) % 64, 0)
    60	    return [int(raw[i:i+64], 16) for i in range(2, len(raw), 64)]
    61	
    62	
    63	def addr_word(value):
    64	    a.need('abi_address_padding', value < 2**160, True)
    65	    return '0x'+format(value, '040x')
    66	
    67	
    68	def addr_array(raw):
    69	    w = uints(raw)
    70	    a.need('address_array_offset', w[0], 32)
    71	    a.need('address_array_size', len(w), 2+w[1])
    72	    result = [addr_word(v) for v in w[2:]]
    73	    a.need('address_array_unique', len(set(result)), len(result))
    74	    return result
    75	
    76	
    77	def config(raw):
    78	    w = uints(raw)
    79	    a.need('config_offset', w[0], 32)
    80	    a.need('config_min_words', len(w) >= 14, True)
    81	    a.need('config_description_offset', w[10], 384)
    82	    size = w[13]
    83	    a.need('config_string_length', len(w), 14+(size+31)//32)
    84	    body = bytes.fromhex(raw[2:])[32:]
    85	    a.need('config_string_padding', body[416+size:], bytes(len(body)-416-size))
    86	    body[416:416+size].decode('utf-8')
    87	    for i, bits in ((2, 32), (3, 32), (4, 32), (5, 32), (6, 16), (7, 1)):
    88	        a.need('config_range_'+str(i), w[i+1] < 2**bits, True)
    89	    return dict(raw=raw, owner=addr_word(w[11]), isActive=bool(w[8]), minStake=w[1], ticketPrice=w[2])
    90	
    91	
    92	def frozen_config(raw):
    93	    config(raw)
    94	    data = bytearray.fromhex(raw[2:])
    95	    data[32+7*32:32+8*32] = bytes(32)
    96	    return '0x'+data.hex()
    97	
    98	
    99	def freeze_calldata(raw):
   100	    body = frozen_config(raw)[66:]
   101	    # getRoleConfig returns an outer tuple offset; configureRole has a role and tuple offset.
   102	    return a.keccak('configureRole(bytes32,'+CONFIG_TYPE+')')[:10]+role()[2:]+format(64, '064x')+body

   105	def code(rpc, target, block):
   106	    return a.typed(rpc('eth_getCode', [target, block]), a.HEXDATA).lower()
   107	
   108	
   109	def creation(rpc, target, block):
   110	    hi = a.qty(block)
   111	    a.need('deployed_'+target, code(rpc, target, block) != '0x', True)
   112	    lo = 0
   113	    while lo < hi:
   114	        mid = (lo+hi)//2
   115	        if code(rpc, target, hex(mid)) == '0x':
   116	            lo = mid+1
   117	        else:
   118	            hi = mid
   119	    a.need('creation_nonzero', lo > 0, True)
   120	    a.need('creation_negative_'+target, code(rpc, target, hex(lo-1)), '0x')
   121	    a.need('creation_positive_'+target, code(rpc, target, hex(lo)) != '0x', True)
   122	    return lo
   123	
   124	
   125	def scan(rpc, target, start, end, topics):
   126	    result, seen = [], set()
   127	    for lo in range(start, a.qty(end)+1, 2000):
   128	        hi = min(lo+1999, a.qty(end))
   129	        logs = rpc('eth_getLogs', [dict(address=target, fromBlock=hex(lo), toBlock=hex(hi), topics=topics)])
   130	        a.need('logs_array', isinstance(logs, list), True)
   131	        for log in logs:
   132	            a.need('log_emitter', log['address'].lower(), target)
   133	            a.need('log_removed', log.get('removed', False), False)
   134	            a.need('log_range', lo <= a.qty(log['blockNumber']) <= hi, True)
   135	            identity = (a.typed(log['blockHash'], a.HASH32), a.typed(log['transactionHash'], a.HASH32),
   136	                        a.qty(log['logIndex']))
   137	            a.need('log_duplicate', identity in seen, False)
   138	            seen.add(identity)
   139	            result.append(log)
   140	    return sorted(result, key=lambda x: (a.qty(x['blockNumber']), a.qty(x['transactionIndex']), a.qty(x['logIndex'])))
   141	
   142	
   143	def inventory(rpc, block, reg_start, sp_start):
   144	    grant = a.keccak('RoleRegistered(bytes32,address,uint256,uint256)')
   145	    logs = scan(rpc, REG, reg_start, block, [grant, role()])
   146	    candidates = set()
   147	    for log in logs:
   148	        a.need('grant_topics', len(log['topics']), 3)
   149	        a.need('grant_topic', log['topics'][:2], [grant, role()])
   150	        candidates.add(addr_word(int(a.typed(log['topics'][2], a.HASH32), 16)))
   151	    holders = []
   152	    for who in sorted(candidates):
   153	        held = a.word(rpc, REG, 'hasRole(bytes32,address)', role(), who, block=block)
   154	        a.need('membership_boolean', held in (0, 1), True)
   155	        if held:
   156	            holders.append(who)

     2	import builtins
     3	from contextlib import contextmanager
     4	import io
     5	import json
     6	import os
     7	from pathlib import Path

    14	def reviewed_allowlist():
    15	    data = json.loads(Path(__file__).with_name('execution_read_allowlist.json').read_text())
    16	    u.a.need('execution_allowlist_schema', set(data), {'paths', 'reason'})
    17	    result = set()
    18	    for relative, reason in data['paths'].items():
    19	        path = Path(relative)
    20	        u.a.need('execution_allowlist_entry', bool(reason.strip()) and not path.is_absolute()
    21	                 and '..' not in path.parts, True)
    22	        result.add((u.ROOT / path).resolve())
    23	    return frozenset(result)
    24	
    25	
    26	def require_read(allowed, description):
    27	    if not allowed:
    28	        raise u.a.Gate('execution_repo_read', description, 'reviewed execution read')
    29	
    30	
    31	@contextmanager
    32	def guard(allowlist):
    33	    root = u.ROOT.resolve()
    34	    original_open, original_io = builtins.open, io.open
    35	    original_text, original_bytes, original_run = Path.read_text, Path.read_bytes, subprocess.run
    36	
    37	    def check_path(file, mode='r'):
    38	        if isinstance(file, int):
    39	            # Subprocess stdout/stderr pipes are not repository files. Regular
    40	            # read descriptors have no reviewed path identity and stay refused.
    41	            kind = os.fstat(file).st_mode
    42	            require_read(stat.S_ISFIFO(kind) or stat.S_ISSOCK(kind)
    43	                         or not ('r' in mode or '+' in mode), 'file descriptor')
    44	            return
    45	        if 'r' not in mode and '+' not in mode:
    46	            return
    47	        path = Path(os.path.abspath(os.fsdecode(file)))
    48	        # Check every resolved prefix: an in-repo symlink may point outside,
    49	        # including when the repo itself is reached through a symlink alias.
    50	        paths = {path, path.resolve(), *(parent.resolve() / path.relative_to(parent)
    51	                                      for parent in path.parents)}
    52	        require_read(all(not p.is_relative_to(root) or p in allowlist for p in paths), str(path))
    53	
    54	    def opened(file, mode='r', *args, **kwargs):
    55	        check_path(file, mode)
    56	        return original_open(file, mode, *args, **kwargs)
    57	
    58	    def io_opened(file, mode='r', *args, **kwargs):
    59	        check_path(file, mode)
    60	        return original_io(file, mode, *args, **kwargs)
    61	
    62	    def read_text(path, *args, **kwargs):
    63	        check_path(path)
    64	        return original_text(path, *args, **kwargs)
    65	
    66	    def read_bytes(path, *args, **kwargs):
    67	        check_path(path)
    68	        return original_bytes(path, *args, **kwargs)
    69	
    70	    def run(*args, **kwargs):
    71	        command = args[0] if args else kwargs['args']
    72	        words = shlex.split(os.fsdecode(command)) if isinstance(command, (str, bytes)) else command
    73	        # No git operation is needed during execution. Reject even -C/--git-dir
    74	        # and shell forms, independently of cwd (worktree gitdirs may be external).
    75	        require_read(not any(Path(os.fsdecode(word)).name == 'git' for word in words), str(command))
    76	        return original_run(*args, **kwargs)
    77	
    78	    try:
    79	        builtins.open, io.open = opened, io_opened
    80	        Path.read_text, Path.read_bytes, subprocess.run = read_text, read_bytes, run
    81	        yield
    82	    finally:
    83	        builtins.open, io.open = original_open, original_io
    84	        Path.read_text, Path.read_bytes, subprocess.run = original_text, original_bytes, original_run

   608	def external(path, stage="full"):
   833	## Generic-path selector gaps and the staking slasher role (2026-11-15)
   834	
   835	BLSAggregator-4.14.0 as merged in #630 had two gaps on the GENERIC `executeProposal` path
   836	(caller-chosen threshold >= `minThreshold`, no `proposalFloor`). Both are closed here.
   837	
   838	1. **`Registry.batchUpdateGlobalReputation` was not blocked.** Registry re-verifies the proof
   839	   itself, but under `blsDomainSeparator() = keccak(DOMAIN_NAME, chainid, blsAggregator, Registry)`.
   840	   That domain does not change on a DVT rotation, and Registry has no floor. A reputation proof
   841	   signed before a rotation and never executed could therefore still execute through a generic call,
   842	   bypassing the M1 floor guarantee.
   843	   - The #630 review recorded this path only as "consumes a raw id". The floor bypass was found
   844	     afterwards.
   845	   - Test: `test_PreRotationReputationProofCannotExecuteThroughGenericPath` (Prague, genuine keys).
   846	     Removing the block turns it red.
   847	2. **`GTokenStaking.slash` and `slashByDVT` were not blocked.** `DeployLive` made the aggregator an
   848	   authorized slasher, so a `minThreshold` quorum could slash any stake by any amount. This bypassed
   849	   severity bps, cooldown and the floor.
   850	   - The live Sepolia 4.11.0 aggregator has the same gap, with `minThreshold` 2. Only its owner (the
   851	     deployer EOA) can call `executeProposal` there.
   852	
   853	**Fixes**
   854	- The H-1 denylist now also blocks `batchUpdateGlobalReputation`, `slash` and `slashByDVT`.
   855	  BLSAggregator is 24,529 B (47 B headroom). Per-selector blocking is close to full: 5.5.x should
   856	  move to a per-target allowlist, or move the gates into the callees.
   857	- Versions are BLSAggregator-4.14.1 and DVTValidator-0.8.1. The 4.14.0 / 0.8.0 merged in #630
   858	  carries the gaps above.
   859	- `scripts/check_generic_selector_coverage.py` (CI, every PR) enumerates every non-view function in
   860	  the SuperPaymaster, Registry, GTokenStaking and DVTValidator ABIs. Each function whose code
   861	  references an aggregator identity must be classified as blocked or allowed-with-reason.
   862	- **No slasher role in 5.5.0.** The aggregator's only stake slash is `executeGuardianSlash`, which
   863	  cannot be reached while `fraudProofVerifier == 0`. `DeployLive` / `DeployAnvil` no longer grant the
   864	  role and assert it is absent.
   865	  - Enabling guardian slash later grants the role in the same timelock batch as
   866	    `proposeFraudProofVerifier`.
   867	  - Before enabling, see YetAnotherAA-Validator#441: the verifier must recompute the 4.14.0 signers
   868	    commitment, which includes `blockhash(block.number-1)`.
   869	- **Review L1.** Owner-forwarded `DVTValidator.registerBLSPublicKey` now requires
   870	  `aggregator.owner() == msg.sender`, as `removeValidator` already did.
   871	  - Test: `test_RegisterBLSPublicKey_OwnerDivergenceClosesOwnerPath`.
   872	
   873	**Disclosed residuals**
   874	- `Registry.updateOperatorBlacklist` stays generic-callable. A pre-rotation proof for the next unused
   875	  `blacklistNonce` remains valid. Its effect is limited to blocking or unblocking users; it moves no
   876	  funds.
   877	- `SP.updatePriceDVT` stays generic-callable. Each update is limited to +-20% against the SP cache.
   878	  The +-20% Chainlink band applies only while Chainlink is fresh (< 2 h). With a stale feed, cumulative
   879	  drift is bounded only by `[MIN, MAX]_ETH_USD_PRICE`, freshness and increasing `updatedAt`.
   880	- `ReputationSystem.setCommunityReputation` stays generic-callable (#631 review L1). It is open to
   881	  any Registry reputation source, and the aggregator is one on both chains. The current quorum can
   882	  therefore write any community score at `minThreshold`, with no proof.
   883	  - `communityReputations` has no reader in `contracts/src`. The coverage script fails if one is
   884	    added; that is the reopen condition.
   885	  - The SDK exposes the value only as a raw getter, documented as unused by the protocol.
   886	- The generic path can call `transfer` / `approve` on any token the aggregator holds. The aggregator
   887	  must hold no assets; step 13 reads its GToken, aPNTs, xPNTs and ETH balances back as 0.
   888	- Every "onlyOwner; the aggregator is not the owner" allowance holds only while the aggregator owns
   889	  nothing and holds no role. This is enforced by the coverage script's deploy-path ownership check
   890	  and by the step-13 readbacks OWNER-NOT-AGGREGATOR and AGG-NOT-DVT-VALIDATOR.
